\begin{center}
{\fontsize{16.8}{19.2}\selectfont\bfseries Finite Fields Forever\par}
\vspace{0.08em}
{\fontsize{12.4}{14.2}\selectfont\bfseries Assured Memory Isolation in a Single Logic Gate\par}
\vspace{0.25em}
{\normalsize\textbf{Eric Murphy}\textsuperscript{$\ast$}\par}
\vspace{0.04em}
{\small Spectral Systems LLC\par}

{\renewcommand{\thefootnote}{\fnsymbol{footnote}}\footnotetext[1]{Correspondence: \href{mailto:eric@spectralsystems.org}{\texttt{eric@spectralsystems.org}}. Canonical Release Kernel Zero (\texttt{v0.0.0} / \texttt{v0.0.0}). Permanent DOI: \href{https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24047902}{\texttt{10.5281/zenodo.24047902}} $\mid$ Web Portal: \href{https://spectralsystems.org}{\texttt{spectralsystems.org}}. Open-source pure \texttt{Init.Prelude} Lean~4 formal proofs (99 zero-axiom headline theorems: \LeanTheoremTotal\ core across \texttt{thm00}--\texttt{thm61} and \texttt{SheafCosetIsometry.lean}, plus 12 cross-domain physics corollaries \texttt{thm62}--\texttt{thm73} in \texttt{CxlSubflitPhysicsCorollaries.lean}), simulators, and silicon receipts: \url{https://github.com/spectralsystems/spectral-cxl-flit-sanitizer} (\texttt{Apache-2.0}; commercial hardware synthesis and silicon implementation rights reserved; patent applications pending).}}
\vspace{-0.85em}
\begin{center}
\begin{minipage}{0.90\textwidth}
\centering\footnotesize\itshape
``There is no point in using exact methods where there is no clarity in the concepts and the issues to which they are to be applied.'' ---~\upshape John~von~Neumann~(1949).\vspace{0.12em}\\
\itshape
``Information is not a disembodied abstract entity; it is always tied to a physical representation.'' ---~\upshape Rolf~Landauer~(1991)~\cite{1}.
\end{minipage}
\end{center}
\vspace{-0.15em}
\begin{abstract}
In this monograph, ``isolation in a single logic gate'' denotes an exact physical and complexity-theoretic correspondence: each individual bit lane of a 64-bit capability vector is partitioned orthogonally into its authorized transmission (\texttt{O5}) and its unauthorized reflection (\texttt{O6}) by the dual outputs of a single physical six-input lookup table (\texttt{LUT6\_2}), so that an entire 512-bit interconnect phase---packing four parallel 128-bit protocol slots---is sanitized in a single combinational logic level of constant $\Theta(1)$ circuit depth in $\mathrm{AC}^0$. Disaggregating High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM2e/HBM3/HBM4) and DDR5 over Compute Express Link (\mbox{CXL~3.0--4.0}~\cite{cxl2025spec, dassharma2024cxl}) and Universal Chiplet Interconnect Express (\mbox{UCIe~1.1--3.0}~\cite{ucie2025spec30}) underpins multi-tenant cloud and AI supercomputing fabrics. Existing hardware isolation mechanisms impose an acute trade-off among memory capacity, tail latency, and link availability. Stateful permission-table firewalls (\textit{Space-Control}~\cite{goswami2026spacecontrol}) carve out $1.5625\%$ of pooled DRAM ($250\text{~GB}$ decimal per $16\text{~TB}$ rack / $256\text{~GiB}$ binary per $16\text{~TiB}$ rack) and incur $32$--$68\text{~ns}$ table-walk stalls on permission-cache misses, whereas tagged capabilities (\textit{CHERI / ARM Morello}~\cite{woodruff2014cheri, watson2015cheri}) impose a $12.5\%$ metadata tax. Conversely, stateless link firewalls that abort unauthorized accesses by poisoning the tail Cyclic Redundancy Check (CRC) of a 512-bit CXL flit phase destroy up to three co-packed innocent 128-bit slots and trigger Go-Back-N NAK replay storms.

We present an algebraic, coding-theoretic, and queueing-theoretic framework for Layer-1/2 Zero-Trust sub-flit access control and introduce \textsc{Kernel Zero} (\texttt{v0.0.0} / \texttt{v0.0.0}), unifying the frozen baseline datapath with full lifecycle closure at $+0$ added core logic gates. The interposer decouples the 14-bit requester $\mathrm{SPID}_i$ onto a prefix-guarded 64-tenant live bitmap and evaluates spatial and temporal bitmask projections ($\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i) = r_i \wedge P_{\mathrm{eff}}$ and $\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i) = r_i \wedge \neg P_{\mathrm{eff}}$) over $\mathbb{F}_2^{64}$ across four parallel 128-bit slots per 512-bit flit phase in \StageOneSieveDelayNs. Wire-carried capabilities ($B_{\mathrm{rest}}=0$), a 64-bucket timing wheel (\texttt{CSR 0x68}~\cite{varghese1997timing}, $\Delta d_{\mathrm{rev}}=0$), and admission-paced shootdown windows ($\Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}} \le 548.0\text{~ns}$) bound active controller state to $64\text{~bits}$ independently of pool capacity $M$ while sustaining $99.20\%$ slot goodput vs.\ $48.90\%$ under CRC poisoning.

We establish six core results. First, hierarchical address partitioning ($\mathcal{X}_{n+1} = \mathcal{X}_n \oplus \mathcal{W}_n$~\cite{chen2001multilevel}) combines $J(64,w)$ and $\mathbb{F}_8$ Steiner $S(2,8,64)$ codes~\cite{welch1974lower, kautz1964nonrandom} with a Cycle-0 $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ Vieta annihilator ($P_{\mathrm{alias}} \equiv 0$ up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 8$ across $132,600$ cohorts) and 8-step shadow-bank B\'{e}zout--Horner root reclamation ($1.095\times 10^7\text{~rev/s}$). Second, spanning-forest orbit clamping, coarse-aperture HDM gauge flattening ($L_j \equiv 1$), and $<4\text{~ns}$ PHY-gated failover $(T_0,T_1)$~\cite{tutte1961problem, nashwilliams1961edge} pair with $+0$-LUT fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} MOESI, 4-tier mantissa clamping, pair-popcount pre-reduction, Wannamaker/BBP LoRA SVD bounds~\cite{wannamaker2000nonsubtractive, benaych2012singular}, and Schur-convex \texttt{spectralc} compilation~\cite{mcconnell2011certifying, doe2025genesis}. Third, renewal-reward and Martingale Network Calculus bounds~\cite{poloczek2014martingale} join dual-epoch grace windows, 0-LUT Home-Agent snoop synthesis ($\le 500\text{~ns}$), a \ScrubShimPhysicalLUTs-LUT Per-\texttt{ARID} read-scrub shim, CSS split-coset CRC48/CRC64 tunneling ($H_{\mathrm{FEC}} \circ E_{\mathrm{fold}} \equiv \mathbf{0}$~\cite{calderbank1996good} with 256-byte CXL first cohomology $H^1(\mathcal{C}^\bullet) \cong \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^8$), and non-abelian $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}$ / Davies--Meyer hop delegation~\cite{daemen1995cipher, birgisson2014macaroons}. Fourth, Stage-4 credit invariance ($\Delta t_{\mathrm{CRD}} \equiv 0$), Tropical $(\max,+) \otimes S_{64}$ pod federation, Mode-D De~Morgan \texttt{RAIN} dual-sieves~\cite{mitra2000commonmode}, Dillon $\mathcal{PS}^\mp$ bent/DOM masking~\cite{gross2016dom, dillon1974elementary}, and 137-tick \texttt{ZK-IC3} liveness~\cite{chakarov2013probabilistic} are unified across 99 zero-axiom pure \texttt{Init.Prelude} Lean~4 headline theorems (\LeanTheoremTotal\ core + 12 cross-domain physics corollaries~\cite{demoura2021lean4}).

On an AMD Virtex UltraScale+ HBM \texttt{xcvu47p-fsvh2892-2L-e} FPGA (Vivado~\VivadoToolVersion\ post-route sign-off and Dual-AFI AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} execution on \texttt{\AwsAgfiId} and 16~GB HBM2 DMA \texttt{\AwsHbmAgfiId}), the 2-stage core occupies \CorePhysicalLUTs\ CLB LUTs (\CoreLUTUtilPct; \FastCoreLuts-LUT fast-core at \FastCoreFmax; +\ScrubShimPhysicalLUTs\ LUTs scrub shim; \WrapperEnclaveLUTs\ LUTs w/ 4-stage wrapper) and \CoreBRAM\ BRAM at \PostRouteSetupWNS\ WNS ($F_{\max} = \PostRouteFmaxMHz$, \PostRouteDynPowerMW\ dynamic power). In the open-source 7nm FinFET \texttt{ASAP7} predictive PDK (\texttt{asap7sc7p5t})~\cite{clark2016asap7}, the standalone core maps to \AsicCoreCells\ cells (\AsicCoreGe\ GE, \AsicCoreAreaMm) and the routed 4-stage enclave closes at \AsicStandardCells\ cells (\AsicEnclaveAreaMm, \AsicStageOneTwoDelayPs\ critical path, \AsicFmaxMhz, \AsicTotalPowerMw). Across 10,000 racks ($160\text{~PB}$ dec / $156.25\text{~PiB}$ bin), eliminating the $1.5625\%$ table carve-out reclaims $2,500\text{~TB}$ ($2,560,000\text{~GiB}$), saving \$$37.50\text{M}$--\$$38.40\text{M}$ CapEx and $8.0$--$39.4\text{~MW}$.
\end{abstract}

\section{Introduction}
\label{sec:intro}

\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\;Disaggregating High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM2e/HBM3/HBM4) and DDR5 pools across Compute Express Link (\mbox{CXL~3.0--4.0}, and projected \mbox{CXL~5.0} capability extensions~\cite{cxl2025spec, dassharma2024cxl, li2023pond, sun2023demystifying}) and Universal Chiplet Interconnect Express (\mbox{UCIe~1.1--3.0}~\cite{ucie2025spec30}) removes per-socket memory stranding in cloud and AI clusters. Yet once multiple hosts and accelerators share a physical memory pool over \texttt{CXL.mem} and \texttt{CXL.cache}, every 512-bit clock phase carries four co-packed 128-bit protocol slots at wire speed. Existing hardware isolation designs force an acute trade-off across four architectural dimensions:\par\smallskip

\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Capacity and tail-latency tax from stateful permission tables:} \textit{Space-Control}~\cite{goswami2026spacecontrol} carves out $1.5625\%$ of pooled DRAM ($250\text{~GB}$/rack) for permission tables and stalls the memory pipeline by $32.0$--$68.0\text{~ns}$ on SRAM permission-cache misses ($+11.0\%$ to $+19.0\%$ throughput loss and $>180\text{~ns}$ P99.99 tail latency on sparse MoE and vLLM KV-cache workloads).\footnote{For static batch workloads whose working-set permissions fit in on-die SRAM, miss penalties are modest; under sparse MoE, vLLM KV-cache sharing, and serverless microVM IPC, permission-cache thrashing dominates tail latency.} Tagged capabilities (\textit{CHERI / ARM Morello}~\cite{woodruff2014cheri, watson2015cheri}) and offloaded re-encryption engines~\cite{li2025aiore, xu2024dataenclave} incur a $12.50\%$ metadata tax or $\Theta(M)$ capability revocation sweeps~\cite{filardo2020cornucopia, filardo2024cornucopiareloaded}.
  \item \textbf{Collateral damage from whole-flit CRC poisoning:} A 512-bit CXL/UCIe flit phase packs four parallel 128-bit protocol slots ($s_0, s_1, s_2, s_3$). Inverting the tail Flit CRC to abort one unauthorized slot destroys up to three co-packed innocent slots and triggers a Go-Back-N NAK replay storm.
  \item \textbf{Control-plane compromise, cyclic HDM permutations, and root reclamation:} Runtime policy writes over PCIe \texttt{BAR0} MMIO are exposed if the software Fabric Manager (FM) is compromised or if a host issues a warm PCIe Function-Level Reset (FLR). Cyclic peer-to-peer topologies ($\beta_1(G) = |E| - |V| + \beta_0 > 0$) with switch Host-Managed Device Memory (HDM) permutations $\pi_e \in S_{64}$ enable Confused-Deputy escalation unless chords are clamped, while long-running Vieta polynomials require non-escalating root reclamation after shootdown completion.
  \item \textbf{Protocol deadlocks on completions, hard-HBM scrub routing, and coherency snoops:} Silently dropping an unauthorized \texttt{M2S\_Req} read, \texttt{M2S\_RwD} write, or \texttt{S2M\_DRS} completion hangs the host Miss Status Holding Register (MSHR) until a Completion Timeout (CTO) raises a fatal Machine Check (\texttt{\#MC}). Likewise, commercial FPGA HBM hard IP blocks ignore custom \texttt{aruser} bits, while blocking an \texttt{M2S\_BIRsp} response, dropping a snoop-solicited \texttt{DirtyEvict}, or losing unsealed Mode-C page indices during \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} walks deadlocks the Home Agent.
\end{enumerate}

\noindent\textbf{Bridging Zero Trust to Interconnect Layers 1--2.}\;Prevailing Zero Trust Architectures (NIST SP~800-207~\cite{rose2020zta}) enforce micro-segmentation at OSI Layers~3--7, implicitly trusting board traces, CXL switches, and OS/hypervisor Fabric Managers once a host attaches to a memory pool. In disaggregated \mbox{CXL~3.0--4.0} and \mbox{UCIe~1.1--3.0} fabrics---and in autonomous scientific platforms such as the U.S.\ Department of Energy (DOE) Genesis Mission and American Science Cloud (\texttt{AmSC})~\cite{doe2025genesis}---a rogue DMA engine, compromised FM, or misconfigured agent tool call bypasses Layer~3--7 gateways by issuing direct cacheline loads, stores, and snoops at $250\text{~MHz}$--$1.0\text{~GHz}$. Enforcing Zero Trust at Interconnect Layers~1--2 requires an inline Policy Enforcement Point (PEP) on the memory-controller boundary that verifies every 128-bit sub-flit slot in $O(1)$ gate depth without trusting the wire, the host OS/FM, intermediate switches, downstream AI consumers, or the EDA synthesis toolchain~\cite{mitra2000commonmode, ncdsmo2020rain}.

\noindent\textbf{Architectural Goal \& Six Unified Contributions.}\;Figure~\ref{fig:pipeline} illustrates \textsc{Kernel Zero}, a cut-through capability interposer enforcing bidirectional Layer-1/2 Zero-Trust sub-flit isolation in $O(1)$ gate depth using $0\text{~BRAM}$ and $0\text{~B}$ of DRAM permission tables. Preserving $100\%$ of the frozen \BaselineVersion\ physical core netlist ($+0$ added core logic gates), this monograph  unites the hardware datapath and its complete dynamic lifecycle across six contributions:
\begin{enumerate}
\renewcommand{\theenumi}{(\arabic{enumi})}\renewcommand{\labelenumi}{\theenumi}
  \item \textbf{Hierarchical Address Splitting, Vieta Sieve, \& Shadow B\'{e}zout Deflation:} Multiscale $\mathrm{HPA}[51:6]$ partitioning ($\mathcal{X}_{n+1} = \mathcal{X}_n \oplus \mathcal{W}_n$~\cite{chen2001multilevel}) with orthogonal 64-bit masks (\texttt{CSR 0x00/0x08} and dual-banked \texttt{0x60\_bank[0:1]/0x68}), $J(64,w)$ and $\mathbb{F}_8$ Steiner $S(2,8,64)$ coding, a Cycle-0 $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ Vieta / 64-bucket timing-wheel sieve~\cite{varghese1997timing}, 8-step inactive shadow-bank B\'{e}zout--Horner root reclamation, and 0-LUT $\beta_{\min}=\texttt{8'h20}$ normal-basis Frobenius equivariance~\cite{mullin1989optimal, lidl1997finite, diamond2025binius} (Section~\ref{sec:multiscale});
  \item \textbf{Spanning-Forest Clamping, \texttt{LUT6\_2} Rate-Distortion / Popcount Sketch, \& \texttt{spectralc}:} Spanning-forest propagation, coarse-aperture HDM gauge flattening ($L_j \equiv 1$) vs.\ $S_{64}$ orbit clamping ($L_j \le 64$), PHY-gated dual-forest failover $(T_0,T_1)$, $+0$-LUT fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} MOESI, 4-tier mantissa clamping ($u \in \{0,9,12,16\}$~\cite{rouhani2023microscaling}), adjacent-pair popcount pre-reduction, Wannamaker/Assouad/rectangular-BBP LoRA SVD bounds~\cite{wannamaker2000nonsubtractive, benaych2012singular, assouad1983deux}, and Schur-convex \texttt{SKILL.md} compilation~\cite{mcconnell2011certifying, doe2025genesis} (Sections~\ref{sec:forest} and~\ref{sec:traceability});
  \item \textbf{Renewal-Reward Goodput, Martingale Tails, \& Mode-C Snoop Compaction:} Renewal-reward goodput, Martingale Network Calculus tail bounds~\cite{poloczek2014martingale}, hitless dual-epoch grace windows, a \ScrubShimPhysicalLUTs-LUT Per-\texttt{ARID} ordered AXI4 read-scrub shim, and 0-LUT Home-Agent Mode-C forward-Feistel \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} synthesis~\cite{luby1988construct} with unbounded dual-bank compaction within $\le 500\text{~ns}$ / $548\text{~ns}$ (Sections~\ref{sec:pipeline} and~\ref{sec:power});
  \item \textbf{CSS Split-Coset Syndrome Exactness \& Non-Abelian Hop Delegation:} Dual-subflit \texttt{LOpt} CRC48 + Tail CRC64 Calderbank--Shor--Steane (CSS) split-coset tunneling ($H_{\mathrm{FEC}} \circ E_{\mathrm{fold}} \equiv \mathbf{0}$~\cite{calderbank1996good, reed1960polynomial}, $8.0\text{~ns}$ recovery, 6 cohorts/flit), Hermitian $\mathcal{H}_q$ scaling, 1-\texttt{LUT4} coprime $\chi_{33}\times\chi_{31}\circ\mathcal{L}_5$ Carter--Wegman MAC~\cite{daemen1995cipher, carter1979universal}, and non-abelian $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}$ / 8-round Davies--Meyer capability attenuation~\cite{birgisson2014macaroons} (Sections~\ref{sec:pipeline} and~\ref{sec:formal});
  \item \textbf{Multi-Track Silicon Sign-Off, De~Morgan \texttt{RAIN} Diversity, \& \texttt{ZK-IC3} Liveness:} Post-route 16nm \texttt{xcvu47p} and live AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} Dual-AFI execution, placed-and-routed open-source 7nm \texttt{ASAP7} predictive PDK CMOS~\cite{clark2016asap7}, CNCF \texttt{k3s} webhook HIL, Tropical $(\max,+) \otimes S_{64}$ pod federation, Mode-D De~Morgan \texttt{RAIN} dual-sieves (\texttt{64'hABABABAB\_FEFEFEFE}~\cite{mitra2000commonmode}), Dillon $\mathcal{PS}^\mp$ bent/DOM duality~\cite{gross2016dom, dillon1974elementary, rothaus1976bent}, and 137-tick ranking-supermartingale \texttt{ZK-IC3} attestation~\cite{chakarov2013probabilistic, bradley2011sat} (Sections~\ref{sec:pipeline}--\ref{sec:traceability}); and
  \item \textbf{99-Theorem Zero-Axiom Lean~4 Unification (\LeanTheoremTotal\ Core + 12 Physics Corollaries):} Pure \texttt{Init.Prelude} term-mode Lean~4 proofs of all \LeanTheoremTotal\ core architectural theorems (\texttt{thm00}--\texttt{thm61} and \id{SheafCosetIsometry.lean}: \LeanPartOneTotal\ baseline + \LeanNewExpansion\ lifecycle + \LeanFastCoreTotal\ fast-core + \LeanBridgeTotal\ cross-layer structural lemmas) plus 12 cross-domain mathematical-physics corollaries (\texttt{thm62}--\texttt{thm73} in \id{CxlSubflitPhysicsCorollaries.lean}; 99 headline theorems across 280 kernel declarations, \texttt{0 sorry}, \texttt{0 axioms}) distilled into four canonical hardware invariants (Sections~\ref{sec:formal} and~\ref{sec:traceability}).
\end{enumerate}

\noindent\textbf{Why Four Field Equations Emerge From One Gate.}\;We did not set out to invent twenty separate laws of physics. Rather, compressing multi-tenant memory isolation down to a single \StageOneSieveDelayNs\ \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} primitive (\texttt{64'h2A2A2A2A\_80808080}) and a 64-bit $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ shift register strips away every software indirection until only \textbf{Four Governing Field Equations (Laws~I--IV)} remain. Under this compression, the datapath's invariants reveal themselves as exact \textbf{Discrete Noether Currents} of the interposer's finite symmetry group; the dual output pins (\texttt{O5}, \texttt{O6}) act as a self-adjoint unitary scattering $S$-matrix $S = \bigl[\begin{smallmatrix}R & T\\ T & R\end{smallmatrix}\bigr]$ ($T = \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}, R = \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}$) whose trace identity $\mathrm{Tr}(T^\dagger T) + \mathrm{Tr}(R^\dagger R) \equiv 64$ unites Rolf Landauer's 1957 ballistic conductance formula~\cite{landauer1957spatial} on pin~\texttt{O5} with his 1961 bit-erasure dissipation floor~\cite{1} on pin~\texttt{O6}; and the finite hardware registers (\texttt{CSR 0x38}, \texttt{0x50}, \texttt{0x60}) serve as the exact discretized parent operators behind the macroscopic BBP singular-value, Cram\'{e}r--Lundberg queueing, and MacWilliams coding limits closed in Section~\ref{sec:compiler} (Table~\ref{tab:additional_exact_laws} and Remark~\ref{rem:noether_parent_synthesis}).

\vspace{-0.55em}
\subsection{Five-Part Verification Methodology and Netlist Separation}
\label{sec:verification_methodology}
\enlargethispage{1.15\baselineskip}
\vspace{-0.25em}
Every mathematical, architectural, and silicon claim in this monograph is cross-gated by five deterministic checks (Table~\ref{tab:provenance}), with an explicit boundary between discrete formal mechanization and continuous stochastic analysis:
\begin{enumerate}[topsep=1.0pt,itemsep=0.15pt,parsep=0pt]
  \item \textbf{Check 1 --- Pure \texttt{Init.Prelude} Lean~4 Kernel (99 Zero-Axiom Headline Theorems: \LeanTheoremTotal\ Core + 12 Physics~\cite{demoura2021lean4}, contrasted with Coq RTL frameworks~\cite{choi2017kami}):} Whereas dependent-type hardware frameworks such as MIT Kami~\cite{choi2017kami} verify parametric Bluespec-style rules in Coq, Check~1 machine-checks all 99 discrete finite-field, Boolean, LUT-SRAM, Peano-inductive, linear-cochain, and mathematical-physics headline theorems (\texttt{thm00}--\texttt{thm61}, \LeanFastCoreTotal\ fast-core theorems in \id{SheafCosetIsometry.lean}, and \texttt{thm62}--\texttt{thm73} in \id{CxlSubflitPhysicsCorollaries.lean}; 280 total kernel declarations) in pure \texttt{Init.Prelude} Lean~4 term-mode with \texttt{0 sorry} and \texttt{0 axioms}. Although the published \texttt{zkPi} toolchain~\cite{laufer2024zkpi} ingests exported Lean~3 kernel environments (where $85.9\%$ of \texttt{mathlib} declarations fail to complete, $88.9\%$ of those due to unsupported quotient, string, or nested-inductive primitives, alongside a $5.48\times$ tactic blowup), prohibiting tactics (\texttt{by}), \texttt{String} literals, \texttt{Quot.sound}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{funext}, \texttt{propext}, and nested recursive inductives---encoding bytes, pairs, degree-8 polynomials, and 4-symbol codewords as flat single-constructor inductives (\texttt{B8}, \texttt{PairB8}, \texttt{Poly8}, \texttt{CssBlock4}) and discharging $\mathrm{GF}(2^8)$ identities in 8 basis evaluations via \texttt{lem\_linear\_eq\_on\_basis}---places all 99 Lean~4 proofs directly inside the identical Calculus of Inductive Constructions fragment $\mathcal{L}_I'$ via a thin \texttt{lean4export} AST bridge.
  \item \textbf{Check 2 --- Analytical Proofs \& Python Stochastic/Matrix Verification (\id{sim/benchmark_workload_and_martingale.py}):} Continuous and asymptotic probability bounds---including hypergeometric aliasing expectations (Theorem~\ref{thm:johnson}(iii)), Wannamaker non-subtractive rounding variance and Le~Cam/Assouad lower bounds (Theorem~\ref{thm:dither_lower_bound}), rectangular Benaych-Georges--Nadakuditi BBP singular-value phase transitions (Corollary~\ref{cor:bbp_wall}), concrete $(256,250)$ Reed--Solomon parity-check evaluation, renewal-reward goodput (Theorem~\ref{thm:goodput}), Doob supermartingale queueing tails (Section~\ref{sec:power}), and Schur-complement power---are proved analytically and cross-checked numerically across GAPBS, vLLM KV-cache, DeepSeek/Mixtral MoE, and 100,000-op \texttt{mmap BAR0} traces.
  \item \textbf{Check 3 --- ANSI-C11 \texttt{BAR0} ABI \& TRL-8 Kubernetes HIL:} Verifies the prototype/simulation PCI-SIG DVSEC header (\texttt{Vendor 0x1E98}, experimental \texttt{DVSEC 0x0023}, \texttt{Device 0x4B4C}; parameterizable to licensee PCI-SIG Vendor ID), all 16 64-bit CSRs (\texttt{0x00}--\texttt{0x78} $= 128\text{~B}$), and the live CNCF \texttt{k3s} \texttt{ValidatingAdmissionWebhook} (\id{TRL8_K8S_OPERATOR_HIL_RECEIPT.json}).
  \item \textbf{Check 4 --- Netlist Equivalence Check \& Inductive \texttt{ZK-LEC} / \texttt{ZK-IC3}:} Enforces the repository separation retaining all Zone-1~\texttt{.sv}, \texttt{.edf}, and~\texttt{.dcp} sources inside the private vault with 0~\texttt{.sv} files in Zone~2 (\id{02_GitHub_Repo_Staging}). Check~4 verifies \id{ZK_LEC_MITER_RECEIPT.json} anchored to $h_{\mathrm{netlist}} = \mathrm{SHA\text{-}256}(\mathrm{EDIF}_{\mathrm{vault}})$ across $100\%$ of $2^{w_k}$ combinational cone states ($w_k \le 24$), 1-step inductive sequential invariance (\texttt{Init}, \texttt{Consecution}, \texttt{Miter Safety}), and 65,536-cycle FIFO/rollover trace equivalence.
  \item \textbf{Check 5 --- Vivado~\VivadoToolVersion, Open-Source 7nm \texttt{ASAP7} Predictive PDK, \& Live Dual-AFI AWS F2 Silicon:} Attests post-route 16nm \texttt{xcvu47p} closure, placed-and-routed open-source 7nm FinFET \texttt{ASAP7} predictive PDK (\texttt{asap7sc7p5t})~\cite{clark2016asap7} CMOS sign-off, and live hardware execution inside an account-locked private AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} enclave across Baseline BIST/TVLA AFI \texttt{\AwsAgfiId} (\AwsBistSlots{} slots, 19,990-trace on-die digital toggle/timing Welch TVLA $\max|t| = 0.0 < 4.5$) and Production 16~GB HBM2 DMA AFI \texttt{\AwsHbmAgfiId} (\AwsLiveBursts{} bursts / \AwsLiveSlots{} slots, $100\%$ \texttt{0xAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA} sentinel retention).
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{Asymptotic Complexity and the Transient Revocation Occupancy Bound}
\label{sec:complexity_silicon}
Table~\ref{tab:complexity} contrasts the asymptotic time, space, Boolean circuit-depth, and communication complexity of \textsc{Kernel Zero} against stateful DRAM permission tables (\textit{Space-Control}~\cite{goswami2026spacecontrol}), tagged capabilities (\textit{CHERI / Morello}~\cite{woodruff2014cheri, watson2015cheri}), and stateless tail-CRC poisoners. At first glance, policing 4-KB page cohorts across a memory pool of $M$ bytes using $0\text{~BRAM}$ and $128\text{~B}$ of \texttt{BAR0} registers appears to conflict with elementary counting bounds: if $M/4096$ pages carried arbitrary, unauthenticated states stored solely inside the memory controller, $\Omega(M)$ bits of controller RAM and $\Theta(M)$ revocation sweeps would be unavoidable~\cite{goswami2026spacecontrol, filardo2020cornucopia, filardo2024cornucopiareloaded}.

\noindent\textbf{Four-Stage Capability Lifecycle.}\;\textsc{Kernel Zero} avoids $\Omega(M)$ controller RAM because capability state traverses four stages: \textit{(Stage~I: Resting Wire Capability, $t < 0$)} baseline page authorization is cryptographically authenticated in the wire capability ($B_{\mathrm{rest}} \equiv 0$ bits of controller RAM); \textit{(Stage~II: Inline Bitmask Sieve, $t = 0$)} Stage~1 evaluates $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} \oplus \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} = \mathrm{Id}$ in \StageOneSieveDelayNs\ while scheduled TTL expirations clear in $O(1)$ ticks on the 64-bucket timing wheel (\texttt{CSR 0x68}~\cite{varghese1997timing}, $\Delta d_{\mathrm{rev}} = 0$); \textit{(Stage~III: Shootdown Drain Window, $t_{\mathrm{rev}}$)} \texttt{CSR 0x60} tracks \emph{only admission-paced emergency mid-lease revocations} across the bounded invalidation-and-drain window $\Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}}$; and \textit{(Stage~IV: Shadow-Bank Deflation and Rotation)} completed roots are retired via 8-step shadow-bank B\'{e}zout--Horner deflation or staggered $\texttt{2'b11}$ bank rotation. Proposition~\ref{prop:little_vieta} establishes the exact information-theoretic and sample-path bounds.

\begin{proposition}[Transient Revocation Occupancy and Windowed Degree Bound]
\label{prop:little_vieta}
Consider a memory pool of $M$ bytes ($N_{\mathrm{page}} = M / B_{\mathrm{page}}$ pages) with cohort descriptor alphabet $\Sigma_{\mathrm{cid}} = \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$ ($|\Sigma_{\mathrm{cid}}| = 255$). Let scheduled lease expirations clear on \texttt{CSR 0x68}~\cite{varghese1997timing} at $\Delta d_{\mathrm{rev}} = 0$, and let $A(s, t)$ denote the counting process of emergency mid-lease cohort revocations admitted during $(s, t]$. Define the deterministic invalidation-and-drain window
\[
\Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}} := \Delta T_{\mathrm{res}}(K_{\mathrm{dir}}, K_{\mathrm{dirty}}) + T_{\mathrm{pipe+FIFO}},
\]
where $\Delta T_{\mathrm{res}} \le 500.0\text{~ns}$ bounds 64-line all-dirty \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} + \texttt{DirtyEvict} completion (Proposition~\ref{prop:bisnp_shootdown}) and $T_{\mathrm{pipe+FIFO}} = (4 + 8)t_{\mathrm{clk}} = 12\,t_{\mathrm{clk}}$ bounds the 4-stage pipeline plus 32-slot \texttt{M2S\_RwD} FIFO drain ($48.0\text{~ns}$ at $250\text{~MHz}$ on 16nm \texttt{xcvu47p}, giving $\Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}} \le 548.0\text{~ns}$ or $137$ ticks; $17.3\text{~ns}$ at $693\text{~MHz}$ in 7nm \texttt{ASAP7}, giving $\Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}} \approx 269.0\text{~ns}$). Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Cut-Set State Conservation vs.\ $\Omega(M)$ Stateful Tables:} Under an arbitrary resting permission assignment $\Pi^{(0)} \in \{0,1\}^{N_{\mathrm{page}}}$ of Shannon entropy $H(\Pi^{(0)}) = N_{\mathrm{page}}$ bits, any inline verifier whose request wire carries unauthenticated addresses ($I(\Pi^{(0)}; \mathrm{Msg}_i) = 0$) requires $B_{\mathrm{ctrl}} \ge H(\Pi^{(0)}) = M / B_{\mathrm{page}} = \Omega(M)$ bits of controller storage. Committing $\Pi^{(0)}$ under a 128-bit MAC key $K_{\mathrm{active}}$ into wire-carried capability descriptors yields $I(\Pi^{(0)}; S_{\mathrm{ctrl}} \mid K_{\mathrm{active}}) = 0$, reducing mutable datapath state to encoding the transient in-flight revocation subset $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{trans}}(t) \subseteq \Sigma_{\mathrm{cid}}$ of size $|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{trans}}(t)| \le d_{\max}$:
  \[
  B_{\mathrm{state}} \ge \left\lceil \log_2 \sum_{r=0}^{d_{\max}} \binom{255}{r} \right\rceil, \qquad
  B_{\mathrm{Vieta}} = d_{\max}\log_2\bigl|\mathbb{F}_{2^8}\bigr| = 8 \times 8 = 64\text{~bits}\quad(d_{\max} = 8),
  \]
  \noindent independently of pool capacity $M$;
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Sample-Path Time-Average Occupancy:} Let cohort $k$'s revocation arrive at $\tau_k$ and remain resident in the active bank for $W_k \in (0, \Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}}]$. Whenever the sample-path limits $\lambda_{\mathrm{rev}} := \lim_{T\to\infty} A(0,T)/T < \infty$ and $\overline{W} := \lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^n W_k \le \Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}}$ exist~\cite{little1961proof, stidham1974sample}, the time-average active polynomial degree satisfies
  \[
  \lim_{T\to\infty} \frac{1}{T}\int_0^T d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)}\,dt = \lambda_{\mathrm{rev}}\,\overline{W} \le \lambda_{\mathrm{rev}}\,\Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}};
  \]
  \item[(iii)] \textbf{Synchronous Dual-Bank Window Conservation:} Partition time into rotation epochs $t_n = n\,\Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}}$ ($n \in \mathbb{N}$). Suppose at each boundary $t_n$ the \texttt{BAR0} driver admits $b_n \le 4$ queued revocations (which complete shootdown and drain by $t_n + \Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}} = t_{n+1}$) and during $(t_n, t_{n+1})$ admits $m_n \le 2$ unqueued mid-window revocations into both the active and shadow banks (which complete shootdown during $(t_{n+1}, t_{n+2})$). Then for all $n \in \mathbb{N}$, $d_{\mathrm{base}}(t_n^+) = m_{n-1} + b_n \le 6$ ($m_{-1} := 0$) and $\sup_{t \in [t_n, t_{n+1})} d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} = m_{n-1} + b_n + m_n \le 8$ (Lean~4 \texttt{thm31}, \texttt{thm57}), retiring $b_n + m_{n-1} \le 6$ completed roots at $t_{n+1}$ and sustaining $\lambda_{\mathrm{rev,max}}^{\mathrm{sync}} = 6 / \Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}} \approx 1.095 \times 10^7\text{~rev/s}$ per port at $250\text{~MHz}$ ($2.23 \times 10^7\text{~rev/s}$ at $693\text{~MHz}$) with $\le 64$ flip-flop updates per swap rather than $\Theta(M)$ capability sweeps~\cite{filardo2020cornucopia, filardo2024cornucopiareloaded}; and
  \item[(iv)] \textbf{Asynchronous Sliding-Window Semaphore Bound with Shadow B\'{e}zout Deflation:} Under arbitrary continuous arrival times $\{\tau_k\}$ without boundary synchronization, reclaiming each completed root on the inactive shadow bank via 8-step B\'{e}zout--Horner division ($T_{\mathrm{defl}} = 8\,t_{\mathrm{clk}} = 32.0\text{~ns}$, Theorem~\ref{thm:bezout_galois}) bounds per-root occupancy to $\Delta T_{\mathrm{eff}} := \Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}} + T_{\mathrm{defl}} \le 580.0\text{~ns}$. Enforcing an 8-permit host sliding-window semaphore $A(t - \Delta T_{\mathrm{eff}}, t) \le 8$ guarantees $d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} = A(t - \Delta T_{\mathrm{eff}}, t) \le 8$ for all $t \ge 0$ at up to $\lambda_{\mathrm{rev,max}}^{\mathrm{async}} = 8 / \Delta T_{\mathrm{eff}} \approx 1.379 \times 10^7\text{~rev/s}$, and any $(\sigma, \rho)$-regulated revocation burst~\cite{cruz1991calculus} with $A_{\mathrm{req}}(s,t) \le \sigma + \rho(t-s)$ and $\rho \le \lambda_{\mathrm{rev,max}}^{\mathrm{async}}$ incurs host admission delay at most $D_{\mathrm{host}} \le \max\!\bigl(0,\, (\sigma - 8)/\lambda_{\mathrm{rev,max}}^{\mathrm{async}} + \Delta T_{\mathrm{eff}}\bigr)$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{proposition}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Proposition~\ref{prop:little_vieta}]
For Clause~(i), exact recovery of $\pi^{(0)}_i$ across all $N_{\mathrm{page}}$ pages implies $N_{\mathrm{page}} = H(\Pi^{(0)}) \allowbreak\le I(\Pi^{(0)};\allowbreak S_{\mathrm{ctrl}}, \mathrm{Msg}_{1:N_{\mathrm{page}}})$; when wire requests carry only unauthenticated addresses, $I(\Pi^{(0)};\allowbreak \mathrm{Msg}_{1:N_{\mathrm{page}}} \mid S_{\mathrm{ctrl}}) = 0 \allowbreak\Longrightarrow\allowbreak H(S_{\mathrm{ctrl}}) \ge N_{\mathrm{page}}$, whereas self-authenticated wire capabilities supply $\pi^{(0)}_i$ per slot so $S_{\mathrm{ctrl}}^{(t)}$ stores only the degree-$\le 8$ Vieta polynomial over $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}$ ($64\text{~bits}$). Clause~(ii) is Stidham's sample-path theorem for $L = \lambda W$~\cite{little1961proof, stidham1974sample} applied to indicator residency $\mathbb{I}(\tau_k \le t < \tau_k + W_k)$. For Clause~(iii), every cohort admitted at or before $t_n$ (both the $m_{n-1}$ mid-window arrivals in $(t_{n-1}, t_n)$ and the $b_n$ boundary arrivals at $t_n$) has elapsed $\ge t_{n+1} - t_n = \Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}}$ by $t_{n+1}$, so atomic swap at $t_{n+1}$ to the shadow bank holding only the $m_n \le 2$ arrivals from $(t_n, t_{n+1})$ plus $b_{n+1} \le 4$ new boundary arrivals inductively preserves $d_{\mathrm{base}}(t_{n+1}^+) \le 6$ and $d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} \le 8$ (\texttt{thm31}, \texttt{thm57}). For Clause~(iv), each root admitted at $\tau_k$ is deflated by $\tau_k + \Delta T_{\mathrm{eff}}$, so $d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} \le A(t - \Delta T_{\mathrm{eff}}, t) \le 8$; the horizontal deviation between arrival curve $\alpha(t) = \sigma + \rho t$ and service curve $\beta(t) = 8\,\mathbb{I}(t > 0) + \lambda_{\mathrm{rev,max}}^{\mathrm{async}} t$~\cite{cruz1991calculus} yields the stated $D_{\mathrm{host}}$ bound.
\end{proof}

\noindent\textbf{So What? (The Horizon Collapse: Turning Pooled Memory Space into 548 Nanoseconds of Time).}\;Proposition~\ref{prop:little_vieta} explains why controller memory no longer has to grow with pool capacity $M$: once resting capabilities travel on the wire ($B_{\mathrm{rest}}=0$) and routine TTL leases expire on the 64-bucket timing wheel (\texttt{CSR 0x68}, $\Delta d_{\mathrm{rev}}=0$), the hardware register \texttt{CSR 0x60} only has to remember emergency revocations across the $548.0\text{~ns}$ shootdown-and-drain horizon ($64\text{~bits}$, \CoreBRAM\ BRAM), decoupling security state from rack capacity forever.

\noindent\textbf{Standing on Classical Foundations (The 60-Year Mathematical Debt).}\;Almost none of the underlying mathematics in this monograph had to be invented from scratch. Sanitizing four parallel 128-bit CXL slots in \CorePhysicalLUTs\ CLB LUTs and $0\text{~BRAM}$ is possible only because Landauer~(1957/1961)~\cite{1, landauer1957spatial}, Tutte and Nash-Williams~(1961)~\cite{tutte1961problem, nashwilliams1961edge}, Little~(1961)~\cite{little1961proof}, Kautz and Singleton~(1964)~\cite{kautz1964nonrandom}, Welch and Sidelnikov~(1971/1974)~\cite{welch1974lower}, Carter and Wegman~(1979)~\cite{carter1979universal}, Goguen and Meseguer~(1982)~\cite{goguen1982security}, Daemen~(1995)~\cite{daemen1995cipher}, and Varghese and Lauck~(1997)~\cite{varghese1997timing} established the governing scattering, graph-theoretic, queueing, superimposed-coding, non-interference, cellular-automaton, and timer-wheel invariants decades ago. Our sole contribution is recognizing that a rotated finite-field coordinate system maps those classical theorems isomorphically onto the dual \texttt{O5}/\texttt{O6} pins of a single physical 6-input lookup table and a 64-bit $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ shift register at CXL wire speed.

\noindent\textbf{Scope \& Price Paid (No Free Lunch --- Transient Admission Pacing vs.\ Unbounded Single-Page Churn).}\;Achieving $O(1)$ ($64\text{-bit}$) controller state across arbitrary pool capacity $M$ trades away ungrouped, unbounded mid-lease revocation bursts: it requires self-authenticated wire capabilities ($B_{\mathrm{rest}}=0$) and admission-paced emergency revocations ($\le 1.095 \times 10^7\text{~rev/s}$ per port at $250\text{~MHz}$, alongside unbounded scheduled TTL expirations on \texttt{CSR 0x68}). Workloads generating higher-rate ungrouped single-page emergency revocations queue at the host token bucket by $\le \Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}}$ or fall back to a cold-tier DRAM capability table~\cite{goswami2026spacecontrol}.

\caption{The Cut-Through Sub-Flit Sanitizer Pipeline (\id{cxl_subflit_slot_sanitizer_core} in Stages~1--2: \CorePhysicalLUTs~physical CLB LUTs and \CoreTotalFFs~FDCE registers with 8-way replicated violation registers bounding fanout to 16~loads/FF; Cycle-0 Mode-C / Time-Wheel / Vieta $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ pre-core evaluator: \PreCoreModeCLUTs~LUTs and \CycleZeroFfs~FFs; soft-logic Per-\texttt{ARID} Ordered AXI4 Read-Scrub Responder Shim: \ScrubShimPhysicalLUTs~CLB LUTs and \ScrubShimFFs~FFs; outer wrapper in Stages~3--4 totaling \WrapperEnclaveLUTs~LUTs and \WrapperEnclaveFFs~registers, \CoreBRAM~Block RAM).\vspace{3pt}\\
\textbf{So What? (Architectural Consequence --- Surgical Slot Isolation Without Neighbor Stalls).}\;For five decades, memory-protection hardware has obeyed a volumetric tax: guarding $M$ bytes of physical memory at page or cacheline granularity demanded $\Theta(M)$ bits of DRAM permission tables plus an on-die SRAM translation cache that stalls the pipeline by $32$--$68\text{~ns}$ whenever sparse AI or graph workloads thrash the cache. Figure~\ref{fig:pipeline} replaces table walks with an inline, zero-BRAM Boolean and finite-field sieve that partitions four parallel 128-bit sub-flit slots every $4.0\text{~ns}$ in constant $\Theta(1)$ circuit depth (\StageOneSieveDelayNs\ Stage-1 \texttt{LUT6\_2} sieve + \StageTwoCriticalPathNs\ Stage-2 clamp), reclaiming $100\%$ of the $250\text{~GB/rack}$ permission-table carve-out while leaving co-packed innocent slots completely untouched.}
\label{fig:pipeline}
\end{figure*}

\section{Background, Sub-Flit Wire Extraction, and Five-Boundary Zero-Trust Threat Model}
\label{sec:background}

\subsection{CXL~3.0--4.0 and UCIe 512-Bit Flit Phases and Disjoint 128-Bit Slot Wire Extraction}
\label{sec:wire}
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\;Network Zero Trust (NIST SP~800-207~\cite{rose2020zta}) assumes a packetized world in which every message owns its own header, frame check sequence, and retry fate. That assumption breaks at Interconnect Layers~1--2: on a 512-bit \texttt{CXL.mem} or \texttt{CXL.cache} bus running at $250\text{~MHz}$--$1.0\text{~GHz}$, four independent 128-bit protocol slots from different tenants share a single clock phase and a single tail CRC, while direct cacheline loads, stores, and coherency snoops bypass OS/hypervisor gateways altogether. Enforcing Zero Trust on the memory bus therefore requires an unrotated wire coordinate map that extracts, authenticates, and sanitizes each 128-bit slot in place without poisoning co-packed neighbors or deadlocking upstream Miss Status Holding Registers (MSHRs) and Home-Agent snoop engines.\par\smallskip

In released \mbox{CXL~3.0--4.0} (64~GT/s PAM-4, and projected \mbox{CXL~5.0} 128~GT/s extensions~\cite{cxl2025spec, dassharma2024cxl}) and \mbox{UCIe~1.1--3.0} Flit-aware Die-to-Die Interfaces (FDI, up to 64~GT/s raw/streaming flit framing and fast emergency sideband failover~\cite{ucie2025spec30}), internal digital pipelines process 512-bit (64-byte) flit phases (\texttt{s\_flit\_payload\_i[511:0]}) indexed by \texttt{flit\_phase\_id\_i[1:0]} $\in \{0,1,2,3\}$. Each 512-bit phase partitions into four 128-bit slots $s_0, s_1, s_2, s_3 \in \mathbb{F}_2^{128}$. Master-to-subordinate \texttt{CXL.mem} requests use \texttt{M2S\_Req}, \texttt{M2S\_RwD}, and \texttt{M2S\_BIRsp}, whereas subordinate-to-master completions use \texttt{S2M\_NDR}, \texttt{S2M\_DRS}, and \texttt{S2M\_BISnp}; coherent \texttt{CXL.cache} requests use \texttt{D2H\_Req} (\texttt{RdShared}, \texttt{RdOwn}, \texttt{RdAny}). Phase~0, $\mathrm{Slot}_0[15:0]$ carries the 16-bit Link-Layer Flit Header ($\mathrm{Slot}_0[3:0]$ credits and $\mathrm{Slot}_0[15:4]$ \texttt{H0..H5} slot formats). Decoupling $\mathrm{SPID}_i[13:0]$ onto \texttt{s\_slot\_spid\_i[55:0]} and per-slot epoch bank selector $b_i = \texttt{slot\_bank\_sel\_i}[i]$ yields three strictly disjoint wire formats plus an ordered read-scrub shim:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Mode A --- Native CPI/FDI Normalized Slot Layout:} On internal CPI/FDI buses, the upstream range/MAC decoder and Cycle-0 Vieta evaluator drive the four authenticated 64-bit capability vectors $r_0,\ldots,r_3 \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$ onto \texttt{s\_slot\_cap\_vec\_i[255:0]} and latch \texttt{slot\_coarse\_arena\_ok\_i[3:0]}. Let $\delta_0 = 16$ on Phase-0 $\mathrm{Slot}_0$ and $\delta_i = 0$ otherwise ($16 + 80 = 96 \le 128$ bits). Slice $\mathrm{Slot}_i[\delta_i+5:\delta_i]$ (6b) encodes channel, opcode, and \texttt{D2H\_Req} MOESI bits $x_i[1:0] \in \{\mathrm{I}=\texttt{2'b00}, \mathrm{S}=\texttt{2'b01}, \mathrm{E}=\texttt{2'b10}, \mathrm{M}=\texttt{2'b11}\}$, clamped in Stage~2 to $\ell_{\mathrm{eff}} = \min(x_i, c_i)$ against slot $i$'s 2-bit ceiling $c_i[1:0]$. Slice $\mathrm{Slot}_i[\delta_i+21:\delta_i+6]$ (16b) preserves \texttt{Tag\_ID[15:0]}, while $\mathrm{Slot}_i[\delta_i+67:\delta_i+22] = \mathrm{HPA}_i[51:6]$ (46b) partitions into the 23-bit coarse aperture $\mathrm{HPA}_i[51:29]$, 17-bit fine 4-KB page $\mathrm{HPA}_i[28:12]$, and 6-bit cacheline offset $\mathrm{HPA}_i[11:6]$. Finally, $\mathrm{Slot}_i[\delta_i+79:\delta_i+68]$ (12b) carries \texttt{LD\_ID\_i[5:0]} and \texttt{Meta\_Tag\_i[5:0]} (encoding timing-wheel bucket $\tau_i = \texttt{Meta\_Tag}_i[5:0] \in \{0,\ldots,63\}$).
  \item \textbf{Mode B --- Enclave-to-Enclave Paired-MAC (Mode B.1) \& Split-Coset Tunneling (Mode B.2):} Mode~B.1 (for non-IDE links) packs $r_i[63:0]$ into $\mathrm{Slot}_i[127:64]$ and $\{\mathrm{LD\_ID}_i[5:0], \mathrm{HPA}_i[51:6], \mathrm{Tag\_ID}_i[7:0], \mathrm{Opcode}_i[3:0]\}$ into $\mathrm{Slot}_i[63:0]$ alongside a paired 64-bit MAC slot ($<2^{-63}$ forgery). Mode~B.2 (for 96-bit CXL IDE AES-GCM links) imposes $0\%$ slot-capacity tax. When $\texttt{LOpt\_Mode\_En} = 0$, four 8-bit cohort descriptors ($\mathrm{cid}_3 \parallel \cdots \parallel \mathrm{cid}_0$) are encrypted via $\mathrm{Enc32}^{(6)}$ into the $[8,4,5]_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)}$ Tail CRC64 coset (Bytes~242--249, Theorem~\ref{thm:modeb}). When $\texttt{LOpt\_Mode\_En} = 1$, a concatenated $[6,2,5]_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)}$ coset in Phase-1 Sub-Flit~0 CRC48 (Bytes~122--127) carries $(\mathrm{cid}_1, \mathrm{cid}_0)$ alongside four descriptors in Tail CRC64, halving cut-through latency to 2~phases ($8.0\text{~ns}$) and expanding capacity to 6~\texttt{cid}s/flit (Theorem~\ref{thm:split_coset}).
  \item \textbf{Mode C --- Pre-Core Zero-Sideband Standard \texttt{CXL.mem} Layout:} For unmodified commercial CXL hosts, the Cycle-0 pre-core unsealer (\PreCoreModeCLUTs~CLB LUTs, \CycleZeroFfs~FFs) decrypts the even-width page slice $2n \in \{24,34,36,40\}$ via $\mathrm{Dec}_{K_{\mathrm{seal}}, T_i}^{(6)}$~\cite{luby1988construct} (binding $\mathrm{SPID}_i$ into tweak $T_i$). Cycle~0 then evaluates $\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}(\mathrm{cid}_i)$, the 1-hot timing-wheel vector $\texttt{64'd1} \ll \tau_i$, or the Vieta non-root check $(\mathrm{cid}_i[7:0] \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}) \wedge (\Lambda_{b_i}^{(t)}(\mathrm{cid}_i[7:0]) \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}})$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:vieta}). Prior to Stage~1, it strips the upper $(c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g)$ bits $\mathrm{HPA}_i[12+2n-1 : 12+2n-(c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g)] \leftarrow \mathbf{0}$ and qualifies automatic 1-cycle $\texttt{CSR\_0x70}[\mathrm{SPID}_i[5:0]] \leftarrow 0$ lockout strictly on architectural \texttt{M2S\_RwD} write guard failures.
  \item \textbf{Per-\texttt{ARID} Ordered Soft-Logic AXI4 Read-Scrub Responder Shim:} Placed upstream of the \texttt{xcvu47p} HBM hard IP slave port (\ScrubShimPhysicalLUTs~CLB LUTs, \ScrubShimFFs~FFs, $0\text{~BRAM}$), the shim intercepts $\texttt{m\_axi\_aruser\_scrub\_o} = \texttt{1'b1}$ on $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{scrub}} = \mathbf{0}_{46}$ and forces $\texttt{m\_hbm\_axi\_arvalid\_o} = \texttt{1'b0}$ (zero physical HBM Bank~0 Row~0 \texttt{ACT}/\texttt{PRE} activity). It consults a 16-thread 2-bit per-\texttt{ARID} counter (\id{arid_hbm_inflight_cnt_q[0:15]}) to prevent same-\texttt{ARID} completion reordering before returning synthetic zeroed completions (\texttt{512'h0}, with \texttt{RRESP = OKAY} in Zero-Fill Mode or CXL \texttt{Poison} in Architectural Fault Mode).
\end{enumerate}
