\subsection{Zero-Trust Threat Model and Five-Boundary Physical Enforcement}
\label{sec:zta_threat}
Following NIST SP~800-207~\cite{rose2020zta}, NIST SP~800-193~\cite{regenscheid2018pfr}, and the NSA NCDSMO Raise-the-Bar \texttt{RAIN} pattern (\textit{Redundant, Always-Invoked, Independent, Non-Bypassable}~\cite{mitra2000commonmode, ncdsmo2020rain}), the interposer resides on the trusted memory-controller/HBM base die. Root keys are provisioned at cold power-on reset ($\texttt{por\_cold\_rst\_n} = 0$) or via an on-die hardware Root-of-Trust pulse ($\texttt{rot\_epoch\_rekey\_rst\_n} = 0$). Table~\ref{tab:zta_crosswalk} maps the seven Federal/DoD Zero Trust pillars onto hardware CSRs, SystemVerilog Assertions (\texttt{SVA~1\textquotesingle}--\texttt{SVA 12}), and Lean~4 proofs across five untrusted boundaries:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Boundary 1 --- Untrusted Flit Wire \& Co-Packed Neighbor Slots:} An adversary may inject unauthorized \texttt{M2S\_RwD} writes, \texttt{M2S\_Req} reads, null vectors $r_i = \mathbf{0}_{64}$, modulo-64 aliased $\mathrm{SPID}' = \mathrm{SPID} + 64m$, or out-of-order (OOO) DMA replay beats co-packed alongside three innocent tenant slots. Stages~1--2 enforce selective per-slot \texttt{CXL\_NOP} / zero replacement, 8-bit port-prefix + 64-tenant live SPID filtering (\texttt{CSR 0x70/0x78}), and 4-channel 64-cacheline OOO duplicate-beat rejection (\texttt{thm01}, \texttt{thm06}, \texttt{thm14}).
  \item \textbf{Boundary 2 --- Untrusted Host OS, Hypervisor \& Compromised Fabric Manager:} After cold-POR boot, the host OS, hypervisor, and software FM are untrusted and may issue \texttt{BAR0} MMIO writes attempting $0 \rightarrow 1$ re-enablement, flood same-slope revocations, or trigger a warm PCIe FLR ($\texttt{host\_warm\_flr\_rst\_n} = 0$). Hardware isolates security CSRs from $\texttt{host\_warm\_flr\_rst\_n}$, enforces the cold-POR bitwise \texttt{AND}-ratchet $M^{(t+1)} = M^{(t)} \wedge w$ (\texttt{thm02}) and active-bank Vieta root permanence $\mathrm{Roots}(\Lambda^{(t)}) \subseteq \mathrm{Roots}(\Lambda^{(t+1)})$ (\texttt{thm20}) while restricting B\'{e}zout root reclamation strictly to the inactive shadow bank (\texttt{thm35}, \texttt{SVA~1\textquotesingle}). Routine leases expire autonomously via the 64-bucket timing wheel (\texttt{CSR 0x68}, \texttt{thm26}), failover forests switch solely via hardware PCS loss-of-signal $p_{\mathrm{phy}} = \texttt{pcs\_link\_up\_i}$~\cite{ucie2025spec30} (\texttt{thm28}), and $0 \rightarrow 1$ tamper attempts increment \texttt{CSR 0x48}.
  \item \textbf{Boundary 3 --- Untrusted Multi-Hop CXL Switches \& Federated Pods:} Intermediate switches may form cyclic routing graphs ($\beta_1(G) > 0$), apply HDM permutations $\pi_e \in S_{64}$, or monitor reverse credit timing. Hardware enforces port anti-hairpinning, coarse-aperture HDM gauge flattening ($L_j \equiv 1$, \texttt{thm34}) or $S_{64}$ orbit chord clamping $\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}$ (\texttt{thm21}), boundary-only pod transfer summaries (\texttt{thm12}), and constant-delay Stage-4 credit release $\Delta t_{\mathrm{CRD}} \equiv 0$ (\texttt{thm08}).
  \item \textbf{Boundary 4 --- Untrusted AI Consumers \& Shared-Weight Inference Partners:} A co-tenant GPU may issue coherent \texttt{RdOwn} requests to corrupt shared weights, average $Q$ dithered reads via SVD~\cite{carlini2024stealing} to extract rank-$r$ LoRA adapters~\cite{hu2022lora}, or probe HBM Bank~0 contention. Hardware downgrades $\mathrm{RdOwn} \mapsto \mathrm{RdShared}$ (\texttt{thm22}), clamps \texttt{S2M\_DRS} mantissas across 4 tiers ($u \in \{0,9,12,16\}$ bits, \texttt{thm27}) with epoch-static spatial dither or \texttt{CSR 0x38} query lockout ($Q_{\max} = 10,000 < Q_{\mathrm{BBP}}^*$, \texttt{thm33}, \texttt{thm39}, \texttt{SVA 11}), suppresses $\texttt{m\_hbm\_axi\_arvalid\_o} = 0$ on scrubbed reads (\texttt{SVA 9}), and bounds \texttt{CSR 0x58} leakage to $\le \log_2(M_{\mathrm{qry}}+1)$ bits (\texttt{thm04}).
  \item \textbf{Boundary 5 --- Untrusted Compiler, EDA Synthesizer, Common-Mode Rail Faults, \& Netlist Air-Gap:} Control-plane bundles carry detached post-quantum \textbf{NIST FIPS~204 \texttt{ML-DSA-87}}~\cite{fips204mldsa} signatures and \texttt{spectralc} certificates checked fail-closed in $O(|V| \allowbreak+ |E| \allowbreak+ |\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{assigned}}|^2)$ time (\texttt{thm18}). Post-route \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}}/\texttt{LUT2} SRAM frames and outer Mode-D De~Morgan negative-logic \texttt{RAIN} dual-sieves (\texttt{64'hABABABAB\_FEFEFEFE}~\cite{mitra2000commonmode}) are bi-simulated in Lean~4 (\texttt{thm05},\allowbreak\ \texttt{thm22},\allowbreak\ \texttt{thm27},\allowbreak\ \texttt{thm36},\allowbreak\ \texttt{thm37}), while the Zone-1 post-route~\texttt{.edf} netlist is proved equivalent to the Lean~4 specification across all $t \in \mathbb{N}$ via inductive \texttt{ZK-IC3}.
\end{enumerate}

\noindent\textbf{Unconditional/Quantum-Immune vs.\ Computational \& Physical Preconditions.}\;Boolean bitmask monotonicity (\texttt{thm01}--\texttt{thm03}), Vieta root permanence and shadow B\'{e}zout exactness (\texttt{thm20}, \texttt{thm35}), $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ disjunct non-aliasing (\texttt{thm17}), HDM gauge/orbit clamping (\texttt{thm21}, \texttt{thm34}), Stage-4 credit non-interference (\texttt{thm08}), Goguen--Meseguer unwinding (\texttt{thm19}), single-read deterministic mantissa zeroing ($I \le u(c_i,\mathrm{viol}_i)$, \texttt{thm27}), and intra-page Carter--Wegman MAC injectivity ($P_{\mathrm{coll}} \equiv 0$) hold \textbf{unconditionally and information-theoretically}. Mode~B.2 payload confidentiality relies on link-layer CXL IDE 96-bit AES-256-GCM; hop attenuation (\texttt{thm29}, \texttt{thm33}) and keyed dither $\eta^{(q)}$ rely on the 8-round $\mathcal{X}_{31,33}^{(8)}$ PRF and 128-bit $\mathrm{GF}(2^{64})$ MAC ($<2^{-63}$ inter-page forgery); bundle authentication relies on FIPS~204 \texttt{ML-DSA-87}~\cite{fips204mldsa}; and physical DPA/laser immunity depends on post-route routing capacitance balance ($C_{\mathrm{true}} \approx C_{\mathrm{comp}}$), FPGA \texttt{dont\_touch} slice separation, and \texttt{HD.ISOLATED} SLR moats.

\noindent\textbf{So What? (The Five-Boundary Security Payoff: Hardware Containment Under Total Software Compromise).}\;By anchoring tenant prefix binding (\texttt{CSR 0x70/0x78}), cold-POR \texttt{AND2} policy ratchets, spanning-forest chord clamps, rate-distortion mantissa ceilings, and De~Morgan \texttt{RAIN} dual-sieves directly in combinational logic and cold-reset-isolated flip-flops, Table~\ref{tab:zta_crosswalk} guarantees that even simultaneous compromise of the host OS, hypervisor, software Fabric Manager, intermediate switches, and co-tenant GPUs cannot widen a single permission bit ($0 \rightarrow 1$), hang a coherency snoop, or corrupt an innocent neighbor slot.

\noindent\textbf{Scope \& Price Paid (No Free Lunch --- Layer-1/2 Access Control vs.\ Bulk Payload Encryption \& Cold-POR Root Trust).}\;\textsc{Kernel Zero} is an inline Layer-1/2 spatial and temporal capability sanitizer, not a replacement for link-layer bulk payload encryption (CXL IDE 96-bit AES-256-GCM) or on-package CPU memory encryption. Furthermore, because the host OS and software Fabric Manager are treated as untrusted after boot, the unconditional monotonicity of the \texttt{BAR0} ratchets (\texttt{CSR 0x00..0x70}) and active Vieta bank (\texttt{CSR 0x60}) trades runtime software flexibility for a strict hardware trust anchor: initial epoch seeds and port prefixes must be provisioned during a trusted cold power-on reset ($\texttt{por\_cold\_rst\_n} = 0$) or dedicated hardware Root-of-Trust pulse ($\texttt{rot\_epoch\_rekey\_rst\_n} = 0$).

\section{Multiscale Address Splitting, \texorpdfstring{$J(64,w)$}{J(64,w)} and \texorpdfstring{$\mathbb{F}_8$}{F8} Steiner \texorpdfstring{$S(2,8,64)$}{S(2,8,64)} Coding, Vieta Ratcheting, and Shadow-Bank B\'{e}zout--Horner Deflation}
\label{sec:multiscale}

\subsection{Notation, Monotonic Bitmask Sieve, and Full 64-Bit \texorpdfstring{$J(64,w)$}{J(64,w)} Cohort Coding}
\label{sec:sieve_prelim}
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\;Conventional firewalls check permissions using arithmetic range comparators ($A_{\min} \le \mathrm{HPA} \le A_{\max}$), which require multi-level carry chains and cannot emit both the surviving capability and the unauthorized residual in a single lookup-table delay. Evaluating bitwise conjunction and complementation over $(\mathbb{F}_2^{64}, \oplus, \wedge)$ splits any capability vector $s$ in one \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} delay into its pass bits on pin~\texttt{O5} and its violation bits on pin~\texttt{O6}.\par\smallskip

\noindent\textbf{Notation \& Bitwise Preliminaries.}\;Equip $(\mathbb{F}_2^{64}, \le, \wedge, \vee)$ with the bitwise partial order $a \le b \iff a \wedge \neg b = \mathbf{0}_{64}$. For any 64-bit policy mask $P \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$ and capability vector $s \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$, define the \textbf{monotonic bitmask sieve} via the pass mask $\Pi_P(s) := s \wedge P$ (evaluated on Stage-1 \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} pin~\texttt{O5}) and violation mask $\Delta_P(s) := s \wedge \neg P$ (evaluated on pin~\texttt{O6}). Following multilevel subspace splitting ($\mathcal{X}_{n+1} = \mathcal{X}_n \oplus \mathcal{W}_n$~\cite{chen2001multilevel}), \textbf{hierarchical address partitioning} splits the 40-bit page number $\mathrm{HPA}[51:12] \in \mathbb{F}_2^{40}$ into a 23-bit coarse 512-MB arena slice $\mathcal{X}_n = \{\mathrm{HPA}[51:29]\}$ governed by $P_{\mathrm{coarse}} = p_{s,\mathrm{coarse}} \wedge p_{t,\mathrm{coarse}}$ (\texttt{CSR 0x00/0x08}) and a 17-bit fine 4-KB page slice $\mathcal{W}_n = \{\mathrm{HPA}[28:12]\}$ governed by dual-banked $P_{\mathrm{fine}}^{[b]} = p_{s,\mathrm{fine}}^{[b]} \wedge p_{t,\mathrm{fine}}$ (\texttt{CSR 0x60\_bank[0:1]/0x68}).

\begin{proposition}[Monotonic Bitmask Sieve and Coarse/Fine Policy Refinement]
\label{prop:monotonic}
For any masks $P_{\mathrm{lo}} \le P_{\mathrm{hi}} \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$ ($P_{\mathrm{lo}} \wedge P_{\mathrm{hi}} = P_{\mathrm{lo}}$) and capability vector $s \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:monotonic}
\begin{aligned}
\bigl(s \wedge (P_{\mathrm{lo}} \wedge P_{\mathrm{hi}})\bigr) \wedge \neg P_{\mathrm{lo}} &= \mathbf{0}_{64},\\
s &= \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{lo}}}(s) \oplus \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{lo}}}(s).
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
If $P_{\mathrm{eff}} = p_s \wedge p_t \le q$, then: (i)~$\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(s) \wedge \neg q = \mathbf{0}_{64}$; (ii)~in coarse mode, $\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(s) = \mathbf{0}_{64} \implies s \le q$; and (iii)~under the fine-cohort survival rule $\gamma_{\mathrm{fine}}(P) := \{c \in \mathcal{C} : \Pi_P(c) \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}\}$, $P_{\mathrm{lo}} \le P_{\mathrm{hi}} \implies \gamma_{\mathrm{fine}}(P_{\mathrm{lo}}) \subseteq \gamma_{\mathrm{fine}}(P_{\mathrm{hi}}) \subseteq \gamma_{\mathrm{fine}}(q)$.
\end{proposition}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Proposition~\ref{prop:monotonic}]
In each bit $j \in \{0,\ldots,63\}$, $x \wedge \neg x = 0$ and $(s_j \wedge P_j) \oplus (s_j \wedge \neg P_j) = s_j \wedge (P_j \oplus \neg P_j) = s_j$, while bitwise transitivity gives $c \wedge P_{\mathrm{lo}} \le c \wedge P_{\mathrm{hi}} \le c \wedge q$ (Lean~4 \texttt{thm00}--\texttt{thm03}).
\end{proof}

The 2-bit submode field $\texttt{CSR~0x78}[51:50]$ selects among four Cycle-0/Stage-1 fine-cohort operating modes: $\texttt{2'b00}$ selects Boolean $J(64,w)$ / $\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}$ cohort mode; $\texttt{2'b01}$ selects Vieta Submode~V.1 (Autonomous 64-Bucket Timing Wheel + Vieta Quarantine); $\texttt{2'b10}$ selects Coarse-Only Vieta Annihilator mode ($p_{s,\mathrm{fine}}^{[b_i]} = p_{t,\mathrm{fine}} = \mathbf{1}_{64}, r_i = \mathbf{1}_{64}$); and $\texttt{2'b11}$ selects Vieta Submode~V.2 (Steiner/MDS $\otimes$ Vieta Tensor-Product Sieve). In Boolean mode ($\texttt{2'b00}$), each 4-KB cohort carries a constant-weight codeword $c \in J(64,w)$~\cite{macwilliams1977theorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheory} checked via $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{fine}}^{[b_i]}}(c) == \mathbf{0}_{64}$ on pin~\texttt{O5} gated by $\texttt{coarse\_ok}_i$, with adaptive weight $w^*(R) = \mathrm{round}((64 \ln 2)/R)$.

\begin{theorem}[Hierarchical Coarse-Fine Splitting and Full 64-Bit Johnson-Scheme $J(64,w)$ Disjunct Cohort Isolation]
\label{thm:johnson}
\label{thm:multiscale}
Let $\mathcal{X}_{n+1} = \mathcal{X}_n \oplus \mathcal{W}_n$ be governed by orthogonal 64-bit masks $P_{\mathrm{coarse}}, P_{\mathrm{fine}} \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$, where fine 4-KB cohorts carry codewords $c \in \mathcal{C} \subseteq J(64,w)$. When $R$ distinct cohorts $c^{(1)},\ldots,c^{(R)} \in \mathcal{C}$ are revoked by setting $P_{\mathrm{fine}} = \neg r_{\mathrm{revoked}}$ with $r_{\mathrm{revoked}} = \bigvee_{m=1}^R c^{(m)}$ of union weight $u(R) := \mathrm{wt}(r_{\mathrm{revoked}}) \le \min(64, Rw)$:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Orthogonal Dual-Rail \texttt{LUT6\_2} Evaluation:} Revoking fine cohorts in $P_{\mathrm{fine}}$ causes zero collateral coarse revocations in $P_{\mathrm{coarse}}$, and both $\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{coarse}}}(r_i) \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}$ (\texttt{O6}) and $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{fine}}}(c) == \mathbf{0}_{64}$ (\texttt{O5}) evaluate in 1~\mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} level with $0\text{~BRAM}$;
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Kautz--Singleton Disjunct Zero-Aliasing Regime ($R \le \lfloor (w-1)/\lambda \rfloor$)~\cite{kautz1964nonrandom}:} For any packing code $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{pack}} \subset J(64,w)$ with maximum pairwise overlap $\lambda := \max_{c \neq c'} |\mathrm{supp}(c) \cap \mathrm{supp}(c')|$, $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{pack}}$ is $R$-disjunct for all $R \le R_{\mathrm{exact}} := \lfloor (w-1)/\lambda \rfloor$, guaranteeing $P_{\mathrm{alias}}(R) \equiv 0$. At $v=64, w=8, \lambda=1$, $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 7$ and $|\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{pack}}| \le A(64,14,8) = \lfloor \frac{64}{8}\lfloor \frac{63}{7}\rfloor \rfloor = 72$, achieved with equality by $\mathrm{AG}(2,8) \cong S(2,8,64)$; at $\lambda = 2$, $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 3$ across 520 MDS codewords; and
  \item[(iii)] \textbf{Exact Without-Replacement Hypergeometric Tail Bound over $J(64,w)$ ($R > R_{\mathrm{exact}}$):} Over full $\mathcal{C} = J(64,w)$ ($\binom{64}{8} = 4,426,165,368 > 2^{32}$; $\binom{64}{4} = 635,376$), conditioned on $u(R)$:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:hypergeom}
  \begin{aligned}
  P_{\mathrm{alias}}^J(R \mid u) &= \frac{\binom{u(R)}{w} - R}{\binom{64}{w} - R}
  < \prod_{j=0}^{w-1} \frac{u(R)-j}{64-j} \le \left(\frac{u(R)}{64}\right)^{\!w},\\
  \mathbb{E}\bigl[P_{\mathrm{alias}}^J(R)\bigr] &\le \bigl(p_{\mathrm{bit}}(R)\bigr)^w
  = \Bigl(1 - \bigl(1 - w/64\bigr)^R\Bigr)^{\!w},
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  which for $v = 64, w = 8$ gives $P_{\mathrm{alias}}^J(1) \equiv 0$, single-draw collision $\binom{64}{8}^{-1} \approx 2.259 \times 10^{-10}$, $R = 2$ ($u \le 16$) worst-case $12,868 / 4,426,165,366 \approx 2.91 \times 10^{-6}$, and $R = 5$ expected rate $\le (1 - (7/8)^5)^8 < 0.318\%$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:johnson}]
Clause~(i) follows from Proposition~\ref{prop:monotonic}. For Clause~(ii), each $c^{(m)}$ shares at most $\lambda$ ones with any $c' \in \mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{pack}} \setminus \{c^{(1)},\ldots,c^{(R)}\}$, so $R \le \lfloor (w-1)/\lambda \rfloor$ revocations cover at most $R\lambda \le w - 1 < w$ ones of $c'$, leaving $\ge 1$ surviving bit in $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{fine}}}(c') \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}$. For Clause~(iii), $\binom{u(R)}{w} - R$ of the $\binom{64}{w} - R$ unrevoked codewords in $J(64,w)$ lie inside $\mathrm{supp}(r_{\mathrm{revoked}})$, and negative association of bit coverage yields Eq.~\eqref{eq:hypergeom}.
\end{proof}

\subsection{Pre-Core Zero-Sideband Carry-Free \texorpdfstring{$\mathbb{F}_8$}{F8} Steiner \texorpdfstring{$S(2,8,64)$}{S(2,8,64)} and \texorpdfstring{$[8,3,6]_8$}{[8,3,6]8} MDS Expander (Mode C)}
\label{sec:modec}
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\;Unmodified commercial CXL hosts issue standard \texttt{CXL.mem} requests carrying only a 52-bit $\mathrm{HPA}$ without a 256-bit capability sideband. Cycle~0 therefore decrypts a compact 8-bit or 10-bit descriptor $\mathrm{cid}_i$ in the upper $\mathrm{HPA}$ slice, expands it into a 64-bit codeword by evaluating polynomial lines over $\mathbb{F}_8 = \mathrm{GF}(2^3) \cong \mathbb{F}_2[z]/(z^3 + z + 1)$ (where $x_r^2 = (0,1,4,5,6,7,2,3)$ and $d^{-1} = d^6$), and zeroes the upper $\mathrm{HPA}$ bits before Stage~1.\par\smallskip

\begin{theorem}[Pre-Core Carry-Free $\mathbb{F}_8$ Steiner $S(2,8,64)$ / MDS Expander and Tweakable Multi-Width Feistel HPA Sealing]
\label{thm:modec}
Let an incoming Mode-C request slot carry $\mathrm{SPID}_i[13:0]$ and a $c_{\mathrm{bits}}$-bit descriptor $\mathrm{cid}_i$ ($c_{\mathrm{bits}} \in \{8,10\}$) in the upper bits of an even-width slice $\mathrm{HPA}_{\mathrm{wire},i}[12 + 2n - 1 : 12]$ across $2n \in \{24, 34, 36, 40\}$ ($n \in \{12, 17, 18, 20\}$). In the 10-bit profile ($\mathrm{cid}_i \in \{0,\ldots,1023\}$), Cycle~0 partitions $\mathrm{cid}_i$ into four classes:
(1)~for $\mathrm{cid}_i \in \{0,\ldots,511\}$ with $c = \mathrm{cid}_i[8:6], b = \mathrm{cid}_i[5:3], a = \mathrm{cid}_i[2:0] \in \mathbb{F}_8$, it asserts $\mathrm{fine}_i = 1$ and evaluates across byte lanes $r \in \{0,\ldots,7\}$:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:f8_expander}
\begin{aligned}
y_r(a,b,c) &:= a \oplus (b \odot x_r) \oplus (c \odot x_r^2) \in \mathbb{F}_8,\\
\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}(a,b,c) &:= \bigl[(\texttt{8'd1} \ll y_7) \parallel \cdots \parallel (\texttt{8'd1} \ll y_0)\bigr] \in J(64,8);
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
(2)~for vertical byte lines $\mathrm{cid}_i \in \{512,\ldots,519\}$, it asserts $\mathrm{fine}_i = 1$ and drives $\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}(\mathrm{cid}_i) = \texttt{64'hFF} \ll (8 \cdot \mathrm{cid}_i[2:0]) \in J(64,8)$;
(3)~for 64 1-hot coarse arenas $\mathrm{cid}_i \in \{576,\ldots,639\}$, it asserts $\mathrm{fine}_i = 0$ and drives $r_i = \texttt{64'd1} \ll \mathrm{cid}_i[5:0]$; and
(4)~for the remaining 440 invalid codepoints, it drives $r_i = \mathbf{0}_{64}$ ($584/1024 = 73/128$ valid ratio; $256/256$ in the 8-bit profile). Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Steiner $S(2,8,64) \cong \mathrm{AG}(2,8)$, Parallel-Slope Resolvability, \& $[8,3,6]_8$ MDS Bounds:} The $64 + 8 = 72$ linear/vertical codewords construct all $\binom{64}{2}/\binom{8}{2} = 72$ lines of $\mathrm{AG}(2,8) \cong S(2,8,64)$, attaining $A(64,14,8) = 72$ ($\lambda = 1 \implies R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 7$). Within any parallel slope $b_0 \in \mathbb{F}_8 \cup \{\infty\}$, the 8 lines are pairwise bit-disjoint ($\lambda = 0$ across 252 intra-slope pairs), whereas inter-slope lines meet in $\lambda = 1$ bit across 2,304 pairs. Across all 520 extended $[8,3,6]_8$ MDS cohorts, $\lambda \le 2 \implies R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 3$ (Lean~4 \texttt{thm17}, \texttt{thm38});
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Coprime Feistel Diffusion \& Speculative-Read-Safe Write Lockout:} For all $n \in \{12, 17, 18, 20\}$, $\gcd(7,n) = 1$, achieving full half-word diffusion in 6 two-round stages with $\mathrm{SPID}_i$ bound into $T_i$. Unauthenticated Mode-C.2 probes fail $(\mathrm{guard}_g == \mathbf{0}_g) \wedge \mathrm{cid\_valid}_i$ with probability $1 - \frac{73}{128} 2^{-g}$; on an \texttt{M2S\_RwD} write guard failure, hardware clears $\texttt{CSR\_0x70}[\mathrm{SPID}_i[5:0]] \leftarrow \texttt{1'b0}$ and increments \texttt{CSR 0x48}, while speculative \texttt{M2S\_Req} reads never lock out an innocent VM; and
  \item[(iii)] \textbf{Canonical HPA Slice Restoration:} Prior to Stage~1, Cycle~0 strips $\{\mathrm{cid}_i, \mathrm{guard}_g\} \leftarrow \mathbf{0}$ across the upper $(c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g)$ slice bits via $\mathrm{HPA}_i[12+2n-1 : 12+2n-(c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g)] \leftarrow \mathbf{0}$ (for example, $\mathrm{HPA}_i[45:32] \leftarrow \mathbf{0}$ when $2n=34$ and $c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g=14$) and latches $\texttt{coarse\_ok}_i = (P_{\mathrm{coarse}} \gg \mathrm{arena\_idx}_i) \ \&\ \texttt{1'b1}$, preventing downstream AXI \texttt{DECERR} faults.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:modec}]
Each fine codeword sets one bit per byte lane (or one vertical byte), so $\mathrm{wt}(\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}) \equiv 8$. Over $\mathbb{F}_8$, linear codewords with $b_1 = b_2$ and $a_1 \neq a_2$ satisfy $y_r(a_1,b_1,0) \oplus y_r(a_2,b_1,0) = a_1 \oplus a_2 \neq 0$ ($\lambda = 0$ intra-slope), whereas $d := b_1 \oplus b_2 \in \mathbb{F}_8^\times$ has unique intersection root $x_r = d^6 \odot (a_1 \oplus a_2)$ ($\lambda = 1$ inter-slope). Non-zero quadratic differences in $\mathbb{F}_8[x]$ have degree $\le 2$ and at most $\lambda = 2$ roots ($R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 3$).
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Distance-Doubling $\mathbb{F}_8^8 \hookrightarrow J(64,8)$ Isometry \& Tight Welch--Sidelnikov Frame Optimality]
\label{cor:frame}
Under the 1-hot byte-lane embedding $\psi_{\text{1-hot}} : \mathbb{F}_8^8 \hookrightarrow J(64,8)$ and the $64 \times 72$ point-line incidence matrix $N$ of $\mathrm{AG}(2,8) \cong S(2,8,64)$:
(i)~for all $\mathbf{y}, \mathbf{y}' \in \mathbb{F}_8^8$ with agreement count $\lambda(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{y}')$,
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:isometry_f8}
\begin{aligned}
d_H^{\mathbb{F}_2}\bigl(\psi_{\text{1-hot}}(\mathbf{y}), \psi_{\text{1-hot}}(\mathbf{y}')\bigr)
&= 2\,d_H^{\mathbb{F}_8}(\mathbf{y}, \mathbf{y}')\\
&= 2\bigl(8 - \lambda(\mathbf{y}, \mathbf{y}')\bigr),
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
and a $t$-of-$8$ survival threshold guarantees $P_{\mathrm{alias}} \equiv 0$ up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}}(t,\lambda) = \lfloor (8 - t)/\lambda \rfloor$; and
(ii)~because every point of $\mathbb{F}_8^2$ lies on $r_{\mathrm{deg}} = 9$ lines and every point pair lies on $\lambda_{\mathrm{design}} = 1$ line, $N N^\top = 8 I_{64} + J_{64}$ with eigenvalues $\mu_0 = 72$ (mult.\ $1$) and $\mu_1 = 8$ (mult.\ $63$), achieving the Welch--Sidelnikov bound~\cite{welch1974lower}.
\end{corollary}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Corollary~\ref{cor:frame}]
Each lane $r \in \{0,\ldots,7\}$ contributes binary Hamming distance $0$ if $y_r = y'_r$ and $2$ if $y_r \neq y'_r$, giving Eq.~\eqref{eq:isometry_f8}. Counting point/pair incidences in $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ gives $N N^\top = 8 I_{64} + J_{64}$.
\end{proof}

\subsection{Cycle-0 Vieta Polynomial-Ideal Sieve and Shadow-Bank B\'{e}zout--Horner Deflation}
\label{sec:vieta}
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\;Any Boolean cover-free family over $v$ bits obeys the Erd\H{o}s--Frankl--F\"{u}redi barrier $v = \Omega((R^2 / \log R)\log N)$~\cite{erdos1985families}, and revoking all 8 lines of one parallel slope in $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ sets $P_{\mathrm{fine}} = \mathbf{0}_{64}$. Reinterpreting the 64-bit register \texttt{CSR 0x60} as the 8 elementary symmetric Vieta coefficients $(\sigma_1,\ldots,\sigma_8) \in (\mathbb{F}_{2^8})^8$ of a degree-8 annihilator polynomial $\Lambda^{(t)}(Z)$ over $\mathbb{F}_{2^8} \cong \mathbb{F}_2[z]/(z^8 + z^4 + z^3 + z + 1)$ breaks this quadratic barrier: a degree-$d_{\mathrm{rev}}$ polynomial over a field has at most $d_{\mathrm{rev}} \le 8$ roots ($P_{\mathrm{alias}} \equiv 0$ up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 8$), while pairing it with the 64-bucket timing wheel in \texttt{CSR 0x68} expires routine leases at $\Delta d_{\mathrm{rev}} = 0$.\par\smallskip

\begin{theorem}[Dual-Submode Cycle-0 Vieta Polynomial-Ideal \& Time-Wheel / Steiner Tensor Sieve over {$\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$}]
\label{thm:vieta}
Let each fine 4-KB cohort carry non-zero descriptor $\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i} \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$ ($N = 255$). In Vieta modes ($\texttt{CSR 0x78}[50] = 1$), each bank of \texttt{CSR 0x60\_bank[0:1]} stores $\boldsymbol{\sigma}^{(t)} = (\sigma_1^{(t)}, \ldots, \sigma_8^{(t)}) \in (\mathbb{F}_{2^8})^8$ ($\boldsymbol{\sigma}^{(0)} = \mathbf{0}_{64}, d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(0)} = 0$) representing the monic degree-8 annihilator polynomial:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:vieta_poly}
\begin{aligned}
\Lambda^{(t)}(Z) &:= Z^8 \oplus \bigoplus_{k=1}^8 \sigma_k^{(t)} \odot Z^{8-k} = Z^{8 - d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)}} \odot P^{(t)}(Z),\\
P^{(t)}(Z) &:= \prod_{j=1}^{d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)}} \bigl(Z \oplus \alpha_j\bigr).
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
When cohort $\alpha \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$ is written to active \texttt{CSR 0x60}, hardware evaluates $\Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha)$ and updates $(\boldsymbol{\sigma}^{(t+1)}, d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t+1)})$ with $\sigma_0^{(t)} \equiv 1_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$ via:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:vieta_ratchet}
\sigma_k^{(t+1)} :=
\begin{dcases}
\sigma_k^{(t)} \oplus \bigl(\alpha \odot \sigma_{k-1}^{(t)}\bigr), & \alpha \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}} \wedge \Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha) \neq 0 \wedge d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} < 8,\\
\sigma_k^{(t)}, & \text{otherwise (dup., }\alpha = 0\text{, or }d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} = 8\text{)},
\end{dcases}
\end{equation}
locking fail-closed at \texttt{CSR 0x20}[12] if a 9th distinct root arrives before bank rotation. In Cycle~0, for each slot $i \in \{0,1,2,3\}$ with bank $b_i = \texttt{slot\_bank\_sel\_i}[i]$, hardware conjoins the non-zero descriptor guard $(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i} \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}})$ (closing the $d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)}=8$ boundary where $\Lambda^{(t)}(0) = \sigma_8^{(t)} \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$) to register
\[
\begin{aligned}
\texttt{slot\_coarse\_arena\_ok\_i}[i] \Leftarrow\;& \bigl(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i} \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}\bigr)
\wedge \bigl(\Lambda_{b_i}^{(t)}(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i}) \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}\bigr)\\
&\wedge \texttt{arena\_ok}_i \wedge \texttt{bank\_valid\_q}[b_i],
\end{aligned}
\]
and selects either \textbf{Submode~V.1} ($\texttt{2'b01}$, driving 1-hot timing-wheel vector $r_i = \texttt{64'd1} \ll \tau_i$ with $p_{s,\mathrm{fine}}^{[b_i]} = \mathbf{1}_{64}$) or \textbf{Submode~V.2} ($\texttt{2'b11}$, driving $r_i = \Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{hi},i}) \in J(64,8)$). Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Linear Bit Scaling $v = R \cdot m$ \& Exact $P_{\mathrm{alias}}(r) \equiv 0$ up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 8$:} Over $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$, $\Lambda^{(t)}(Z)$ has at most $d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} \le 8$ roots in $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:vieta_roots}
  \begin{aligned}
  \mathrm{Roots}\bigl(\Lambda^{(t)}\bigr)
  &:= \bigl\{z \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times : \Lambda^{(t)}(z) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}\bigr\}\\
  &\phantom{:}= \bigl\{\alpha_1, \ldots, \alpha_{d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)}}\bigr\},
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  yielding $P_{\mathrm{alias}}(r) \equiv 0$ for any $1 \le r \le 8$ revocations (including same-slope bursts) across all 255 cohorts with $64\text{~bits}$ and $0$ changes to the \CorePhysicalLUTs-LUT Stage-1/2 core;
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Unconditional Active-Bank Polynomial-Ideal Root Permanence:} Because $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}$ is an integral domain, $P^{(t+1)}(Z) = (Z \oplus \alpha) \odot P^{(t)}(Z)$ generates a descending ideal chain $\langle P^{(0)} \rangle \supseteq \cdots \supseteq \langle P^{(t)} \rangle$. In the active shift-multiply branch ($\Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha) \neq 0 \wedge d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} < 8$, where $\sigma_8^{(t)} = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$), update~\eqref{eq:vieta_ratchet} satisfies for all $Z \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}$ and every non-zero $z^* \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:vieta_monotone}
  \begin{aligned}
  Z \odot \Lambda^{(t+1)}(Z) &= \bigl(Z \oplus \alpha\bigr) \odot \Lambda^{(t)}(Z),\\
  z^* \odot \Lambda^{(t+1)}(z^*) &= \bigl(z^* \oplus \alpha\bigr) \odot \Lambda^{(t)}(z^*),\\
  \Lambda^{(t)}(z^*) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}} &\implies \Lambda^{(t+1)}(z^*) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}},
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  preventing any compromised FM write from un-revoking an active root $z^*$ (Lean~4 \texttt{thm20}); and
  \item[(iii)] \textbf{64-Bucket Timing-Wheel Selectivity ($\Delta d_{\mathrm{rev}} = 0$) \& Tensor Namespace Multiplication:} Stage~1's \texttt{O5} projection satisfies:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:timewheel_tensor}
  \begin{aligned}
  \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{fine}}^{[b_i]}}\bigl(\texttt{64'd1} \ll \tau_i\bigr) = \mathbf{0}_{64} &\iff p_{t,\mathrm{fine}}[\tau_i] = 0,\\
  \mathrm{Pass}_{\mathrm{V.2}}(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{hi}}, \mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo}}) &\iff \bigl(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo}} \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}\bigr)\\
  &\quad\wedge \bigl(\Lambda_{b_i}^{(t)}(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo}}) \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}\bigr)\\
  &\quad\wedge \bigl(\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{fine}}^{[b_i]}}(\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{hi}})) \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}\bigr),
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  expiring scheduled leases at $\Delta d_{\mathrm{rev}} = 0$ in Submode~V.1 and expanding Submode~V.2 to $72 \times 255 = 18,360$ Steiner--Vieta cohorts or $520 \times 255 = 132,600$ MDS--Vieta cohorts in $0\text{~BRAM}$ (Lean~4 \texttt{thm26}).
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:vieta}]
Unique factorization in $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ gives Clause~(i). For $d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} < 8$, $\sigma_8^{(t)} = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$, so expanding $Z \odot \Lambda^{(t+1)}(Z) = (Z \oplus \alpha) \odot \Lambda^{(t)}(Z)$ yields Eq.~\eqref{eq:vieta_ratchet}; multiplying $z^* \odot \Lambda^{(t+1)}(z^*) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$ by $(z^*)^{-1} \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$ proves Eq.~\eqref{eq:vieta_monotone} (\texttt{thm20}). Bitwise evaluation of $(\texttt{64'd1} \ll \tau_i) \wedge p_{t,\mathrm{fine}}$ and tensor gating prove Eq.~\eqref{eq:timewheel_tensor} (\texttt{thm26}).
\end{proof}

\begin{figure*}[!tp]
\centering
\IncludeSpectralPlate{fig4_vieta_scaling.pdf}
\caption{Dual-Submode Cycle-0 Vieta $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ polynomial-ideal and Steiner/MDS tensor-sieve cohort scaling: $P_{\mathrm{alias}}(r) \equiv 0$ up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}}=8$ revocations (including same-slope bursts, whereas unscheduled same-slope revocation at $R=8$ incurs a $100\%$ wipeout $P_{\mathrm{fine}}=\mathbf{0}_{64}$, off-scale on the $0$--$12\%$ vertical axis) across $18,360$ Steiner--Vieta and $132,600$ MDS--Vieta cohorts in $0\text{~BRAM}$.\vspace{3pt}\\
\textbf{So What? (The Horizon Collapse --- Rotating Memory Isolation from $\Theta(M)$ Space into $548\text{~ns}$ of Time).}\;Any purely Boolean bitmap or Bloom filter over 64 bits hits either the Erd\H{o}s--Frankl--F\"{u}redi quadratic cover-free ceiling or a $100\%$ wipeout when 8 cohorts in one affine slope are revoked together. Figure~\ref{fig:vieta_scaling} shows what happens when the same 64 flip-flops are rotated into the elementary symmetric coordinates of a degree-8 polynomial $\Lambda^{(t)}(Z) \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ paired with the Stage-1 Steiner/time-wheel sieve: false-positive revocations vanish identically ($P_{\mathrm{alias}} \equiv 0$) up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 8$ arbitrary revocations across $18,360$--$132,600$ cohorts in $0\text{~bits}$ of SRAM, while routine TTL leases expire on \texttt{CSR 0x68} with $\Delta d_{\mathrm{rev}} = 0$ root consumption.}
\label{fig:vieta_scaling}
\end{figure*}

\noindent\textbf{Motivation (Selective Root Reclamation).}\;Monotonic root insertion in Eq.~\eqref{eq:vieta_ratchet} cannot evict an individual cohort $\alpha_j$ after its $\Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}}$ shootdown completes if newer cohorts $\alpha_m \neq \alpha_j$ are still draining~\cite{filardo2024cornucopiareloaded}. Theorem~\ref{thm:bezout_galois} proves that when duplicate insertions are filtered so the active root set remains square-free, a single 8-step Horner pass on the inactive shadow bank simultaneously computes the exact quotient polynomial, verifies root membership via $q_8 = \Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha_j) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$, and restores $Z^{8-(d-1)}$ low-order zero-padding automatically while preserving active-bank monotonicity (\texttt{SVA~1\textquotesingle}).

\begin{theorem}[Unified 8-Step Inactive Shadow-Bank B\'{e}zout--Horner Deflation \& 0-LUT Normal-Basis Frobenius Equivariance]
\label{thm:bezout_galois}
\label{thm:vieta_deflation_galois}
Let $\Lambda^{(t)}(Z) \allowbreak= Z^8 \allowbreak\oplus \bigoplus_{k=1}^8 \sigma_k^{(t)} \odot Z^{8-k} \allowbreak= Z^{8 - d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)}} \odot \prod_{m=1}^{d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)}}(Z \oplus \alpha_m) \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ ($1 \le d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} \le 8$) be maintained as a square-free non-zero root set via duplicate-filtering insertion idempotence ($\Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}} \allowbreak\implies \boldsymbol{\sigma}^{(t+1)} := \boldsymbol{\sigma}^{(t)}$, ensuring $(Z \oplus \alpha)^2 \not\mid P^{(t)}(Z)$). Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{8-Step Shadow-Bank B\'{e}zout--Horner Division \& Two-Sided Commutation:} Once cohort $\alpha_j \in \mathrm{Roots}^\times(\Lambda^{(t)})$ completes its $\Delta T_{\mathrm{shootdown}}$ window, evaluating on the inactive shadow bank ($\texttt{bank\_valid\_q}[b_{\mathrm{shad}}] \allowbreak= 0$) with $q_0 := 1_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$ the 8-step recurrence
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:bezout_horner}
  q_k := \sigma_k^{(t)} \oplus \bigl(\alpha_j \odot q_{k-1}\bigr), \quad k \in \{1,\ldots,8\},
  \end{equation}
  yields the exact polynomial division identity $\Lambda^{(t)}(Z) = \bigl((Z \oplus \alpha_j) \odot Q^{\mathrm{defl}}(Z)\bigr) \oplus q_8$ where $Q^{\mathrm{defl}}(Z) = Z^7 \oplus \bigoplus_{k=1}^7 q_k \odot Z^{7-k}$ and $q_8 = \Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha_j)$ (Lean~4 \texttt{thm35}). Whenever $\alpha_j \in \mathrm{Roots}^\times(\Lambda^{(t)})$, $q_8 = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$ holds identically---rejecting non-roots fail-closed if $q_8 \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$ and zero-padding $\sigma_8^{\mathrm{defl}} := q_8 = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$ so $\boldsymbol{\sigma}^{\mathrm{defl}} := (q_1,\ldots,q_8)$ represents $\Lambda^{\mathrm{defl}}(Z) = Z \odot Q^{\mathrm{defl}}(Z)$ ($d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{\mathrm{defl}} = d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} - 1$) with:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:bezout_exact}
  \begin{aligned}
  \bigl(Z \oplus \alpha_j\bigr) \odot \Lambda^{\mathrm{defl}}(Z) &= Z \odot \Lambda^{(t)}(Z),\\
  \mathrm{Roots}^\times\bigl(\Lambda^{\mathrm{defl}}\bigr) &= \mathrm{Roots}^\times\bigl(\Lambda^{(t)}\bigr) \setminus \{\alpha_j\}.
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  Insertion and deflation commute out of order ($\mathrm{Deflate}_{\alpha_1}(\mathrm{Insert}_{\alpha_2}(\mathrm{Insert}_{\alpha_1}(P))) = \mathrm{Insert}_{\alpha_2}(P)$) and deflate 4 transient roots down to the degree-4 $[8,4,5]_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$ Reed--Solomon MDS generator ($d = 8 - 4 + 1 = 5$; Lean~4 \texttt{thm42}); and
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{0-LUT Normal-Basis Frobenius Equivariance ($\beta_{\min} = \texttt{8'h20}$)~\cite{mullin1989optimal, lidl1997finite, diamond2025binius}:} Over $\mathbb{F}_2$, $x^8 - 1 = (x + 1)^8$, so $\beta \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}$ generates a normal basis $\{\beta^{2^r}\}_{r=0}^7$ iff $(\phi + \mathrm{Id})^7(\beta) \allowbreak= \mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}/\mathbb{F}_2}(\beta) \allowbreak= 1$~\cite{lidl1997finite}, yielding $\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_2}(x^8 - 1) = 2^8 - 2^7 = 128$ normal elements with minimal element $\beta_{\min} = z^5 = \texttt{8'h20}$ ($\mathrm{Tr}(z^k)=0$ for $0 \le k \le 4$, $\mathrm{Tr}(z^5)=1$). In $\beta_{\min}$ normal-basis coordinates (routing bijectively onto $\mathrm{AG}(2,8) \times \mathrm{ClampTier}$ via \texttt{thm40}), Frobenius $\phi^k(x) = x^{2^k}$ reduces to a 0-LUT wire rotation $\mathrm{RotL}_k(x)$ ($\phi^8 = \mathrm{Id}$) satisfying $\Lambda_{\phi^k(\boldsymbol{\sigma})}(\phi^k(x)) \allowbreak= \phi^k(\Lambda_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}(x))$ and $P_{\mathcal{O}(\beta_{\min})}(Z) \allowbreak= Z^8 \allowbreak\oplus Z^7 \allowbreak\oplus Z^4 \allowbreak\oplus Z^3 \allowbreak\oplus Z^2 \allowbreak\oplus Z \allowbreak\oplus 1 \in \mathbb{F}_2[Z]$ (Lean~4 \texttt{thm35}, \texttt{thm40}).
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:bezout_galois}]
Rather than $256^3$ brute-force case-splitting, Lean~4 proves $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}$ (\texttt{0x11B}) associativity, commutativity, and distributivity constructively via 8-basis $\mathbb{F}_2$-linearity (\id{lem_linear_eq_on_basis}) and the degree-8 Cayley--Hamilton identity (\id{lem_cayley_hamilton} in \texttt{thm33}, \texttt{thm35}, \texttt{thm40}, \texttt{thm42}). Substituting $\sigma_k^{(t)} = q_k \oplus (\alpha_j \odot q_{k-1})$ ($1 \le k \le 8$) into $\Lambda^{(t)}(Z)$ yields $\Lambda^{(t)}(Z) = ((Z \oplus \alpha_j) \odot Q^{\mathrm{defl}}(Z)) \oplus q_8$ universally (\id{lem_bezout_div_identity}). Setting $Z = \alpha_j$ gives $q_8 = \Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha_j) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$, and multiplying by $Z$ establishes Eq.~\eqref{eq:bezout_exact} and out-of-order commutation (\texttt{thm42}). Newton's identities on $z^8+z^4+z^3+z+1$ give $\mathrm{Tr}(z^k)=0$ ($0 \le k \le 4$) and $\mathrm{Tr}(z^5)=1$, establishing $\beta_{\min} = \texttt{8'h20}$ and 0-LUT $\mathrm{RotL}_1$ squaring (\texttt{thm35}, \texttt{thm40}).
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[Algebraic Duality with Neff's Multiset Root Hash in \texttt{zkPi}, \texttt{SVA~1\textquotesingle}-Gated Clifford CAR Fock Space, \& Discrete Noether Currents]
\label{rem:neff_zkpi_duality}
The 8-step B\'{e}zout--Horner quotient recurrence $q_k := \sigma_k^{(t)} \oplus (\alpha_j \odot q_{k-1})$ of Eqs.~\eqref{eq:bezout_horner}--\eqref{eq:bezout_exact} is the characteristic-2 hardware dual of Neff's prime-field multiset root polynomial $H_r(k,L) = \prod_{i=0}^{|L|-1}(k - L[i]) \in \mathbb{F}_p[k]$ employed in \texttt{zkPi}'s \texttt{ContextContains} typing-context lookup and deletion gadget~\cite{laufer2024zkpi, neff2001verifiable}. Whereas naive typing-context removal or hardware revocation-list compaction requires $\Theta(n\log n)$ sorting networks or $\Theta(n)$ CAM shifts, encoding $R^{(t)} = \{\alpha_1,\dots,\alpha_{d_{\mathrm{rev}}}\}$ into the descending Vieta coefficients $(\sigma_1^{(t)},\dots,\sigma_8^{(t)})$ of $\Lambda^{(t)}(Z) = Z^8 \oplus \bigoplus_{k=1}^8 \sigma_k^{(t)} \odot Z^{8-k}$ reduces single-element deletion $R^{(t+1)} = R^{(t)} \setminus \{\alpha_j\}$ to an $O(1)$-depth (8-step Horner) quotient check gated by remainder annihilation $q_8 = \Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha_j) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$. Furthermore, while ungated polynomial multiplication $M_\alpha(P) := (Z\oplus\alpha)\odot P$ on $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ is non-nilpotent ($M_\alpha^2 \neq 0$) and obeys the Toeplitz--Cuntz identity~\cite{cuntz1977simple} $D_\alpha M_\alpha = \mathrm{Id}$ with rank-1 commutator $[D_\alpha,M_\alpha] = \mathbf{1}\cdot\mathrm{ev}_\alpha$, Cycle-0 duplicate rejection ($\Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha)\neq 0$) paired with \texttt{SVA~1\textquotesingle} zero-remainder gating ($q_8 = 0$) restricts \texttt{CSR 0x60} to the square-free radical Fock space $\mathcal{F} = \bigoplus_{d=0}^{255}\mathcal{F}^d = \mathbb{F}_2[\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times)]$, on which the gated hardware operators $(\hat{a}_\alpha^\dagger, \hat{a}_\alpha)$ satisfy the exact 255-mode Canonical Anticommutation Relations (CAR)~\cite{jordan1928pauli} of $\mathrm{Cl}_{255,255}(\mathbb{F}_2)$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:law1_gauss_car}). Under this symmetry lens, decoupling routine 64-bucket timing-wheel TTL expirations (\texttt{CSR 0x68}, \texttt{thm26}) from the Fock number operator $\hat{N}_{\mathrm{rev}}$ (\texttt{CSR 0x60}) yields exact degree conservation $\Delta d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{\mathrm{TTL}} \equiv 0$ (\textbf{Discrete Noether Pair~2}), just as $(\mathbb{F}_8,+)$ affine translation symmetry across the 9 parallel classes of $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ enforces constant codeword Hamming weight $\mathrm{wt}_H(c_\ell)\equiv 8$ and zero first-order toggle variance $\mathrm{Var}(\Delta I_{\mathrm{dyn}})\equiv 0$ (\textbf{Discrete Noether Pair~3}, unified in Section~\ref{sec:compiler}, Remark~\ref{rem:noether_parent_synthesis}).
\end{remark}

\noindent\textbf{Scope \& Price Paid (No Free Lunch --- Structured Algebraic Cohorts vs.\ Unbounded Scatter-Gather).}\;Eliminating $\Theta(M)$ DRAM permission tables trades arbitrary page-by-page scatter-gather mutability for structured algebraic cohorts ($R_{\mathrm{exact}}=7$ in $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ or $R_{\mathrm{exact}}=8$ in $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ per active window) reclaimed on the inactive shadow bank in 8 clock cycles ($1.095 \times 10^7\text{~rev/s}$; note also that pure integer threshold equalities in Lean~4 \texttt{thm39}, \texttt{thm48}, and \texttt{thm49} serve strictly as discrete parameter sanity checks alongside our analytical proofs). Workloads demanding millions of ungrouped single-page emergency revocations within a single sub-microsecond shootdown window without epoch batching still require conventional stateful MMU page tables. Likewise, in zero-sideband Mode~C, embedding $(c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g)\in[10,14]$ capability/guard bits in the upper slice of an even-width $2n$-bit page window trades $c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g$ upper HPA address bits within that window ($2^{2n-(c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g)}$ addressable 4-KB pages per coarse arena tweak) in exchange for $0\%$ DRAM table carve-out and $0\%$ flit-slot payload tax on unmodified \texttt{CXL.mem} hosts.

\section{Spanning-Forest Chord Clamping, \texorpdfstring{$S_{64}$}{S\_64} Orbits, Fractured \texorpdfstring{\texttt{LUT6\_2}}{LUT6\_2} Clamping, Streaming Sketch, and Keyed-Dither / BBP Thresholds}
\label{sec:forest}

\subsection{Spanning-Forest Chord Clamping, Coarse HDM Gauge Flattening, and \texorpdfstring{\texttt{LUT6\_2}}{LUT6\_2} MOESI / Mantissa Clamping}
\label{sec:sheaf_moesi}
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\;In a multi-hop CXL switch fabric containing routing loops ($\beta_1(G) = |E| - |V| + \beta_0 > 0$), a deputized node can forward a request around a cycle to launder permissions unless every cycle is intercepted. Note first from Eq.~\eqref{eq:monotonic} that $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i) = r_i \wedge P_{\mathrm{eff}}$ and $\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i) = r_i \wedge \neg P_{\mathrm{eff}}$ partition every capability bit-by-bit ($r_i = \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i) \oplus \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i)$) without needing vector-space kernel/image machinery. Decomposing $G$ into a spanning forest $T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ plus $\beta_1$ fundamental chords $e^* \in E \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ ensures every non-backtracking cyclic walk crosses at least one chord, collapsing transitive capability propagation to the loop-free tree in zero online consensus rounds.\par\smallskip

\begin{theorem}[Spanning-Forest Decomposition, Anti-Hairpinning, \& Fundamental-Chord Cycle Interception]
\label{thm:forest}
Let the interconnect graph $G = (V, E)$ with incidence matrix $B \in \{-1, 0, +1\}^{|V| \times |E|}$ and $\beta_0$ connected components be decomposed in $O(|E|)$ offline time~\cite{tarjan1983data} via a spanning forest $T_{\mathrm{forest}} \subset E$ into $|V| - \beta_0$ tree branches and $\beta_1 = \dim \ker(B) = |E| - |V| + \beta_0$ fundamental chords $e^* \in E \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ (satisfying constructive Euler cycle-rank invariance $|E| + \beta_0 = |V| + \beta_1$ across vertex, tree-edge, and chord-edge additions, Lean~4 \texttt{thm32}), with port anti-hairpinning ($\texttt{egress\_port} \neq \texttt{ingress\_port}$) at every switch port. Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] every chord-avoiding subgraph $S \subseteq T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ has $\beta_1(S) = 0$ and admits zero non-backtracking closed walks of length $\ge 2$; and
  \item[(ii)] every simple cycle $C \subseteq E$ of length $\ge 3$ intersects $E \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ in at least one chord $e^* \in C \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}$, so ratcheting the $\beta_1$ chords intercepts $100\%$ of cyclic walks in $G$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:forest}]
Any $S \subseteq T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ is acyclic, and immediate backtracking ($u \rightarrow v \rightarrow u$) is blocked by $\texttt{egress\_port} \neq \texttt{ingress\_port}$. Any simple cycle $C$ has $\mathbf{1}_C \in \ker(B) \setminus \{\mathbf{0}\}$, whereas columns of $B$ on $T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ are linearly independent ($\ker(B) \cap \mathbb{R}^{T_{\mathrm{forest}}} = \{\mathbf{0}\}$), forcing $C \cap (E \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}) \neq \emptyset$.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Spanning-Forest Zero-Iteration Agreement, Chord-Ratchet Cycle Clamping, \& Dioidal Kleene-Star Tree-Closure Collapse]
\label{thm:agreement}
Let nodes and links carry capability tuples in the bounded meet-semilattice $(\mathcal{L}, \preceq, \sqcap)$ with $\mathcal{L} = \mathbb{F}_2^{64} \times D_\perp$, where $\tau_1 \sqcap \tau_2 := [(r_1 \wedge r_2) \parallel (\mathrm{SPID}_1 \wedge_{\mathrm{flat}} \mathrm{SPID}_2)]$ and $\alpha_{v \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_v) = x_v \sqcap m_{v,e}$. Define the consistency operator $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{cons}}^0$ and admissible sets:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:cons}
\begin{aligned}
(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{cons}}^0 x)_v &:= x_v \sqcap \smashoperator{\bigsqcap_{e=(u,v) \in E}} \bigl(\alpha_{u \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_u) \sqcap \alpha_{v \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_v)\bigr),\\
\Gamma_{\mathrm{adm}}^0(G) &:= \mathrm{Fix}(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{cons}}^0), \quad
\Gamma_{\mathrm{adm}}^1(G) := \delta_{\mathrm{edge}}^0\bigl(\Gamma_{\mathrm{adm}}^0(G)\bigr),
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
where $(\delta_{\mathrm{edge}}^0 x)_e := \alpha_{u \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_u) \sqcap \alpha_{v \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_v)$. If tree branches $e \in T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ satisfy $x_u = \alpha_{u \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_u) = \alpha_{v \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_v) = x_v$ and every chord $e^* = (u,v) \in E \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ instantiates the combinational meet ratchet across $C(e^*) \subset T_{\mathrm{forest}} \cup \{e^*\}$:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:chord_ratchet}
\begin{aligned}
\tau_{e^*}^{\mathrm{out}} &:= \tau_{e^*}^{\mathrm{in}} \sqcap \Gamma_{e^*},\\
\Gamma_{e^*} &:= \alpha_{u \trianglelefteq e^*}^*(x_u) \sqcap \alpha_{v \trianglelefteq e^*}^*(x_v) \sqcap \smashoperator{\bigsqcap_{w \in C(e^*)}} x_w,
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
then: (i)~tree-consistent vertex assignments are stationary under $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{cons}}^0$ in $0$ online consensus rounds; (ii)~the meet operators $\{\mu_a(\tau) = \tau \sqcap a\}$ clamp any closed walk $W$ traversing chords $\{e_1^*,\ldots,e_r^*\}$ to $\tau_W^{\mathrm{out}} \preceq \bigsqcap_{q=1}^r \Gamma_{e_q^*}$ in 1~gate delay per chord; and (iii)~over the dioid $\mathcal{D}_{64} = (\mathbb{F}_2^{64}, \vee, \wedge, \mathbf{0}_{64}, \mathbf{1}_{64})$, the all-pairs Kleene-star closure across $G$ collapses identically to the spanning-forest closure:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:kleene_collapse}
\begin{aligned}
\bigl(A_{G,\mathrm{ratcheted}}\bigr)_{a,b}^*
&:= \bigvee_{k=0}^{|V|-1} \bigl(A_{G,\mathrm{ratcheted}}^k\bigr)_{a,b}\\
&\equiv \bigl(A_{T_{\mathrm{forest}}}\bigr)_{a,b}^* = \smashoperator{\bigsqcap_{w \in P_{T_{\mathrm{forest}}}(a,b)}} x_w.
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:agreement}]
Tree agreement gives $(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{cons}}^0 x)_v = x_v$, proving Clause~(i). Associativity, commutativity, and idempotence of $\sqcap$ prove Clause~(ii). Replacing any chord $e^* = (u,v)$ in $W$ by $P_{T_{\mathrm{forest}}}(u,v) \subset C(e^*)$ yields a tree path dominating $\Gamma_{e^*}$, so absorption $X \vee Y = X$ for $Y \preceq X$ in $\mathcal{D}_{64}$ proves Eq.~\eqref{eq:kleene_collapse}.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Admissible Non-Zero Capability Sieve \& Cold-Reset-Isolated \texttt{BAR0} AND-Ratchet]
\label{cor:ratchet}
Define the non-zero prefix-guarded admissible set
\[
\begin{aligned}
\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{adm}} := \bigl\{(r_i, \mathrm{SPID}_i) :\;& r_i \neq \mathbf{0}_{64},\; \mathrm{SPID}_i \neq 0,\\
&\mathrm{SPID}_i[13:6] == \texttt{port\_spid\_prefix},\\
&\texttt{CSR\_0x70}[\mathrm{SPID}_i[5:0]] == 1\bigr\}.
\end{aligned}
\]
Intersecting Stage~1 inputs with $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{adm}}$ rejects null capabilities $r_i = \mathbf{0}_{64}$ and modulo-64 aliased tenant IDs, while enforcing $M^{(t+1)} := M^{(t)} \wedge w$ on \texttt{BAR0 CSR 0x00, 0x08, 0x60, 0x68, 0x70} isolated from \texttt{host\_warm\_flr\_rst\_n} guarantees $(M^{(t)} \wedge w) \wedge \neg M^{(t)} = \mathbf{0}_{64}$, preventing $0 \rightarrow 1$ escalation across warm FLRs and incrementing \texttt{CSR 0x48} whenever $(w \wedge \neg M^{(t)}) \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}$ (Lean~4 \texttt{thm01}, \texttt{thm02}).
\end{corollary}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Corollary~\ref{cor:ratchet}]
Immediate from Proposition~\ref{prop:monotonic} and $(m_j \wedge w_j) \wedge \neg m_j = 0$ across all 64 bits (\texttt{thm01}, \texttt{thm02}).
\end{proof}

\noindent\textbf{Motivation (Switch HDM Gauge, PHY Failover, \& $+0$-LUT Stage-2 Clamps).}\;Intermediate CXL switches may apply HDM permutations $\pi_e \in S_{64}$ whose group order reaches Landau's $g(64) = \mathrm{lcm}(8,3,5,7,11,13,17) = 2,042,040$; physical link cuts require $<4.0\text{~ns}$ failover without $0 \rightarrow 1$ MMIO writes; and shared-HBM inference requires downgrading coherent \texttt{RdOwn} requests and clamping \texttt{S2M\_DRS} mantissas without adding LUTs. When \texttt{CSR 0x00} arena indices map 1-to-1 onto programmable switch HDM coarse-aperture decoders ($\mathrm{HPA}[51:29]$), \texttt{spectralc} flattens cycle holonomy to $L_j \equiv 1$ via $\widetilde{\pi}_{e^*} := \pi_{e^*}^{-1} \circ \Pi_v \circ \Pi_u^{-1}$; when switch hardware bit-lanes are fixed, every bit-coordinate orbit still has cardinality $L_j \le 64$. Simultaneously, fracturing the 3 unused inputs of Stage~2's existing \texttt{LUT2} gates into 5-shared-input \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} primitives implements both MOESI downgrades and 4-tier mantissa clamping at $+0$ CLB LUTs.

\begin{theorem}[Coarse HDM Gauge Flattening, $S_{64}$ Orbit Clamping, PHY-Gated Dual-Forest Failover, \& Fractured \texttt{LUT6\_2} MOESI and Mantissa Clamping]
\label{thm:sheaf_moesi}
Let $G = (V, E)$ carry switch HDM permutations $\pi_e \in S_{64}$ with tree gauge $\Pi_v := \pi_{e_m} \circ \cdots \circ \pi_{e_1} \in S_{64}$ along $P_{T_{\mathrm{forest}}}(r_0, v)$ and chord holonomy $H_{C(e^*)} := \Pi_v^{-1} \circ \pi_{e^*} \circ \Pi_u \in S_{64}$ on $e^* = (u,v) \in E \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}$. Define the coordinate-orbit-invariant chord mask ($L_j \le 64$) and hardware PCS loss-of-signal mux ($p_{\mathrm{phy}} = \texttt{pcs\_link\_up\_i} \in \{0,1\}$~\cite{ucie2025spec30}) across edge-disjoint forests $(T_0, T_1)$~\cite{tutte1961problem, nashwilliams1961edge}:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:holonomy_orbit}
\begin{aligned}
\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}} &:= \bigwedge_{k=0}^{63} H_{C(e^*)}^k\bigl(\Gamma_{e^*}\bigr),\quad
\tau_{e^*}^{\mathrm{out}} := \pi_{e^*}\bigl(\tau_{e^*}^{\mathrm{in}}\bigr) \sqcap \Pi_v\bigl(\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}\bigr),\\
\Gamma_e^{\mathrm{active}}(p_{\mathrm{phy}}) &:= p_{\mathrm{phy}}\ ?\ \Gamma_e^{\mathrm{orb},(0)} : \Gamma_e^{\mathrm{orb},(1)}.
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
In Stage~2, let $c_i[1:0] \in \{\texttt{2'b00},\texttt{2'b01},\texttt{2'b10},\texttt{2'b11}\}$ denote slot $i$'s 2-bit tier ceiling, with 4 CLB sites configured as $\texttt{INIT} = \texttt{64'hCC00CC00\_A2E0A2E0}$ on \texttt{CXL.cache D2H\_Req} MOESI bits $x_i[1:0]$ and 252 CLB sites configured as 5-shared-input fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} primitives ($\texttt{I5}=\texttt{1'b1}, \texttt{I4}=c_i[1], \texttt{I3}=c_i[0], \texttt{I2}=\mathrm{viol}_i, \texttt{I1}=f_{2m+1}, \texttt{I0}=f_{2m}$) on \texttt{S2M\_DRS} read payloads. Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Coarse HDM Gauge Flattening ($L_j \equiv 1$), $S_{64}$ Orbit Clamping ($L_j \le 64$), \& Failover:} On programmable coarse-aperture switch decoders, setting chord relabeling $\widetilde{\pi}_{e^*} := \pi_{e^*}^{-1} \circ \Pi_v \circ \Pi_u^{-1} \in S_{64}$ flattens $H_{C(e^*)}^{\mathrm{flat}} = \Pi_v^{-1} \circ \pi_{e^*} \circ \widetilde{\pi}_{e^*} \circ \Pi_u = \mathrm{Id}_{S_{64}}$ ($L_j \equiv 1\;\forall j$, Lean~4 \texttt{thm34}); on fixed hardware bit-lanes, $L_j \le 64$ ensures $H_{C(e^*)}(\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}) \equiv \Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}$ at 0~LUT wire-gauge cost (\texttt{thm21}). When a primary branch $e_{\mathrm{cut}} \in T_0$ fails ($p_{\mathrm{phy}} : 1 \rightarrow 0$), hardware switches to $\Gamma_e^{\mathrm{orb},(1)}$ in $< 4.0$~ns with zero MMIO escalation and collapses the Kleene-star closure over $G \setminus \{e_{\mathrm{cut}}\}$ to $(A_{T_1})^*$ (\texttt{thm28});
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Single-\texttt{LUT6\_2} 2-Bit \texttt{CXL.cache} MOESI Clamp ($+0$ CLB LUTs):} $\texttt{INIT} = \texttt{64'hCC00CC00\_A2E0A2E0}$ evaluates $\{\texttt{O6},\texttt{O5}\} = \min(x_i, c_i)$ across all 16 $(c_i, x_i)$ states in 1~LUT delay, enforcing \texttt{CXL\_NOP} ($c_i=\texttt{00}$), pass-through ($c_i=\texttt{11}$), and Girard linear $\mathrm{RdOwn} \mapsto \mathrm{RdShared}$ borrow-checking ($x_i \in \{\texttt{10},\texttt{11}\}, c_i=\texttt{01} \implies \texttt{01}$; Lean~4 \texttt{thm22}, \texttt{thm58}); and
  \item[(iii)] \textbf{Zero-Area Fractured \texttt{LUT6\_2} 4-Tier Mantissa Clamping \& Single-Read Shannon Ceiling:} Across all 32 input states at $+0$ CLB LUTs, the four 64-bit masks
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:graduated_init}
  \begin{aligned}
  \texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{SE/T1}[15:8]} &= \texttt{64'h0C0C0C00\_0A0A0A00}, &
  \texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{BF16}[7:6]} &= \texttt{64'h0C0C0C00\_0A0A0000}, \\
  \texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{Mid}[5:4]} &= \texttt{64'h0C0C0000\_0A0A0000}, &
  \texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{Low}[3:0]} &= \texttt{64'h0C000000\_0A000000},
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  partition each 16-bit \texttt{BF16} lane ($1\mathrm{s} + 8\mathrm{e} + 7\mathrm{m}$) into four precision tiers~\cite{rouhani2023microscaling}---passing sign/exponent bits $\texttt{[15:8]}$ under $c_1 \vee c_0$ ($\texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{SE/T1}[15:8]}$), splitting the boundary pair $\texttt{[7:6]}$ between bit~7 under $c_1 \vee c_0$ on \texttt{O6} and bit~6 under $c_1$ on \texttt{O5} ($\texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{BF16}[7:6]}$, \texttt{thm27}), passing mid-mantissa bits $\texttt{[5:4]}$ under $c_1$ ($\texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{Mid}[5:4]}$), and passing low-mantissa bits $\texttt{[3:0]}$ under $c_1 \wedge c_0$ ($\texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{Low}[3:0]}$), with all 16 bits scrubbing identically to \texttt{16'h0000} at $c_i = \texttt{2'b00}$ or $\mathrm{viol}_i = 1$---yielding $u = 0$ (\texttt{16'h0000} scrub at $c_i=\texttt{00}$ or $\mathrm{viol}_i=1$), $u = 9$ (\texttt{E8M0} scale at $c_i=\texttt{01}$), $u = 12$ (\texttt{BF12} inference at $c_i=\texttt{10}$), and $u = 16$ (\texttt{BF16} at $c_i=\texttt{11}$). These four diagonal projectors form an exact Kadanoff--Wilson coarse-graining semigroup $P_{u_1}P_{u_2} = P_{\min(u_1,u_2)}$ erasing $16 - u \in \{16, 7, 4, 0\}$ bits per lane ($\Delta Q_{\mathrm{Landauer}} = (16-u)k_B T\ln 2$, \texttt{thm68}) and bounding single-read Shannon mutual information by
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:shannon_mantissa}
  \mathrm{I}\bigl(W_{\mathrm{exact}};\,\hat{W} \;\big|\; c_i,\,\mathrm{viol}_i\bigr) \;\le\; \log_2\bigl|\mathrm{im}(\mathcal{Q}_{c_i,\mathrm{viol}_i})\bigr| \;=\; u(c_i, \mathrm{viol}_i),
  \end{equation}
  verified across all 32 states in Lean~4 \texttt{thm27} and \texttt{thm44}.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:sheaf_moesi}]
Cancelling $\Pi_u^{-1}\Pi_u$, $\pi_{e^*}\pi_{e^*}^{-1}$, and $\Pi_v^{-1}\Pi_v$ proves $H_{C(e^*)}^{\mathrm{flat}} = \mathrm{Id}_{S_{64}}$ (\texttt{thm34}), while $|O_j| \le 64$ gives $H_{C(e^*)}(\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}) = \Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}$ (\texttt{thm21}) and $P_{T_1} \vee (r \wedge \Gamma_e^{\mathrm{orb},(1)}) = P_{T_1}$ under $p_{\mathrm{phy}}=0$ (\texttt{thm28}). Exhaustive 16-state and 32-state LUT evaluations establish Clauses~(ii)--(iii) (\texttt{thm22}, \texttt{thm27}).
\end{proof}

\subsection{Wannamaker Keyed-Dither Variance, Rectangular BBP LoRA Thresholds, and Stage-2 \texorpdfstring{\texttt{LUT6\_2}}{LUT6\_2} Streaming Sketch}
\label{sec:dither_bbp_sketch}
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\;Under deterministic truncation, an adversary controlling activation perturbations $\xi^{(q)}$ can bisect a rounding step in $Q_{\mathrm{det}} = \lceil\log_2(\Delta_u/\delta w)\rceil = 6$ queries; at the matrix level, an attacker who knows a public base model $W_{\mathrm{base}}$ can average $Q$ dithered reads of $W = W_{\mathrm{base}} + \Delta W_{\mathrm{LoRA}}$ and compute an SVD~\cite{carlini2024stealing} to extract low-rank LoRA adapters~\cite{hu2022lora}. Closing both threat vectors requires exact non-subtractive quantization variance~\cite{wannamaker2000nonsubtractive} (twice classical subtractive dither~\cite{schuchman1964dither, gray1993dithered}), rectangular Benaych-Georges--Nadakuditi BBP phase-transition bounds~\cite{benaych2012singular}, and separating epoch-static spatial dither from \texttt{CSR 0x38}-capped temporal dither.\par\smallskip

\begin{theorem}[Keyed-Dither Sample-Complexity Lower Bound \& Wannamaker Non-Subtractive Quantization Variance]
\label{thm:dither_lower_bound}
Consider quantized inference tiers $u \in \{9, 12\}$ with mantissa-ULP step size $\Delta_u := 2^{16 - u} \in \{128, 16\}$, fractional grid offset $p := \{w/\Delta_u\} \in [0,1)$, and target precision $\delta w \in (0, \Delta_u/4]$ across $Q_{\mathrm{eff}}$ independent dither realizations $\eta^{(q)} \stackrel{\mathrm{i.i.d.}}{\sim} \mathrm{Unif}[0, \Delta_u)$ derived from the Cycle-0 $\mathcal{X}_{31,33}$ PRF stream seeded by counter $q_{\mathrm{cnt}}$ prior to floor truncation $\mathcal{Q}_{c_i}(z) = \Delta_u \lfloor z/\Delta_u \rfloor$:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Deterministic Boundary Dichotomy:} Without dither, unperturbed reads ($\xi^{(q)} \equiv 0$) yield $0$ extra bits across $Q$ reads, whereas adaptive $\xi^{(q)}$ binary search localizes $w$ in $Q_{\mathrm{det}} = \lceil \log_2(\Delta_u / \delta w) \rceil$ queries ($Q_{\mathrm{det}} = 6$ at $\Delta_9=128, \delta w=2$);
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Conditional vs.\ Prior-Averaged Non-Subtractive Variance~\cite{wannamaker2000nonsubtractive, schuchman1964dither, gray1993dithered}:} Randomized rounding $Y^{(q)} = \mathcal{Q}_{c_i}(w + \eta^{(q)})$ satisfies $\mathbb{E}[Y^{(q)} \mid w] = w$ (so $d_{\mathrm{model}} \ge 4,096$ inner products self-average at $O(1/\sqrt{d_{\mathrm{model}}})$) with conditional sample-mean variance $\mathrm{Var}(\overline{Y}_{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}} \mid p) = \frac{p(1-p)\Delta_u^2}{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ (peaking at $\frac{\Delta_u^2}{4Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ at $p=1/2$, $\ge \frac{3\Delta_u^2}{16Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ on $[1/4,3/4]$, and vanishing at exact grid points $p=0$ unless triangular TPDF dither $\eta_1^{(q)}+\eta_2^{(q)}$~\cite{wannamaker2000nonsubtractive} is used). Under $p \sim \mathrm{Unif}[0,1)$, the prior-averaged variance is $\sigma_{\mathrm{avg}}^2(Q_{\mathrm{eff}}) = \int_0^1 p(1-p)\,dp\,\frac{\Delta_u^2}{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}} = \frac{\Delta_u^2}{6 Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ ($c_{\mathrm{div}} = 6$), twice Schuchman's subtractive-dither floor $\sigma_{\mathrm{sub}}^2(Q_{\mathrm{eff}}) = \frac{\Delta_u^2}{12 Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ ($c_{\mathrm{div}} = 12$); and
  \item[(iii)] \textbf{Le~Cam \& 16-Lane Assouad Quadratic Sample Lower Bound~\cite{assouad1983deux}:} For $|w_1 - w_0| = \delta w$ on $[\Delta_u/4, 3\Delta_u/4]$, any adaptive $Q$-query estimator achieving $\sup_w \mathbb{P}_w(|\hat{w} - w| \ge \delta w/2) \le p_{\mathrm{err}} < 1/2$ obeys:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:lecam_dither}
  \begin{aligned}
  D_{\mathrm{KL}}\bigl(P_{w_0}^{1:Q} \,\|\, P_{w_1}^{1:Q}\bigr) &\le \frac{16}{3} Q \left(\frac{\delta w}{\Delta_u}\right)^{\!2},\\
  Q \ge \frac{3(1 - 2p_{\mathrm{err}})^2}{8}\left(\frac{\Delta_u}{\delta w}\right)^{\!2} &= \Omega\!\left(\left(\frac{\Delta_u}{\delta w}\right)^{\!2}\right),
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  requiring $Q \ge 192$ globally on $[1/4,3/4]$, $Q \ge 512$ at midpoint $p_0 = 1/2$, and $16 \times 512 = 8,192$ subspace queries across 16-lane \texttt{BF16} Assouad hypercubes~\cite{assouad1983deux} (parameter scaling verified in Lean~4 \texttt{thm38}, \texttt{thm39}).
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:dither_lower_bound}]
Under $\eta^{(q)} \sim \mathrm{Unif}[0,\Delta_u)$, $Y^{(q)} - w \in \{-p\Delta_u, (1-p)\Delta_u\}$ with probabilities $(1-p, p)$, giving mean $0$, conditional variance $p(1-p)\Delta_u^2$, and uniform integral $\Delta_u^2/6$~\cite{wannamaker2000nonsubtractive}. Bounding $D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\mathrm{Bern}(p_0)\,\|\,\mathrm{Bern}(p_1)) \le \frac{(p_0-p_1)^2}{p_1(1-p_1)} \le \frac{16}{3}(\delta w/\Delta_u)^2$ on $[1/4,3/4]$ ($2(\delta w/\Delta_u)^2$ at $p_0=1/2$) and applying adaptive KL tensorization, Pinsker's inequality, and Le~Cam / Assouad's method~\cite{assouad1983deux} establishes Eq.~\eqref{eq:lecam_dither}.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Exact Finite-$d$ Weinstein--Aronszajn Gate Parent Law \& Rectangular BGN/BBP LoRA Phase Transition]
\label{cor:bbp_lora}
\label{cor:bbp_wall}
\label{thm:bbp_spectral_wall}
In the white-box-$W_{\mathrm{base}}$ / black-box-$\Delta W_{\mathrm{LoRA}}$ model where $W = W_{\mathrm{base}} + \Delta W_{\mathrm{LoRA}} \in \mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathrm{out}} \times d_{\mathrm{in}}}$ ($\Delta W_{\mathrm{LoRA}} = U_r S_r V_r^\top = \sum_{k=1}^r s_k u_k v_k^\top$~\cite{hu2022lora}) is observed via $\widehat{\Delta W}_{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}} = \overline{Y}_{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}} - W_{\mathrm{base}} = \Delta W_{\mathrm{LoRA}} + E_{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ across $Q_{\mathrm{eff}}$ queries gated by Stage-2 \texttt{LUT6\_2} precision RG projectors $P_{u_1}P_{u_2} = P_{\min(u_1,u_2)}$ (\texttt{thm44}, \texttt{thm68}) and \texttt{CSR 0x38}:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Exact Finite-$(Q_{\mathrm{eff}}, d_{\mathrm{out}}, d_{\mathrm{in}})$ Discretized Gate Parent Law:} At every finite query count $Q_{\mathrm{eff}} \in \{1,\dots,10{,}000\}$ and every finite matrix dimension $(d_{\mathrm{out}} \times d_{\mathrm{in}})$, non-subtractive uniform dither obeys the exact non-asymptotic Wannamaker variance $\sigma_{\mathrm{avg}}^2(Q_{\mathrm{eff}}) = \frac{\Delta_u^2}{6\,Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ (strictly $2\times$ Schuchman's subtractive floor $\frac{\Delta_u^2}{12\,Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}$, zero remainder), and every outlier singular value $s > \sigma_{\max}(E_{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}})$ of $\Delta W_{\mathrm{LoRA}} + E_{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ satisfies the exact finite-$d$ Weinstein--Aronszajn master resolvent determinant identity on the Hermitian dilation $\mathcal{H}_E := \bigl[\begin{smallmatrix} 0 & E_{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}} \\ E_{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}^\top & 0 \end{smallmatrix}\bigr]$:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:weinstein_aronszajn_parent}
  \det\!\Bigl(I_{2r} - \mathcal{D}_r\,\mathcal{G}_{E_{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}}(s)\Bigr) \;\equiv\; 0, \qquad
  \mathcal{D}_r := \begin{bmatrix} 0 & S_r \\ S_r & 0 \end{bmatrix},\;\;
  \mathcal{G}_{E_{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}}(s) := \begin{bmatrix} U_r & 0 \\ 0 & V_r \end{bmatrix}^\top \!\bigl(s\,I_{d_{\mathrm{out}}+d_{\mathrm{in}}} - \mathcal{H}_E\bigr)^{-1}\! \begin{bmatrix} U_r & 0 \\ 0 & V_r \end{bmatrix};
  \end{equation}
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Macroscopic Rectangular BGN/BBP Limit ($d_{\mathrm{out}}/d_{\mathrm{in}} \to \gamma$)~\cite{benaych2012singular, marchenko1967distribution, baik2005phase, gavish2014optimal}:} In the Mar\v{c}enko--Pastur limit $d_{\mathrm{out}}, d_{\mathrm{in}} \to \infty$ with $d_{\mathrm{out}}/d_{\mathrm{in}} \to \gamma \in (0,\infty)$ under $\sigma_{\mathrm{eff}}^2(Q_{\mathrm{eff}}) = \frac{\Delta_u^2}{c_{\mathrm{div}} Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ ($c_{\mathrm{div}} \in \{6,12\}$), $\mathcal{G}_{E_{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}}(s)$ concentrates isotropically, projecting the exact finite-$d$ determinant~\eqref{eq:weinstein_aronszajn_parent} onto the critical query threshold
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:bbp_thresholds}
  \begin{aligned}
  Q_{\mathrm{BBP}}^*\bigl(c_{\mathrm{div}}\bigr) &:= \frac{\Delta_u^2 \sqrt{d_{\mathrm{out}} d_{\mathrm{in}}}}{c_{\mathrm{div}}\,s_r^2}, &
  Q_{\mathrm{Weyl}}^*\bigl(c_{\mathrm{div}}\bigr) &:= \frac{4\,\Delta_u^2 \bigl(\sqrt{d_{\mathrm{out}}} + \sqrt{d_{\mathrm{in}}}\bigr)^2}{c_{\mathrm{div}}\,s_r^2},
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  yielding $Q_{\mathrm{BBP}}^*(12) = 21,845.33$ and $Q_{\mathrm{BBP}}^*(6) = 43,690.67$ at $d_{\mathrm{out}} = d_{\mathrm{in}} = 4,096$, $\Delta_9 = 128\text{~ULP}$, $s_r = 16\text{~ULP}$. Setting $\rho := Q_{\mathrm{eff}} / Q_{\mathrm{BBP}}^*(c_{\mathrm{div}})$, both singular-vector overlaps vanish below threshold ($|\langle \hat{v}_r, v_r \rangle|^2, |\langle \hat{u}_r, u_r \rangle|^2 = O_{\mathbb{P}}(d_{\mathrm{model}}^{-1}) \xrightarrow{\mathrm{a.s.}} 0$ for $\rho \le 1$) and bifurcate above $\rho > 1$ via a second-order Landau--Ginzburg phase transition with exact critical exponent $\beta_{\mathrm{crit}} = 1$:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:bgn_left_right_overlap}
  \bigl|\langle \hat{v}_r, v_r \rangle\bigr|^2 = \frac{\rho^2 - 1}{\rho\bigl(\rho + \sqrt{\gamma}\bigr)}\bigl(1 + o(1)\bigr), \qquad
  \bigl|\langle \hat{u}_r, u_r \rangle\bigr|^2 = \frac{\rho^2 - 1}{\rho\bigl(\rho + 1/\sqrt{\gamma}\bigr)}\bigl(1 + o(1)\bigr) \qquad (\rho > 1),
  \end{equation}
  reducing symmetrically at $\gamma = 1$ to $1 - \rho^{-1}(1+o(1))$ (Table~\ref{tab:additional_exact_laws}, Near-Exact Law~1 \& Remark~\ref{rem:noether_parent_synthesis}; discrete threshold arithmetic checked in Lean~4 \texttt{thm39}, \texttt{thm49}); and
  \item[(iii)] \textbf{Epoch-Static Spatial Dither vs.\ Temporal Seed Rotation:} Under \emph{per-query temporal dither} $\mathrm{PRF}_K(\mathrm{HPA}, i, j, q_{\mathrm{cnt}})$, rotating PRF key $K \mapsto K'$ preserves $\mathbb{E}[Y^{(q)} \mid W] = W$ and \emph{does not} halt $1/Q$ sample-mean convergence across epochs; temporal dither therefore requires SPID-qualified lockout (\texttt{CSR 0x70}$[s] \leftarrow 0$) or adapter re-masking when \texttt{CSR 0x38} reaches $Q_{\max} = 10,000 \le \lfloor Q_{\mathrm{BBP}}^*(12)/2 \rfloor$ (\texttt{SVA 11}, \texttt{thm33}). Conversely, under \emph{epoch-static spatial dither} $\eta_{i,j}^{(e)} = \mathrm{PRF}_{K_e}(\mathrm{HPA}, i, j)$ (omitting $q_{\mathrm{cnt}}$), arbitrary reads within epoch $e$ observe one fixed rounded matrix ($Q_{\mathrm{eff}} = 1$ per key $K_e$), bounding $Q_{\mathrm{eff}} \le E_{\max}$ without cross-tenant DoS.
\end{enumerate}
\end{corollary}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Corollary~\ref{cor:bbp_wall}]
For Clause~(i), $\mathcal{H}_E + \bigl[\begin{smallmatrix}U_r & 0\\ 0 & V_r\end{smallmatrix}\bigr]\mathcal{D}_r\bigl[\begin{smallmatrix}U_r & 0\\ 0 & V_r\end{smallmatrix}\bigr]^\top$ is a rank-$2r$ perturbation of $\mathcal{H}_E$, so Sylvester's determinant identity $\det(I + AB) = \det(I + BA)$ yields Eq.~\eqref{eq:weinstein_aronszajn_parent} identically at every finite $(d_{\mathrm{out}}, d_{\mathrm{in}})$. For Clause~(ii), Benaych-Georges and Nadakuditi~\cite[Thm.~2.8]{benaych2012singular} show that replacing $\mathcal{G}_{E_{Q_{\mathrm{eff}}}}(s)$ by its deterministic Mar\v{c}enko--Pastur Stieltjes limit yields spike emergence iff $s_r^2 > \sigma_{\mathrm{eff}}^2(Q_{\mathrm{eff}})\sqrt{d_{\mathrm{out}} d_{\mathrm{in}}}$ ($\rho > 1$), with right ($\sqrt{\gamma}$) and left ($1/\sqrt{\gamma}$) eigenvector residues given by Eq.~\eqref{eq:bgn_left_right_overlap} (whose Taylor expansion at $\rho = 1 + \epsilon$ begins linearly as $\frac{2}{1+\gamma^{\pm 1/2}}\epsilon^1$, giving $\beta_{\mathrm{crit}} = 1$). Clause~(iii) follows because $Y^{(e)}$ is $K_e$-measurable under static spatial dither ($Q_{\mathrm{eff}} \le E_{\max}$), whereas unbiased temporal samples remain independent across PRF rekeys and must be capped at $Q_{\max}$ (\texttt{thm33}, \texttt{thm39}).
\end{proof}

Figure~\ref{fig:tvla_dither} turns Stage~2's existing \texttt{LUT2} gates into $+0$-LUT rate-distortion valves ($u \in \{0,9,12,16\}$ bits): high-dimensional forward-pass inner products self-average cleanly at $O(1/\sqrt{d_{\mathrm{model}}})$, while \texttt{CSR 0x38} halts query accumulation at $Q_{\max} = 10,000$---strictly inside the Mar\v{c}enko--Pastur bulk ($Q_{\max} < Q_{\mathrm{BBP}}^*$) where empirical singular-vector overlap with the secret LoRA adapter remains trapped at zero---and 2-share DOM masking holds on-die digital toggle/timing Welch TVLA disparity at $\max|t| = 0.0 < 4.5$ across 19,990 live silicon traces.}

\begin{proposition}[Adjacent-Pair Popcount Pre-Reduction (\texttt{64'h060C0A00\_08000000}) \& Stage-2 \texttt{LUT6\_2} Streaming Sketch]
\label{prop:lut6_functional_sketch}
In Stage~2's 252 payload fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} primitives ($\texttt{I5}=\texttt{1'b1}, \texttt{I4}=x_1, \texttt{I3}=x_0, \texttt{I2}=\mathrm{viol}_i, \texttt{I1}=f_{2m+1}, \texttt{I0}=f_{2m}$, table index $a = 16x_1 + 8x_0 + 4\mathrm{viol}_i + 2f_{2m+1} + f_{2m} \in \{0,\ldots,31\}$), configuration mask $\texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{Sketch}} := \texttt{64'h060C0A00\_08000000}$ (or its lane-swapped twin $\texttt{64'h060A0C00\_08000000}$ when $(x_1,x_0)$ swaps byte lanes $(0,1) \leftrightarrow (1,0)$) evaluates across all 32 input states in 1~LUT delay:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:functional_sketch}
\begin{aligned}
\texttt{O6}_{\mathrm{sum}} &= \neg\mathrm{viol}_i \wedge \bigl((f_{2m+1} \wedge x_1) \oplus (f_{2m} \wedge x_0)\bigr),\\
\texttt{O5}_{\mathrm{carry}} &= \neg\mathrm{viol}_i \wedge \bigl((f_{2m+1} \wedge x_1) \wedge (f_{2m} \wedge x_0)\bigr),\\
2\,\texttt{O5}_{\mathrm{carry}} + \texttt{O6}_{\mathrm{sum}} &= (1 - \mathrm{viol}_i)\bigl(f_{2m+1}x_1 + f_{2m}x_0\bigr) \in \{0, 1, 2\},
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
compressing uniform bit-pair Shannon entropy at $(x_1,x_0)=(1,1)$ from $2.0\text{~bits}$ to $1.5\text{~bits}$. Driving the existing 2-bit per-slot bus \texttt{c\_i[1:0]} with $(1,1)$ executes 64 parallel violation-masked adjacent-pair popcount half-additions per 128-bit slot at $+0$ core gates and $+0$ wire changes, whereas lane-heterogeneous binary weights $x \in \{0,1\}^{128}$ and downstream adder-tree accumulation require a dedicated FPGA wrapper persona~\cite{koch2021fabulous} (Lean~4 \texttt{thm36}, \texttt{thm43}).
\end{proposition}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Proposition~\ref{prop:lut6_functional_sketch}]
In $\texttt{32'h060C\_0A00}$ (\texttt{O6}), the bytes for $(x_1,x_0) \in \{(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)\}$ are $\texttt{8'h00}$, $\texttt{8'h0A}$ ($\neg\mathrm{viol}_i \wedge f_{2m}$), $\texttt{8'h0C}$ ($\neg\mathrm{viol}_i \wedge f_{2m+1}$), and $\texttt{8'h06}$ ($\neg\mathrm{viol}_i \wedge (f_{2m+1}\oplus f_{2m})$); in $\texttt{32'h0800\_0000}$ (\texttt{O5}), byte $(1,1)$ is $\texttt{8'h08}$ ($\neg\mathrm{viol}_i \wedge f_{2m+1} \wedge f_{2m}$), verifying Eq.~\eqref{eq:functional_sketch} across all 32 states (\texttt{thm36}).
\end{proof}

\noindent\textbf{Scope \& Price Paid (No Free Lunch --- Rate-Distortion Deterrence \& Dual-Forest Resilience vs.\ Semantic Encryption \& Multi-Cut Faults).}\;Three explicit trade-offs govern Section~\ref{sec:forest}: (1)~graduated mantissa clamping ($u \in \{9,12\}$) is an inline rate-distortion extraction barrier rather than semantic ciphertext---halting multi-epoch sample-mean averaging of unbiased temporal dither requires either epoch-static spatial dither or enforcing the \texttt{CSR 0x38} query budget ($Q_{\max} = 10,000$); (2)~within the frozen 2-bit per-slot \texttt{c\_i[1:0]} core bus, Stage-2 \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} popcount pre-reduction supports uniform slot-broadcast masks $(x_1,x_0) \in \{0,1\}^2$, whereas lane-heterogeneous binary weights require a dedicated wrapper persona; and (3)~PHY-gated $(T_0,T_1)$ failover absorbs any single physical link cut in $<4.0\text{~ns}$, whereas simultaneous cuts disconnecting both $T_0$ and $T_1$ fail closed until \texttt{spectralc} stages a fresh spanning forest.

\section{Renewal-Reward Goodput, CSS Split-Coset Syndrome Exactness, Physical Silicon Sign-Off, and Mode-C Snoop Compaction}
\label{sec:pipeline}

\subsection{Go-Back-N Renewal-Reward Goodput vs.\ Locked-Epoch Drain Scheduling}
\label{sec:goodput}
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\;Stateless link firewalls that abort one unauthorized 128-bit slot by inverting the tail Flit CRC force the receiver to discard the entire 512-bit flit and trigger a Go-Back-N NAK replay of all $N_{\mathrm{pipe}} = 32$ in-flight flits. At an unauthorized slot rate of $p_{\mathrm{bad}} = 0.8\%$, $1 - 0.992^4 = 3.16\%$ of flits are poisoned and link goodput collapses to $48.90\%$. Selective Stage-2 replacement of only the offending slot with a valid \texttt{CXL\_NOP} followed by clean Stage-3 CRC regeneration eliminates link replays ($N_{\mathrm{replay}} \equiv 0$) and preserves $99.20\%$ goodput.\par\smallskip

\begin{theorem}[Go-Back-N Renewal-Reward Goodput Collapse and Monotone Locked-Epoch Utilization Bound]
\label{thm:goodput}
Consider a \mbox{CXL~3.0--4.0} link packing $m = 4$ slots per flit phase with Go-Back-N replay pipe depth $N_{\mathrm{pipe}} \ge 1$ flits under unauthorized slot rate $p_{\mathrm{bad}} \in [0, 1)$. Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Whole-Flit CRC Poisoning Collapse vs.\ Selective Per-Slot Work Conservation:} Whole-flit CRC poisoning aborts any flit containing $\ge 1$ bad slot ($q_{\mathrm{flit}} = 1 - (1 - p_{\mathrm{bad}})^m$). Under fresh-flit replacement where each NAK expends $N_{\mathrm{pipe}}$ cycles, renewal-reward theory gives:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:goodput}
  \begin{aligned}
  \eta_{\mathrm{goodput}}^{\mathrm{CRC}}(p_{\mathrm{bad}}) &= \frac{(1 - p_{\mathrm{bad}})^m}{1 + (N_{\mathrm{pipe}} - 1)\bigl(1 - (1 - p_{\mathrm{bad}})^m\bigr)},\\
  \eta_{\mathrm{goodput}}^{\mathrm{slot}}(p_{\mathrm{bad}}) &= 1 - p_{\mathrm{bad}},
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  so at $m = 4, N_{\mathrm{pipe}} = 32, p_{\mathrm{bad}} = 0.8\%$, legacy goodput collapses to $48.90\%$, whereas \textsc{Kernel Zero} maintains $99.20\%$ ($N_{\mathrm{replay}} \equiv 0$); and
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Monotone Locked-Epoch Reconfiguration Bound~\cite{azar2009buffer, azar2013metric, azar2026lossless}:} For colored packet streams $\sigma$ with deadlines and minimum laxity $L = \min_i\{d_i - r_i\}$~\cite{azar2009buffer} along a monotone epoch schedule $G_{\mathrm{active}}$ with transition drain cost $1 \le \mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}}) \le \frac{\delta_{\max}^2}{9} L$~\cite{azar2013metric}, batching transitions at $K = \lceil \sqrt{\mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}}) L} \rceil$ cycles~\cite{azar2026lossless} guarantees:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:azar_vardi}
  \begin{aligned}
  \mathrm{ALG}(\sigma) &\ge \left(1 - \frac{3\sqrt{\mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}})}}{\sqrt{L}}\right) \mathrm{OPT}(\sigma)\\
  &\ge (1 - \delta_{\max})\mathrm{OPT}(\sigma) > 0.
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:goodput}]
By the renewal-reward theorem, $\mathbb{E}[R_{\mathrm{cyc}}] / (m\,\mathbb{E}[T_{\mathrm{cyc}}]) = (1 - q_{\mathrm{flit}}) / [1 + (N_{\mathrm{pipe}} - 1)q_{\mathrm{flit}}]$, yielding at $m = 4, N_{\mathrm{pipe}} = 32, p_{\mathrm{bad}} = 0.008$ the exact ratio $0.992^4 / [1 + 31(1 - 0.992^4)] = 0.9683820 / 1.9801594 = 0.4890424 \approx 48.90\%$ vs.\ $1 - p_{\mathrm{bad}} = 99.20\%$. For Clause~(ii), setting $x = \sqrt{\mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}}) L} \ge 1$ gives $x \le K < 2x$, bounding deadline rounding loss by $\le 2\sqrt{\mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}})/L}$~\cite{azar2009buffer} and drain loss by $\le \sqrt{\mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}})/L}$~\cite{azar2013metric, azar2026lossless}.
\end{proof}

\textbf{So What? (The Link-Availability Payoff --- Ending Co-Packed Fate Sharing Without Replay Storms).}\;Legacy stateless firewalls that poison the tail Flit CRC treat a 512-bit CXL flit as an indivisible fate-sharing unit, allowing a single misbehaving tenant ($p_{\mathrm{bad}} = 0.8\%$) to inflict a $51.1\%$ bandwidth denial-of-service penalty on three innocent co-packed tenants via Go-Back-N link replay storms. Figure~\ref{fig:hbm_goodput} proves both analytically and on live 16~GB HBM2 DMA silicon (\texttt{\AwsHbmAgfiId}) that surgical Stage-2 per-slot sanitization decouples tenant fate, sustaining $99.20\%$ link goodput with $0$ link NAKs and $100\%$ HBM2 target data integrity.}
