\section{Operator-to-Netlist Traceability, Inductive \texorpdfstring{\texttt{ZK-IC3}}{ZK-IC3}, Karamata Water-Filling, Four-Invariant Summary, and Exascale Roadmap}
\label{sec:traceability}

\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\;Closing the verification loop from abstract algebra to deployed exascale clusters requires four final bridges: proving that the private post-route gate netlist contains zero reachable sequential specification mismatches across $t \in \mathbb{N}$; federating multi-pod interconnects without leaking interior switch topologies; scheduling online cohort arrivals/departures without 8-line intra-slope wipeouts; and uniting all 99 headline theorems (\LeanTheoremTotal\ core + 12 mathematical-physics corollaries) under exact cochain complexes and four canonical hardware invariants.\par\smallskip

\subsection{Inductive \texorpdfstring{\texttt{ZK-IC3}}{ZK-IC3} Attestation, Tropical Pod Federation, and WDDL Boundary}
\label{sec:zk_tropical}
Table~\ref{tab:traceability} maps each mathematical operator onto its physical \texttt{xcvu47p} netlist primitive. Under the source-netlist separation policy, Zone~2 publishes \id{reports_public/ZK_LEC_MITER_RECEIPT.json} proving exhaustive per-cone CNF/LRAT Miter UNSAT ($\mathcal{M}_k(x) \equiv 0$ across $100\%$ of $2^{w_k}$ states, $w_k \le 24$), 65,536-cycle sequential trace equivalence, and infinite-horizon 1-step inductive \texttt{ZK-IC3} safety~\cite{bradley2011sat}.

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{ZK-IC3} Unbounded Sequential Miter Equivalence]
\label{thm:zk_ic3}
Let $C_{\mathrm{edf}}$ denote the SHA-256-committed Zone-1 post-route~\texttt{.edf} netlist with sequential register vector $S = (P_{\mathrm{CSR}}, \mathrm{cnt}_{\mathrm{RwD}}, Q_{\mathrm{RwD}}, \mathrm{lock}_{\mathrm{eg}}, S_{\mathrm{dp}}) \in \mathbb{F}_2^{n_s}$, primary inputs $X \in \mathbb{F}_2^{n_x}$, Tseitin transition relation $T_{\mathrm{edf}}(S,X,S') := \mathrm{Tseitin}(C_{\mathrm{edf}})$, public Lean~4 specification $C_{\mathrm{spec}}$ with abstraction map $\pi_{\mathrm{abs}}$, and miter violation predicate $\mathrm{Bad}_{\mathrm{miter}}(S,X) := [\mathrm{Out}_{\mathrm{edf}}(S,X) \neq \mathrm{Out}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\pi_{\mathrm{abs}}(S),X)] \vee [(P'_{\mathrm{CSR}} \wedge \neg P_{\mathrm{CSR}}) \neq \mathbf{0}]$. Define the 1-step inductive invariant:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:ic3_invariant}
\begin{aligned}
\mathcal{I}(S) :\iff\;& \bigl(0 \le \mathrm{cnt}_{\mathrm{RwD}} \le 32\bigr)
\wedge \bigl(\forall j \ge \mathrm{cnt}_{\mathrm{RwD}},\; Q_{\mathrm{RwD}}[j] = 0\bigr)\\
&\wedge \bigl(P_{\mathrm{CSR}} \preceq P_{\mathrm{CSR}}^{(0)}\bigr)
\wedge \bigl(\pi_{\mathrm{abs}}(S_{\mathrm{edf}}) = S_{\mathrm{spec}}\bigr)\\
&\wedge \bigwedge_{i=0}^3 \bigl(\texttt{s1\_slot\_viol\_rep\_q}[i] \in \{\texttt{8'h00}, \texttt{8'hFF}\}\bigr).
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
If a prover attests LRAT resolution proofs $(\pi_{\mathrm{init}}, \pi_{\mathrm{ind}}, \pi_{\mathrm{bad}})$ for $\mathrm{Init}(S) \wedge \neg\mathcal{I}(S)$, $\mathcal{I}(S) \wedge T_{\mathrm{edf}}(S,X,S') \wedge \neg\mathcal{I}(S')$, and $\mathcal{I}(S) \wedge \mathrm{Bad}_{\mathrm{miter}}(S,X)$ anchored to $h_{\mathrm{netlist}} = \mathrm{SHA\text{-}256}(C_{\mathrm{edf}})$ (locally in Tier~1 or inside a Tier-2 VOLE-ZK/STARK circuit), then across all $t \in \mathbb{N}$ and all input sequences $\{X^{(t)}\}_{t=0}^{\infty}$: (i)~$\mathcal{I}(S^{(t)})$ holds for all $t \ge 0$; (ii)~$\mathrm{Bad}_{\mathrm{miter}}(S^{(t)}, X^{(t)}) \equiv 0$, guaranteeing zero reachable sequential miter-divergence or $0 \rightarrow 1$ escalation states; and (iii)~0~\texttt{.sv} or~\texttt{.edf} gates enter the public repository (\texttt{thm45}).
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:zk_ic3}]
Soundness of $\mathrm{VerifyLRAT}$ on $(\pi_{\mathrm{init}}, \pi_{\mathrm{ind}})$ establishes $\mathcal{I}(S^{(t)})$ for all $t \in \mathbb{N}$ by mathematical induction, and $\pi_{\mathrm{bad}}$ proves $\mathcal{I}(S^{(t)}) \implies \neg\mathrm{Bad}_{\mathrm{miter}}(S^{(t)}, X^{(t)})$.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Boundary Transfer Summaries for Multi-Pod Routing \& Shadow IDE Key Filtration]
\label{thm:pod_gluing}
Across federated pods $G_A, G_B, G_C$ interconnected via gateways $\Sigma_{AB}, \Sigma_{BC}, \Sigma_{CA}$ with chord-ratcheted interior edges $\tilde{G}_{a,b}^{\mathrm{int}} := G_{a,b}^{\mathrm{int}} \wedge \Gamma_{a,b}$: (i)~exporting only boundary Kleene-star transfer summaries $T_{u,v} = \tilde{G}_{u,v}^{\mathrm{dir}} \vee \bigvee_{s_1, s_2 \in S} (\tilde{G}_{u,s_1}^{\mathrm{int}} \wedge (\tilde{G}_{S,S}^{\mathrm{int}})_{s_1,s_2}^* \wedge \tilde{G}_{s_2,v}^{\mathrm{int}})$ conceals $100\%$ of interior switch nodes $|S|$ while enforcing $\Gamma_{ABC} = T_A \wedge T_B \wedge T_C$; (ii)~boundary syndromes cancel via $(c_A \oplus c_\Sigma) \oplus (c_B \oplus c_\Sigma) = c_A \oplus c_B$; and (iii)~staging keys into \texttt{CSR 0x10/0x18} leaves the active bank invariant (\texttt{thm12}, \texttt{thm13}).
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:pod_gluing}]
Boolean absorption $a \vee (a \wedge b) = a$ collapses walks of length $\ge |S|$ into simple paths, and $c_\Sigma \oplus c_\Sigma = \mathbf{0}_{64}$ cancels shared boundary syndromes (\texttt{thm12}, \texttt{thm13}).
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Tropical-Holonomy $(\max,+) \otimes S_{64}$ Pod Federation \& Optical Deskew Credit Sufficiency]
\label{thm:tropical_pod}
Lift each directed edge $e = (u,v)$ to the capability-annihilated dioid $\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{trop}} := (\mathbb{R}_{\max,+} \cup \{-\infty\}) \times \mathcal{D}_{64}$ with edge weight $W_e = (d_e, \pi_e, \Gamma_e^{\mathrm{active}}(p_{\mathrm{phy}}))$, mapping walks with $\Gamma(P) = \mathbf{0}_{64}$ to $-\infty$. Exporting only $(D_{B,B}^*, T_{B,B}^*)$ leaks $0$ bits of interior topology $|S|$, while sizing the Stage-4 credit deskew buffer across optical lane skew $\Delta_{\mathrm{skew}}$ and round-trip $\mathrm{RTT}_{\max}$ to
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:tropical_buffer}
B^* := \left\lceil \frac{\mathrm{RTT}_{\max} + \Delta_{\mathrm{skew}}}{t_{\mathrm{flit}}} \right\rceil
\end{equation}
guarantees $100\%$ line-rate utilization ($\eta = 1$) with zero credit starvation and $\Delta t_{\mathrm{CRD}} \equiv 0$.
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:tropical_pod}]
Orbit clamping $\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}$ and anti-hairpinning restrict $(D_{B,B}^*, T_{B,B}^*)$ to boundary pairs $B \times B$, and Little's law~\cite{little1961proof} in $(\max,+)$ calculus yields $B^*$ in-flight flits.
\end{proof}

\begin{proposition}[Mode-D WDDL $50\%$ Duty-Cycle Boundary vs.\ Cut-Through Bandwidth--Power Dichotomy]
\label{prop:wddl_dichotomy}
Across a $W$-bit bus at clock $f_{\mathrm{clk}}$ and activity $\alpha_{\mathrm{data}} \in (0, 1/2]$, Wave Dynamic Differential Logic (WDDL 1-of-2 precharge~\cite{gross2016dom}) alternates $(0,0) \rightarrow (x_i, \neg x_i) \rightarrow (0,0)$, eliminating first-order current variance under $C_{\mathrm{true}} = C_{\mathrm{comp}}$ but imposing a mandatory $50\%$ duty-cycle ceiling ($R_{\mathrm{WDDL}} = \frac{1}{2} W f_{\mathrm{clk}}$) and $\frac{E_{\mathrm{WDDL}}}{E_{\mathrm{cut}}} = \frac{2}{\alpha_{\mathrm{data}}} \ge 4\times$ dynamic energy per bit. Single-rail cut-through (Modes~A/B.2/C) is therefore uniquely Pareto-optimal inside intra-pod HBM pools, whereas Mode~D (DOM \texttt{thm24} + De~Morgan \texttt{RAIN} dual \texttt{thm37} + \texttt{HD.ISOLATED} moats) is deployed strictly at cross-domain \texttt{RAIN} perimeter gateways~\cite{ncdsmo2020rain}.
\end{proposition}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Proposition~\ref{prop:wddl_dichotomy}]
Each WDDL wire pair charges and discharges once per 2-phase symbol ($C_0 V_{\mathrm{DD}}^2$ energy, $\frac{1}{2} W f_{\mathrm{clk}}$ rate) vs.\ $\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{\mathrm{data}} C_0 V_{\mathrm{DD}}^2$ and $W f_{\mathrm{clk}}$ for single-rail cut-through.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Modulo-$2^k$ Ripple-Carry Telescoping, 4-Channel OOO DMA Ratchet, \& Dual-Rail Fault Detector]
\label{thm:extensions}
Around the enclave periphery: (i)~the recursive ripple-carry recurrence $c_{i+1}(a,b) = (a_i \wedge b_i) \oplus ((a_i \oplus b_i) \wedge c_i)$ ($c_0 = 0$) over $\mathbb{Z}/2^k\mathbb{Z}$ satisfies exact telescoping $\mathrm{sub}_k(\mathrm{add}_k(\mathrm{acc}, \Delta), \Delta) = \mathrm{acc}_k$ for all $k \in \mathbb{N}$ in $0\text{~BRAM/DSP}$ (\texttt{thm15}); (ii)~4-channel 64-cacheline OOO DMA masks clear $\mathrm{step}(\mathrm{live}_{q,c}, \mathrm{idx})_j := \mathrm{live}_{q,c,j} \wedge \neg\mathrm{eqb}(j, \mathrm{idx})$ on each beat $\mathrm{idx} = \mathrm{HPA}[11:6]$ to reject duplicate beats (\texttt{thm14}); and (iii)~1-of-2 reduction trees over $\texttt{O5}, \texttt{O6}$, and \texttt{CSR 0x70} trap $\texttt{2'b00}/\texttt{2'b11}$ faults in \texttt{CSR 0x48} (\texttt{thm16}).
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:extensions}]
Peano induction via \texttt{@Nat.rec} proves Clause~(i) (\texttt{thm15}), while $\mathrm{step}(\mathrm{live}_{q,c},\mathrm{idx})_{\mathrm{idx}} = \mathrm{false}$ and $\texttt{O5} \oplus \texttt{O6} = r_i$ prove Clauses~(ii)--(iii) (\texttt{thm14}, \texttt{thm16}).
\end{proof}

\subsection{Certifying \texorpdfstring{\texttt{SKILL.md}}{SKILL.md} Compilation, Karamata Water-Filling, and Four-Complex / Four-Invariant Unification}
\label{sec:compiler}
For DOE Genesis \texttt{SKILL.md} / Argonne \texttt{ModCon} manifests~\cite{doe2025genesis}, Flux/Slurm prologs, and Kubernetes \texttt{SpectralMemoryPolicy} CRDs, the modular connection homomorphism $\Psi_{\mathrm{ModCon}} : \mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SKILL}} \rightarrow \mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{BAR0}}$ in Eq.~\eqref{eq:modcon_map} compiles declarative agent scopes directly into certified 16-CSR \texttt{BAR0} bundles at $+0$ silicon gates.
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:modcon_map}
\begin{aligned}
\Psi_{\mathrm{ModCon}}\bigl(\mathcal{M}_1 \sqcap \mathcal{M}_2\bigr) &= \Psi_{\mathrm{ModCon}}(\mathcal{M}_1) \sqcap_{\mathrm{BAR0}} \Psi_{\mathrm{ModCon}}(\mathcal{M}_2),\\[3pt]
\Psi_{\mathrm{ModCon}}(\mathcal{M}) &= \Bigl(\underbrace{P_{\mathrm{coarse}}, P_{\mathrm{fine}}^{[0:1]}, \mathrm{SPID}_{\mathrm{live}}}_{\texttt{CSR 0x00, 0x60, 0x70}},\; \underbrace{p_{t,\mathrm{wheel}}[\tau_{\mathrm{TTL}}]}_{\texttt{CSR 0x68 (thm26)}},\\[-2pt]
&\phantom{=\Bigl(\;}\underbrace{c_{\mathrm{MOESI}}, c_{\mathrm{mant}}}_{\texttt{CSR 0x78 (thm22,27)}},\; \underbrace{\Sigma_{\mathrm{prov}}}_{\texttt{CSR 0x58 (thm04)}}\Bigr).
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}

\begin{theorem}[Polynomial-Time Soundness of Certifying \texttt{BAR0} Compilation (\texttt{spectralc})~\cite{mcconnell2011certifying}]
\label{thm:compiler}
Although offline MSFCB cycle-basis selection and $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ hypergraph coloring are NP/APX-hard, if the Lean~4 certificate checker (\texttt{thm18}, \texttt{thm28}) verifies in $O(|V| + |E| + |\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{assigned}}|^2)$ time that: (1)~$(M_{\mathrm{new}}[i] \wedge \neg M_{\mathrm{curr}}[i]) = \mathrm{false}$ and active $\mathrm{Roots}(\Lambda^{(t)}) \subseteq \mathrm{Roots}(\Lambda^{(t+1)})$; (2)~the edge-disjoint failover forests $T_0, T_1 \subset E$ are acyclic with $(\mathrm{chord}_{e^*}^{(b)}[i] \wedge \neg\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb},(b)}[i]) = \mathrm{false}$; and (3)~$\mathrm{wt}(c) = w$ and $\mathrm{wt}(c \wedge c') \le \lambda$ across $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{assigned}}$; then $(r \wedge M_{\mathrm{new}}) \wedge \neg M_{\mathrm{curr}} = \mathbf{0}_{64}$, $(r \wedge \mathrm{chord}_{e^*}^{(b)}) \wedge \neg\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb},(b)} = \mathbf{0}_{64}$, and $P_{\mathrm{alias}}(R) \equiv 0$ up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}}$.
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:compiler}]
Bitwise verification across $i \in \{0,\ldots,63\}$ combined with Theorems~\ref{thm:johnson}, \ref{thm:vieta}, and~\ref{thm:sheaf_moesi} guarantees $100\%$ fail-closed soundness~\cite{mcconnell2011certifying} (\texttt{thm18}, \texttt{thm28}).
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Karamata Schur-Convex Online Water-Filling \& Submodular Parallel-Slope Cohort Packing]
\label{thm:karamata_waterfilling}
\label{thm:submodular_modcon}
Partition the 72 lines of $\mathrm{AG}(2,8) \cong S(2,8,64)$ into $m_s = 9$ parallel-slope classes $\mathcal{P}_0,\ldots,\mathcal{P}_8$ of $8$ bit-disjoint lines each ($\lambda = 0$ intra-slope, $\lambda = 1$ inter-slope) under the partition matroid $\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{slope}} := \{S \subseteq \mathrm{AG}(2,8) : |S \cap \mathcal{P}_b| \le 7\;\forall b\}$. Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Karamata Schur-Convex Online Water-Filling ($W_8 \equiv 0$ for $N_{\mathrm{tot}} \le 63$)~\cite{marshall2011inequalities}:} Under online arrivals and departures with slope occupancy $\mathbf{n}^{(t)} = (n_0^{(t)},\ldots,n_8^{(t)}) \in \{0,\ldots,7\}^9$ and $N_{\mathrm{tot}}^{(t)} = \sum_{b=0}^8 n_b^{(t)} \le 63$, assigning each arrival to $b^* = \arg\min_{b : n_b^{(t)} < 7} n_b^{(t)}$ succeeds with zero migrations (by Pigeonhole, $N_{\mathrm{tot}}^{(t)} \le 62 \implies \min_b n_b^{(t)} \le \lfloor 62/9 \rfloor = 6 < 7$) and holds same-slope 8-line wipeouts identically at $W_8(\mathbf{n}^{(t)}) := \sum_{b=0}^8 \binom{n_b^{(t)}}{8} \equiv 0$. Because $\Delta^2 \binom{x}{k} = \binom{x}{k-2} \ge 0$ ($k \ge 2$), $W_k(\mathbf{n}) := \sum_{b=0}^8 \binom{n_b}{k}$ is Schur-convex~\cite{marshall2011inequalities}, so water-filled occupancies minimize intra-slope $k$-tuples $W_k$ and maximize inter-slope pairs $\Omega_{\mathrm{pairs}}(\mathbf{n}) = \binom{N_{\mathrm{tot}}}{2} - W_2(\mathbf{n})$ (Lean~4 \texttt{thm50}, \texttt{thm53}--\texttt{thm57}); and
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Submodular Bit-Dispersion Potential $G_{\mathcal{H}}(S)$~\cite{calinescu2011maximizing}:} For dispersion weights $\omega_c \in (0,1]$,
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:submodular_dispersion}
  \begin{aligned}
  G_{\mathcal{H}}(S) &:= \sum_{j=0}^{63} \Biggl(1 - \smashoperator{\prod_{c \in S : j \in \mathrm{supp}(c)}} \bigl(1 - \omega_c\bigr)\Biggr),\\
  \Omega_{\mathrm{coll}}(S) &:= 8 \sum_{c \in S} \omega_c - G_{\mathcal{H}}(S) \ge 0,
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  is non-negative, monotone, and submodular over $\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{slope}}$, admitting a $(1 - 1/e) \approx 63.21\%$ continuous-greedy guarantee~\cite{calinescu2011maximizing} and $1/2$ deterministic greedy guarantee in $O(N_{\mathrm{ag}} |\mathrm{AG}(2,8)|)$ time.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:submodular_modcon}]
For $N_{\mathrm{tot}}^{(t)} \le 62$, $\lfloor 62/9 \rfloor = 6 < 7 \implies n_b^{(t)} \le 7 \implies \binom{n_b^{(t)}}{8} = 0$, and Robin-Hood transfers from $n_{\mathrm{hi}} \ge n_{\mathrm{lo}} + 2$ strictly decrease $W_2$ by $n_{\mathrm{hi}} - n_{\mathrm{lo}} - 1 \ge 1$ via Karamata's theorem~\cite{marshall2011inequalities} (\texttt{thm53}--\texttt{thm56}). Decreasing marginal bit-coverage products $(1 - \omega_a) \in [0,1)$ establish submodularity of $G_{\mathcal{H}}(S)$~\cite{calinescu2011maximizing}.
\end{proof}

\subsubsection*{Outright Proofs of the Four Mathematical-Physics Isomorphisms (Laws~I--IV)}

While continuum Lorentz-invariant electrodynamics on $(\mathbb{R}^{3,1},\eta_{\mu\nu})$ is governed by continuous partial differential operators over $\mathbb{R}$, the four hardware invariants of Theorem~\ref{thm:homological_unification} satisfy four strict, symbol-for-symbol isomorphism theorems in discrete gauge theory, Hodge--Kirchhoff network theory, and characteristic-2 Clifford/cochain algebra once two boundary distinctions are stated explicitly: (1)~on the full ungated polynomial ring $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$, linear factor multiplication $M_\alpha(P):=(Z\oplus\alpha)\odot P$ is not nilpotent ($M_\alpha^2\neq 0$) and obeys the Toeplitz--Cuntz identity $D_\alpha M_\alpha=\mathrm{Id}$ with rank-1 commutator $[D_\alpha,M_\alpha]=\mathbf{1}\cdot\mathrm{ev}_\alpha$, whereas Cycle-0 duplicate rejection ($\Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha)\neq 0$) paired with \texttt{SVA~1\textquotesingle} zero-remainder gating ($q_8=\Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha)=0$) restricts \texttt{CSR 0x60} to the square-free Fock space $\mathcal{F}=\mathbb{F}_2[\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times)]$, on which $(\hat{a}_\alpha^\dagger,\hat{a}_\alpha)$ satisfy the exact 255-mode Canonical Anticommutation Relations (CAR)~\cite{jordan1928pauli} of $\mathrm{Cl}_{255,255}(\mathbb{F}_2)$; and (2)~whereas the 4-byte miniature witness $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}^2 \xrightarrow{E_{\mathrm{fold}}} \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^4 \xrightarrow{H_{\mathrm{FEC}}} \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^2$ (\texttt{thm52}/\texttt{thm61}) is short exact ($H^1=0$), the full 256-byte CXL~3.0/4.0 flit complex $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{242} \xrightarrow{E_{\mathrm{fold}}} \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{256} \xrightarrow{H_{\mathrm{FEC}}} \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^6$ has $\dim\ker H_{\mathrm{FEC}}=250$ and $\dim\mathrm{im}\,E_{\mathrm{fold}}=242$, yielding the exact 8-byte first cohomology group $H^1(\mathcal{C}^\bullet)\cong\mathbb{F}_{2^8}^8$ ($\chi=-8$).

\begin{theorem}[Law~I: Cut-Set Gauss Divergence and Gated Vieta--B\'{e}zout Clifford/Fermion CAR Algebra]
\label{thm:law1_gauss_car}
Let $G=(V,E)$ be a directed interconnect graph partitioned into interior memory-pool bulk $\Omega\subset V$ and untrusted exterior $V\setminus\Omega$ across the oriented boundary cut-set $\partial\Omega = E(V\setminus\Omega, \Omega) \sqcup E(\Omega, V\setminus\Omega)$, and let $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}\cong\mathbb{F}_2[x]/(x^8+x^4+x^3+x+1)$ (\texttt{0x11B}).
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Discrete Gauss Divergence \& Markov Cut-Set Sufficiency:} Let $d : R^V \to R^E$, $(df)_{u\to v} = f(v) - f(u)$, and $d^* : R^E \to R^V$ denote the coboundary and divergence operators over any commutative ring $R$ ($\mathbb{Z}, \mathbb{R}, \mathbb{F}_2$). For every 1-chain flux $J \in R^E$ with bulk charge density $\rho := d^* J \in R^V$, the indicator $\mathbf{1}_\Omega \in R^V$ has coboundary $(d\mathbf{1}_\Omega)_{u\to v} = +1$ on ingress edges $E(V\setminus\Omega,\Omega)$ and $-1$ on egress edges $E(\Omega,V\setminus\Omega)$, satisfying
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:law1_gauss_div}
\sum_{v \in \Omega} \rho(v) \;=\; \langle \mathbf{1}_\Omega, d^* J \rangle \;=\; \langle d\mathbf{1}_\Omega, J \rangle \;=\; \sum_{e \in E(V\setminus\Omega,\Omega)} J(e) \;-\; \sum_{e \in E(\Omega,V\setminus\Omega)} J(e)
\end{equation}
(reducing modulo $2$ to $\bigoplus_{e\in\partial\Omega}J(e)$ over $R = \mathbb{F}_2$). In particular, a charge-free controller bulk ($\rho|_{\Omega} \equiv 0 \iff B_{\mathrm{rest}} \equiv 0$) forces exact oriented boundary flux balance $\sum_{e \in E(V\setminus\Omega,\Omega)} J(e) - \sum_{e \in E(\Omega,V\setminus\Omega)} J(e) = 0$, and for any Markov chain $X_{V\setminus\Omega} \to X_{\partial\Omega} \to X_\Omega$, conditional mutual information vanishes identically: $I(X_{V\setminus\Omega}; X_\Omega \mid X_{\partial\Omega}) = 0$, which under the identification $(X_{V\setminus\Omega}, X_{\partial\Omega}, X_\Omega) = (\Pi^{(0)}, K_{\mathrm{active}}, S_{\mathrm{ctrl}})$ recovers $I(\Pi^{(0)}; S_{\mathrm{ctrl}} \mid K_{\mathrm{active}}) = 0$ of Proposition~\ref{prop:little_vieta}(i).
\item \textbf{Ungated Toeplitz--Cuntz Commutator on $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$:} For each $\alpha \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$, define root multiplication $M_\alpha(P)(Z) := (Z \oplus \alpha) \odot P(Z)$ and B\'{e}zout--Horner quotient $D_\alpha(P)(Z) := Q^{\mathrm{defl}}_\alpha(P)(Z)$ from $P(Z) = ((Z \oplus \alpha)\odot Q^{\mathrm{defl}}_\alpha(P)(Z)) \oplus P(\alpha)$. Then for all $\alpha, \beta \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$ and $P \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:law1_toeplitz_cuntz}
D_\alpha \circ M_\alpha = \mathrm{Id}_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]}, \qquad [D_\alpha, M_\alpha](P) := (D_\alpha M_\alpha \oplus M_\alpha D_\alpha)(P) = P(\alpha)\cdot 1_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]}, \qquad [D_\alpha, D_\beta] = [M_\alpha, M_\beta] = 0.
\end{equation}
\item \textbf{Exact 255-Mode Clifford / Fermion CAR Algebra on the \texttt{SVA~1\textquotesingle}-Gated Fock Space:} Let $\mathcal{F} := \bigoplus_{S \subseteq \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times} \mathbb{F}_2\,|\Lambda_S\rangle$ denote the $\mathbb{F}_2$-vector space spanned by monic square-free annihilators $\Lambda_S(Z) = \prod_{\gamma \in S} (Z \oplus \gamma)$. Define the hardware-gated root creation operator $\hat{a}_\alpha^\dagger |\Lambda_S\rangle := \mathbb{I}[\Lambda_S(\alpha) \neq 0]\,|(Z \oplus \alpha)\odot \Lambda_S\rangle$ and \texttt{SVA~1\textquotesingle} remainder-gated annihilation operator $\hat{a}_\alpha |\Lambda_S\rangle := \mathbb{I}[\Lambda_S(\alpha) = 0]\,|D_\alpha(\Lambda_S)\rangle$. Then for all $\alpha, \beta \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$, the anticommutator $\{X, Y\}_+ := X Y \oplus Y X$ satisfies the exact Canonical Anticommutation Relations of $\mathrm{Cl}_{255,255}(\mathbb{F}_2)$:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:law1_clifford_car}
\bigl\{\hat{a}_\alpha, \hat{a}_\beta^\dagger\bigr\}_+ = \delta_{\alpha,\beta}\,\mathrm{Id}_{\mathcal{F}}, \qquad \bigl\{\hat{a}_\alpha^\dagger, \hat{a}_\beta^\dagger\bigr\}_+ = 0, \qquad \bigl\{\hat{a}_\alpha, \hat{a}_\beta\bigr\}_+ = 0, \qquad \sum_{\alpha \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times} \hat{a}_\alpha^\dagger \hat{a}_\alpha \,|\Lambda_S\rangle = \bigl(|S| \bmod 2\bigr)|\Lambda_S\rangle,
\end{equation}
while lifting the idempotent mode-occupation projectors $\hat{n}_\alpha := \hat{a}_\alpha^\dagger \hat{a}_\alpha$ ($\hat{n}_\alpha|\Lambda_S\rangle = \mathbb{I}[\alpha \in S]|\Lambda_S\rangle$) to $\mathbb{Z}$ via $\mathrm{b2n}$ (or adjoining the Jordan--Wigner sign $(-1)^{|\{\gamma \in S : \gamma < \alpha\}|}$ over $\mathbb{C}$) yields the exact integer revocation degree counter $\hat{N}_{\mathrm{rev}}|\Lambda_S\rangle = \sum_{\alpha \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times} \mathrm{b2n}(\hat{n}_\alpha)|\Lambda_S\rangle = |S|\,|\Lambda_S\rangle = d_{\mathrm{rev}}|\Lambda_S\rangle \in \{0,\dots,8\}$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}
We establish the three clauses by discrete exterior duality, Euclidean division in $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$, and basis evaluation on $\mathcal{F}$. Clause~(1) follows from discrete adjointness $\langle f, d^* J \rangle = \sum_{e=(u\to v)} (f(v) - f(u)) J(e)$: for $f = \mathbf{1}_\Omega$, the difference $\mathbf{1}_\Omega(v) - \mathbf{1}_\Omega(u)$ vanishes on all edges internal to $\Omega$ or internal to $V \setminus \Omega$, leaving only cut-set edges $\partial\Omega$. In Clause~(2), since $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ is an integral domain and $((Z\oplus\alpha)P)(\alpha) = 0$, uniqueness of Euclidean division gives $D_\alpha(M_\alpha P) = P$, while $M_\alpha(D_\alpha P) = P \oplus P(\alpha)$, so $(D_\alpha M_\alpha \oplus M_\alpha D_\alpha)(P) = P \oplus (P \oplus P(\alpha)) = P(\alpha)$. For $\alpha \neq \beta$, dividing $P(Z) = (Z\oplus\alpha)(Z\oplus\beta)Q(Z) \oplus r_1 Z \oplus r_0$ first by $(Z\oplus\beta)$ yields $(Z\oplus\alpha)Q(Z) \oplus r_1$, and subsequent division by $(Z\oplus\alpha)$ annihilates the degree-0 constant $r_1$ to yield $Q(Z)$ symmetrically, proving $D_\alpha D_\beta = D_\beta D_\alpha$. For Clause~(3), let $S \subseteq \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$. Because $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}$ is a field, $\Lambda_S(\alpha) = \prod_{\gamma \in S}(\alpha \oplus \gamma) = 0 \iff \alpha \in S$. Thus $\hat{a}_\alpha^\dagger |\Lambda_S\rangle = |\Lambda_{S\cup\{\alpha\}}\rangle$ if $\alpha \notin S$ and $0$ if $\alpha \in S$, while for $\alpha \in S$, $D_\alpha(\Lambda_S) = \Lambda_{S\setminus\{\alpha\}}$, so $\hat{a}_\alpha |\Lambda_S\rangle = |\Lambda_{S\setminus\{\alpha\}}\rangle$ if $\alpha \in S$ and $0$ if $\alpha \notin S$. When $\alpha = \beta$, $(\hat{a}_\alpha^\dagger)^2 = (\hat{a}_\alpha)^2 = 0$, and $(\hat{a}_\alpha \hat{a}_\alpha^\dagger \oplus \hat{a}_\alpha^\dagger \hat{a}_\alpha)|\Lambda_S\rangle$ equals $|\Lambda_S\rangle \oplus 0 = |\Lambda_S\rangle$ when $\alpha \notin S$ and $0 \oplus |\Lambda_S\rangle = |\Lambda_S\rangle$ when $\alpha \in S$. When $\alpha \neq \beta$, $\hat{a}_\alpha^\dagger \hat{a}_\beta^\dagger |\Lambda_S\rangle$ and $\hat{a}_\beta^\dagger \hat{a}_\alpha^\dagger |\Lambda_S\rangle$ both vanish unless $\alpha,\beta \notin S$, in which case both equal $|\Lambda_{S\cup\{\alpha,\beta\}}\rangle$, whose sum in $\mathbb{F}_2$ is $|\Lambda_{S\cup\{\alpha,\beta\}}\rangle \oplus |\Lambda_{S\cup\{\alpha,\beta\}}\rangle = 0$; dually, $\{\hat{a}_\alpha, \hat{a}_\beta\}_+ = 0$. Finally, $\hat{a}_\alpha \hat{a}_\beta^\dagger |\Lambda_S\rangle$ and $\hat{a}_\beta^\dagger \hat{a}_\alpha |\Lambda_S\rangle$ both vanish unless $\alpha \in S \wedge \beta \notin S$, in which case $(S \cup \{\beta\}) \setminus \{\alpha\} = (S \setminus \{\alpha\}) \cup \{\beta\}$ so their $\mathbb{F}_2$-sum vanishes identically. Over $\mathbb{C}$, multiplying by $(-1)^{|\{\gamma \in S : \gamma < \alpha\}|}$ flips the relative sign between the two orderings for $\alpha \neq \beta$, replacing $1 \oplus 1 = 0 \pmod 2$ by $1 + (-1) = 0 \in \mathbb{C}$.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Law~II: Bilinear-Orthogonal Projector Split, Heyting Right-Adjoint, and $\mathbb{Z}$-Exact Credit Kirchhoff Law]
\label{thm:law2_hodge_kirchhoff}
For any 64-bit effective epoch mask $P_{\mathrm{eff}} = P_s \wedge P_t \in \{0,1\}^{64}$, define the diagonal matrix operators $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} := \mathrm{diag}(P_{\mathrm{eff}})$ and $\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} := I_{64} - \mathrm{diag}(P_{\mathrm{eff}})$:
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Characteristic-Free Orthogonal Direct-Sum Decomposition:} Over every commutative ring $R$ (simultaneously for $R = \mathbb{F}_2$ and $R = \mathbb{R}$, avoiding characteristic-2 self-orthogonality), $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ and $\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ are mutually annihilating self-adjoint idempotents ($\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}^2 = \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} = \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}^\top$, $\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}^2 = \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} = \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}^\top$, $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} = 0$) satisfying $R^{64} = \ker \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} \oplus \ker \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ and exact bilinear orthogonality
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:law2_bilinear_orth}
\bigl\langle \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r),\; \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r') \bigr\rangle_R \;=\; \sum_{k=0}^{63} r_k r'_k\, P_{\mathrm{eff},k}(1 - P_{\mathrm{eff},k}) \;=\; 0 \qquad \forall r, r' \in R^{64}.
\end{equation}
\item \textbf{Closed Monoidal / Heyting Categorical Adjunction~\cite{heyting1930formalen}:} Viewing $\mathbb{B}^{64}$ as a cartesian-closed thin category under pointwise implication $(A \Rightarrow B)_k := \neg A_k \vee B_k$, forward \texttt{M2S} projection $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ is the exact categorical left adjoint of reverse \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} snoop conditioning $\Gamma_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(Q) := (P_{\mathrm{eff}} \Rightarrow Q)$:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:law2_heyting_adj}
\mathrm{Hom}_{\mathbb{B}^{64}}\bigl(\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r),\; Q\bigr) \;\cong\; \mathrm{Hom}_{\mathbb{B}^{64}}\bigl(r,\; \Gamma_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(Q)\bigr), \qquad \text{i.e.,}\quad \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r) \le Q \iff r \le (P_{\mathrm{eff}} \Rightarrow Q).
\end{equation}
\item \textbf{$\mathbb{Z}$-Exact Kirchhoff Current Conservation:} Let $I_{\mathrm{in}}(t) = \mathrm{b2n}(\mathrm{crd}(t)) \in \{0,1\} \subset \mathbb{Z}$ denote ingress credit current into Stage~4, and let $I_{\mathrm{HBM}}(t) = \mathrm{b2n}(\mathrm{crd}(t)\wedge\neg\mathrm{viol}(t))$ and $I_{\mathrm{scrub}}(t) = \mathrm{b2n}(\mathrm{crd}(t)\wedge\mathrm{viol}(t))$ denote the HBM-commit and scrubbed-return branch currents. Then over the integers $\mathbb{Z}$ (not merely $\bmod\,2$),
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:law2_kirchhoff_z}
I_{\mathrm{in}}(t) - I_{\mathrm{HBM}}(t) - I_{\mathrm{scrub}}(t) \;=\; 0 \qquad \forall t \in \mathbb{N}.
\end{equation}
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}
We verify each identity coordinatewise across $k \in \{0,\dots,63\}$. In Clause~(1), $P_{\mathrm{eff},k} \in \{0,1\} \subset R$ implies $P_{\mathrm{eff},k}^2 = P_{\mathrm{eff},k}$ and $P_{\mathrm{eff},k}(1 - P_{\mathrm{eff},k}) = 0$ in $R$, so $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} + \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} = I_{64}$, $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} = 0$, and every coordinate product vanishes prior to summation. Clause~(2) holds coordinatewise by Boolean residuation (\texttt{thm59}). For Clause~(3), for any $a, b \in \mathbb{B}$, integer lifting satisfies $\mathrm{b2n}(a \oplus b) = \mathrm{b2n}(a) + \mathrm{b2n}(b) - 2\,\mathrm{b2n}(a \wedge b)$; setting $a = \mathrm{crd}\wedge\neg\mathrm{viol}$ and $b = \mathrm{crd}\wedge\mathrm{viol}$ gives $a \wedge b = \mathrm{false}$, so the carry term $-2\,\mathrm{b2n}(\mathrm{false}) = 0$ vanishes in $\mathbb{Z}$ and $\mathrm{b2n}(a) + \mathrm{b2n}(b) = \mathrm{b2n}(a \oplus b) = \mathrm{b2n}(\mathrm{crd})$ (\texttt{thm58}).
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Law~III: Bose--Mesner Spectral Commutativity, Thomson--Kron Dirichlet Minimum, and $\mathcal{C}$-Parity Involution]
\label{thm:law3_bose_kron_cparity}
\label{thm:law3_spectral_dirichlet}
Let $N \in \{0,1\}^{64 \times 72}$ be the point-line incidence matrix of the resolvable affine Steiner plane $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ ($S(2,8,64)$), let $G_{\mathrm{WS}} := N N^\top \in \mathbb{Z}^{64 \times 64}$ be its Gram matrix, and let $L := 64\,I_{64} - J_{64} \in \mathbb{Z}^{64 \times 64}$ be the collinearity graph Laplacian:
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Exact Bose--Mesner Algebra~\cite{bose1959linear}, Minimal Polynomials, \& Zero Heisenberg/von~Neumann Commutator:} Over $\mathbb{Z}^{64 \times 64}$, $G_{\mathrm{WS}}$ and $L$ satisfy the exact operator identities
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:law3_bose_mesner}
G_{\mathrm{WS}} = 8\,I_{64} + J_{64}, \quad G_{\mathrm{WS}} + L = 72\,I_{64}, \quad G_{\mathrm{WS}} L = L G_{\mathrm{WS}} = 8\,L, \quad [G_{\mathrm{WS}}, L] = 0, \quad (G_{\mathrm{WS}}-8I_{64})(G_{\mathrm{WS}}-72I_{64})=0,
\end{equation}
with $L^2 = 64\,L$ and $(G_{\mathrm{WS}} - 8\,I_{64})L = 0$, proving that the 63-dimensional zero-mean switching subspace $\mathbf{1}^\perp$ is a simultaneous eigenspace of $G_{\mathrm{WS}}$ ($\lambda_1 = 8$) and $L$ ($\mu_1 = 64$).
\item \textbf{Thomson's Dirichlet Principle \& Kron Schur-Complement Dissipation:} On any capacitive switching network with symmetric positive-semidefinite conductance/capacitance Laplacian $\tilde{L}_G = \bigl[\begin{smallmatrix} L_{BB} & L_{BI} \\ L_{IB} & L_{II} \end{smallmatrix}\bigr]$ ($L_{II} \succ 0$) partitioned into Stage-1/2 boundary registers $B$ ($\phi_B = \Delta B$) and interior crossbar nodes $I$, the switching quadratic form decomposes identically as
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:law3_kron_schur}
\frac{1}{2}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta B^\top & \phi_I^\top\end{bmatrix} \begin{bmatrix} L_{BB} & L_{BI} \\ L_{IB} & L_{II} \end{bmatrix} \begin{bmatrix}\Delta B \\ \phi_I\end{bmatrix} \;=\; \frac{1}{2}\Delta B^\top S_\Gamma \Delta B \;+\; \frac{1}{2}\bigl(\phi_I + L_{II}^{-1} L_{IB}\Delta B\bigr)^\top L_{II} \bigl(\phi_I + L_{II}^{-1} L_{IB}\Delta B\bigr),
\end{equation}
where $S_\Gamma := L_{BB} - L_{BI} L_{II}^{-1} L_{IB}$ is the Kron Schur-complement (Dirichlet-to-Neumann) operator~\cite{kron1939tensor}; hence harmonic equilibrium achieves $\min_{\phi_I} \mathcal{E}(\Delta B, \phi_I) = \frac{1}{2}\Delta B^\top S_\Gamma \Delta B$, which vanishes identically ($\mathcal{E} = 0$) whenever Stage~2 clamps $\Delta B = \mathbf{0}$.
\item \textbf{Exact Fractured CMOS Charge-Conjugation ($\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{LUT6\_2}} = \mathcal{C}_5 \oplus \mathcal{C}_5$) LUT Involution:} For an unfractured $k$-input SRAM lookup mask $M \in \mathbb{F}_2^{2^k}$, $\mathcal{C}_k(M)_\alpha := 1 \oplus M_{(2^k-1)\oplus\alpha}$ satisfies $\mathcal{C}_k^2 = \mathrm{Id}$ and $\mathrm{LUT}_{\mathcal{C}_k(M)}(\neg x) = \neg\,\mathrm{LUT}_M(x)$. On a physical AMD UG574 fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} with $\texttt{I5} = 1$ fixed to separate the 32-bit \texttt{O5} ($\alpha \in \{0,\dots,31\}$) and 32-bit \texttt{O6} ($\alpha \in \{32,\dots,63\}$) halves, active-low De~Morgan complementation of the 5 shared signal inputs $x \in \mathbb{B}^5$ acts via the intra-half involution $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{LUT6\_2}}(M)_\alpha := (\mathcal{C}_5 \oplus \mathcal{C}_5)(M)_\alpha = 1 \oplus M_{\alpha \oplus 31}$ ($\alpha \in \{0,\dots,63\}$, distinct from unfractured $63 - \alpha$), mapping $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{LUT6\_2}}(\texttt{64'h2A2A2A2A\_80808080}) = \texttt{64'hABABABAB\_FEFEFEFE}$ (\texttt{thm37}, \texttt{thm67}).
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}
We establish the three clauses by affine incidence counting, block-LDU completion of squares, and intra-half Boolean address complementation. In $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$, every point lies on 9 lines (one per slope in $\mathbb{F}_8 \cup \{\infty\}$) and any two distinct points determine a unique line (\texttt{thm17}), so $(N N^\top)_{x,x} = 9$ and $(N N^\top)_{x,y} = 1$ ($x \neq y$), giving $G_{\mathrm{WS}} = 8\,I_{64} + J_{64}$ and $G_{\mathrm{WS}} + L = 72\,I_{64}$. Using $J_{64}^2 = 64\,J_{64}$, both $(8\,I_{64} + J_{64})(64\,I_{64} - J_{64})$ and $(64\,I_{64} - J_{64})(8\,I_{64} + J_{64})$ expand to $512\,I_{64} + 56\,J_{64} - 64\,J_{64} = 512\,I_{64} - 8\,J_{64} = 8\,L$, proving $[G_{\mathrm{WS}}, L] = 0$, $(G_{\mathrm{WS}}-8I_{64})L = J_{64}(64I_{64}-J_{64}) = 0$, and $(G_{\mathrm{WS}}-8I_{64})(G_{\mathrm{WS}}-72I_{64}) = J_{64}(J_{64}-64I_{64}) = 0$ (\texttt{thm41}). Clause~(2) follows immediately by expanding the right-hand side of Eq.~\eqref{eq:law3_kron_schur} using $L_{IB}^\top = L_{BI}$ and $L_{II}^\top = L_{II}$. For Clause~(3), holding $\texttt{I5} = 1$ fixed ($\alpha_5 \in \{0,1\}$ selecting \texttt{O5} vs.\ \texttt{O6}) while negating the 5 shared address bits $x_0,\dots,x_4$ maps $\alpha = 32\alpha_5 + \sum_{j=0}^4 x_j 2^j$ to $32\alpha_5 + \sum_{j=0}^4(1-x_j)2^j = \alpha \oplus 31$, so $\mathrm{LUT}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{LUT6\_2}}(M)}(\neg x) = 1 \oplus M_{(\alpha\oplus 31)\oplus 31} = \neg\,\mathrm{LUT}_M(x)$; on each repeated 8-bit sub-mask ($\alpha \mapsto \alpha \oplus 7$), reversing and bitwise-inverting \texttt{8'h80 = 8'b1000\_0000} yields \texttt{\~{}8'b0000\_0001 = 8'hFE} and reversing and bitwise-inverting \texttt{8'h2A = 8'b0010\_1010} yields \texttt{\~{}8'b0101\_0100 = 8'hAB} (\texttt{thm37}).
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Law~IV: Non-Abelian Maximal-Tree Wilson-Loop Flatness and CXL First-Cohomology Isomorphism]
\label{thm:law4_wilson_cohomology}
\label{thm:law4_gauge_cohomology}
Let $G = (V, E)$ be a connected CXL switch graph with spanning tree $T \subset E$ rooted at $r_0 \in V$ and $\beta_1(G) = |E| - |V| + 1$ fundamental chords $E \setminus T$:
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Non-Abelian Maximal-Tree Gauge Fixing \& Path-Independence Equivalence:} Let $\mathcal{G}$ be any group ($\mathcal{G} = S_{64}$ on capability lanes or $\mathcal{G} = \mathrm{Aff}(\mathbb{F}_{2^8})$ on hop history tags), and let $\mathcal{U} = \{\pi_e \in \mathcal{G}\}_{e \in E}$ ($\pi_{v\to u} = \pi_{u\to v}^{-1}$) be a discrete $\mathcal{G}$-connection. Define the root-to-vertex tree transport $\Pi_v := P_{\gamma_T(r_0 \leadsto v)}(\mathcal{U}) \in \mathcal{G}$ and apply the vertex gauge transformation $g(v) := \Pi_v^{-1}$, $\pi_{u\to v}^{(g)} := \Pi_v^{-1} \circ \pi_{u\to v} \circ \Pi_u$. Then:
\begin{enumerate}
\item $\pi_e^{(g)} = \mathrm{Id}_{\mathcal{G}}$ identically for every tree edge $e \in T$;
\item for every off-tree chord $e^* = (u \to v) \in E \setminus T$, $\pi_{e^*}^{(g)} = \Pi_v^{-1} \circ \pi_{e^*} \circ \Pi_u$ equals the rooted Wilson-loop holonomy~\cite{wilson1974confinement, kogut1975hamiltonian} $H_{C(e^*)}(\mathcal{U})$ around the fundamental cycle $C(e^*)$;
\item parallel transport $P_\gamma(\mathcal{U})$ is path-independent across all coterminal walks $\gamma_1, \gamma_2 : x \leadsto y$ in $G$ \textbf{if and only if} $H_{C(e^*)}(\mathcal{U}) = \mathrm{Id}_{\mathcal{G}}$ for all $\beta_1(G)$ chords $e^* \in E \setminus T$; and
\item programming each chord's coarse-aperture compensation permutation to $\widetilde{\pi}_{e^*} := \pi_{e^*}^{-1} \circ \Pi_v \circ \Pi_u^{-1}$ (\texttt{thm34}) enforces exact zero curvature:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:law4_flat_holonomy}
H_{C(e^*)}^{\mathrm{flat}} \;=\; \Pi_v^{-1} \circ \bigl(\pi_{e^*} \circ \widetilde{\pi}_{e^*}\bigr) \circ \Pi_u \;=\; \Pi_v^{-1} \circ \pi_{e^*} \circ \pi_{e^*}^{-1} \circ \Pi_v \circ \Pi_u^{-1} \circ \Pi_u \;=\; \mathrm{Id}_{\mathcal{G}}.
\end{equation}
\end{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Exact First-Cohomology Isomorphism of the 256-Byte CXL Split-Coset Complex:} Consider the 3-term $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}$-cochain complex of a 256-byte CXL~3.0/4.0 flit (242 message bytes, 8 Flit-CRC bytes, 6 RS-FEC parity bytes),
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:cxl_cochain_256}
0 \longrightarrow C^0\bigl(\mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{242}\bigr) \xrightarrow{\;\;d^0 = E_{\mathrm{fold}}\;\;} C^1\bigl(\mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{256}\bigr) \xrightarrow{\;\;d^1 = H_{\mathrm{FEC}}\;\;} C^2\bigl(\mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{6}\bigr) \longrightarrow 0,
\end{equation}
where $E_{\mathrm{fold}}(\delta) := (\delta, G_{\mathrm{CRC}}(\delta), G_{\mathrm{FEC}}(\delta, G_{\mathrm{CRC}}(\delta)))$ and $H_{\mathrm{FEC}}(\delta, c, p) := G_{\mathrm{FEC}}(\delta, c) \oplus p$. Then $d^1 \circ d^0 = 0$, $H^0(\mathcal{C}^\bullet) = 0$, $H^2(\mathcal{C}^\bullet) = 0$, and the linear CRC syndrome map $S_{\mathrm{CRC}}(\delta, c, p) := G_{\mathrm{CRC}}(\delta) \oplus c$ induces an exact isomorphism of $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}$-vector spaces
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:h1_isomorphism}
H^1(\mathcal{C}^\bullet) \;:=\; \frac{\ker\bigl(H_{\mathrm{FEC}} : \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{256} \to \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{6}\bigr)}{\mathrm{im}\bigl(E_{\mathrm{fold}} : \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{242} \to \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{256}\bigr)} \;\xrightarrow[\;\;\cong\;\;]{S_{\mathrm{CRC}}}\; \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{8},
\end{equation}
with Euler--Poincar\'{e} characteristic $\chi(\mathcal{C}^\bullet) = 242 - 256 + 6 = -8 = -\dim_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}} H^1(\mathcal{C}^\bullet)$. Thus the 6-byte (\texttt{CRC48}) tunneled cohort capabilities $\tau \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^6 \hookrightarrow \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^8$ are canonical representatives of non-trivial cohomology classes in $H^1(\mathcal{C}^\bullet)$: invisible to link FEC check $H_{\mathrm{FEC}}$, invariant under Stage-2 surgical payload edits $E_{\mathrm{fold}}(\delta)$, and losslessly recovered by $S_{\mathrm{CRC}}$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}
We prove Clause~(1) via the fundamental group $\pi_1(G,r_0)$ and Clause~(2) via the First Isomorphism Theorem for linear cochain complexes. For Clause~(1), if $e = (u \to v) \in T$, the unique tree path to $v$ extends that to $u$ by $e$ (or deletes $e^{-1}$), so $\Pi_v = \pi_e \circ \Pi_u$, whence $\Pi_v^{-1} \circ \pi_e \circ \Pi_u = \mathrm{Id}_{\mathcal{G}}$. For $e^* = (u \to v) \notin T$, the closed walk $C(e^*)$ traverses $r_0 \leadsto u$ (transport $\Pi_u$), crosses $e^*$ (transport $\pi_{e^*}$), and returns $v \leadsto r_0$ (transport $\Pi_v^{-1}$), giving $H_{C(e^*)}(\mathcal{U}) = \Pi_v^{-1} \circ \pi_{e^*} \circ \Pi_u = \pi_{e^*}^{(g)}$. Because $\pi_1(G, r_0)$ is freely generated by $\{[C(e^*)] : e^* \in E \setminus T\}$ and $\mathrm{Hol}_{\mathcal{U}} : \pi_1(G, r_0) \to \mathcal{G}$ is a group homomorphism, $\mathrm{Hol}_{\mathcal{U}} \equiv \mathrm{Id}_{\mathcal{G}}$ holds iff every generator maps to $\mathrm{Id}_{\mathcal{G}}$; coterminal walks $\gamma_1, \gamma_2 : x \leadsto y$ satisfy $P_{\gamma_1} = P_{\gamma_2} \iff P_{\gamma_2^{-1}\cdot\gamma_1} = \mathrm{Id}_{\mathcal{G}}$, proving (c), and direct associative cancellation proves (d) (\texttt{thm34}). For Clause~(2), $E_{\mathrm{fold}}$ is injective ($\ker d^0 = 0$) because its first 242 coordinates are the identity on $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{242}$, and $H_{\mathrm{FEC}}$ is surjective ($\mathrm{im}\,d^1 = \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^6$) because $H_{\mathrm{FEC}}(\mathbf{0}_{242}, \mathbf{0}_8, p) = p$ for all $p \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^6$. Moreover, $H_{\mathrm{FEC}}(E_{\mathrm{fold}}(\delta)) = G_{\mathrm{FEC}}(\delta, G_{\mathrm{CRC}}(\delta)) \oplus G_{\mathrm{FEC}}(\delta, G_{\mathrm{CRC}}(\delta)) = \mathbf{0}_6$. Restricting $S_{\mathrm{CRC}}$ to $\ker H_{\mathrm{FEC}} = \{(\delta, c, G_{\mathrm{FEC}}(\delta, c)) : \delta \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{242},\, c \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^8\}$: (i)~for any $\tau \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^8$, the lift $\tilde{\sigma}(\tau) := (\mathbf{0}_{242}, \tau, G_{\mathrm{FEC}}(\mathbf{0}_{242}, \tau))$ lies in $\ker H_{\mathrm{FEC}}$ and satisfies $S_{\mathrm{CRC}}(\tilde{\sigma}(\tau)) = G_{\mathrm{CRC}}(\mathbf{0}_{242}) \oplus \tau = \tau$ (surjectivity); and (ii)~an element $(\delta, c, G_{\mathrm{FEC}}(\delta, c)) \in \ker H_{\mathrm{FEC}}$ satisfies $G_{\mathrm{CRC}}(\delta) \oplus c = \mathbf{0}_8 \iff c = G_{\mathrm{CRC}}(\delta) \iff (\delta, c, G_{\mathrm{FEC}}(\delta, c)) = E_{\mathrm{fold}}(\delta)$, so $\ker(S_{\mathrm{CRC}}|_{\ker H_{\mathrm{FEC}}}) = \mathrm{im}\,E_{\mathrm{fold}}$, yielding Eq.~\eqref{eq:h1_isomorphism}.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Twelve Cross-Domain Mathematical-Physics Equalities on the Frozen 1,585-LUT Netlist (\texttt{thm62}--\texttt{thm73})]
\label{cor:six_cross_domain_physics}
The four governing field equations of Theorems~\ref{thm:law1_gauss_car}--\ref{thm:law4_wilson_cohomology} (with Kirchhoff credit conservation~\cite{kirchhoff1847ueber}) induce twelve additional cross-domain mathematical-physics equalities on the frozen 1,585-LUT Stage-1/2 core and outer wrapper at $+0$ added logic gates (all twelve machine-checked with \texttt{0 sorry} and \texttt{0} axioms in \texttt{formal\_lean4/CxlSubflitPhysicsCorollaries.lean}):
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=1.4em,label=\textup{(\roman*)},topsep=2pt,itemsep=1.5pt]
  \item \textbf{Atiyah--Singer / Hodge--Dirac Index on the 256-Byte CXL Flit ($\mathrm{Ind}(D_{\mathrm{flit}}) = -8 = \chi(C^\bullet)$, \texttt{thm62}):} To avoid characteristic-2 self-orthogonality of the naive matrix transpose $(d^1)^\top$, equip the 3-term CXL cochain complex of Theorem~\ref{thm:law4_wilson_cohomology}(2) with its canonical characteristic-free split-exact algebraic sections $(d^1)^* := \sigma_{\mathrm{split}} : C^2 \to C^1$, $\sigma_{\mathrm{split}}(p) = (\mathbf{0}_{242}, \mathbf{0}_8, p)$ ($d^1 \circ \sigma_{\mathrm{split}} = \mathrm{Id}_{C^2}$), and $(d^0)^* := \pi_{\mathrm{msg}} : C^1 \to C^0$, $\pi_{\mathrm{msg}}(\delta,c,p) = \delta$ ($\pi_{\mathrm{msg}} \circ d^0 = \mathrm{Id}_{C^0}$). Assembling the graded Dirac--K\"{a}hler operator~\cite{atiyah1968index, kahler1962innerer} $D_{\mathrm{flit}} := \begin{bmatrix} d^0 & \sigma_{\mathrm{split}} \end{bmatrix} : C^0 \oplus C^2 \bigl(\mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{248}\bigr) \to C^1\bigl(\mathbb{F}_{2^8}^{256}\bigr)$ with split-exact partner $D_{\mathrm{flit}}^* := \begin{bmatrix} \pi_{\mathrm{msg}}\bigl(\mathrm{Id}_{C^1} - \sigma_{\mathrm{split}} d^1\bigr) \\ d^1 \end{bmatrix} : C^1 \to C^0 \oplus C^2$ yields $\ker D_{\mathrm{flit}} = H^0(C^\bullet) \oplus H^2(C^\bullet) = \{0\}$ and $\ker D_{\mathrm{flit}}^* = \{(\mathbf{0}_{242}, \tau, G_{\mathrm{FEC}}(\mathbf{0}_{242},\tau)) : \tau \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^8\} \cong H^1(C^\bullet) \cong \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^8$, so the analytical Dirac index equals the Euler--Poincar\'{e} characteristic:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:dirac_flit_index}
  \mathrm{Ind}(D_{\mathrm{flit}}) := \dim_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}\ker D_{\mathrm{flit}} - \dim_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}\ker D_{\mathrm{flit}}^* = 0 - 8 = -8 = \chi(C^\bullet),
  \end{equation}
  identifying the 8 Flit-CRC bytes (carrying the 6 \texttt{CRC48}/\texttt{CRC64} capability coset tags $\tau \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^6 \hookrightarrow \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^8$) as topologically protected chiral zero-modes of the CXL frame.
  \item \textbf{Vanishing Robertson--Schr\"{o}dinger Quantum Uncertainty on $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ ($\sigma_{G_{\mathrm{WS}}}\,\sigma_L \equiv 0$ on $\mathbf{1}^\perp$, \texttt{thm63}):} For any normalized switching wavepacket $\psi \in \mathbb{R}^{64}$ ($\|\psi\|_2 = 1$) with operator variance $\sigma_A^2(\psi) := \langle \psi, A^2\psi\rangle - \langle\psi, A\psi\rangle^2$, the Robertson--Schr\"{o}dinger commutator lower bound~\cite{robertson1929uncertainty, schrodinger1930heisenbergschen} of Theorem~\ref{thm:law3_bose_kron_cparity}(1) satisfies
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:robertson_schrodinger_ag28}
  \sigma_{G_{\mathrm{WS}}}(\psi)\,\sigma_L(\psi) \;\ge\; \frac{1}{2}\bigl|\langle \psi, [G_{\mathrm{WS}}, L]\psi\rangle\bigr| \equiv 0, \qquad \text{with}\quad \sigma_{G_{\mathrm{WS}}}(\psi) = \sigma_L(\psi) \equiv 0 \;\;\;\forall \psi \in \mathbf{1}^\perp,
  \end{equation}
  proving zero quantum/spectral uncertainty trade-off between minimum cohort aliasing ($G_{\mathrm{WS}}$) and minimum capacitive crossbar switching dissipation ($L$) across the entire 63-dimensional zero-mean switching subspace $\mathbf{1}^\perp$ (\texttt{thm41}, \texttt{thm63}).
  \item \textbf{Discrete Holographic Bulk--Boundary (GKPW) Partition-Function Duality (\texttt{thm64}):} Integrating out the interior switch-fabric potentials $\phi_I \in \mathbb{R}^{|I|}$ in the Gaussian capacitive Dirichlet partition function~\cite{gubser1998gauge, witten1998anti} of Theorem~\ref{thm:law3_bose_kron_cparity}(2) collapses the bulk path integral identically onto the Stage-1/2 boundary Schur-complement action $S_{\mathrm{bdy}}[\Delta B] = \frac{1}{2}\Delta B^\top S_\Gamma \Delta B$:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:gkpw_holographic_schur}
  Z_{\mathrm{bulk}}[\Delta B] := \int_{\mathbb{R}^{|I|}} \exp\bigl(-\mathcal{E}(\Delta B, \phi_I)\bigr)\,d\phi_I = (2\pi)^{|I|/2}(\det L_{II})^{-1/2}\exp\Bigl(-\frac{1}{2}\Delta B^\top S_\Gamma \Delta B\Bigr),
  \end{equation}
  so clamping the boundary source $\Delta B \leftarrow 0$ at the Stage-2 ingress \texttt{LUT6\_2} forces the harmonic bulk field $\phi_I^{\mathrm{harm}} = -L_{II}^{-1}L_{IB}\Delta B \equiv 0$ everywhere in the fabric interior.
  \item \textbf{$\mathcal{N}=2$ Supersymmetric Witten Index on the \texttt{SVA 1\textquotesingle}-Gated Vieta--B\'{e}zout Fock Space (\texttt{thm65}):} For any mode-weight vector $v \in \mathbb{F}_2^{255}$, the linear supercharges $Q_v^\dagger := \sum_{\alpha \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times} v_\alpha \hat{a}_\alpha^\dagger$ (Vieta root insertion) and $Q_v := \sum_{\alpha \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times} v_\alpha \hat{a}_\alpha$ (shadow B\'{e}zout deflation) on the Fock space $\mathcal{F}$ of Theorem~\ref{thm:law1_gauss_car}(3) are nilpotent ($Q_v^2 = (Q_v^\dagger)^2 = 0$) and satisfy the $\mathcal{N}=2$ supersymmetry algebra $\{Q_v, Q_v^\dagger\}_+ = (\mathrm{wt}(v)\bmod 2)\,\mathrm{Id}_{\mathcal{F}}$ with vanishing Witten index~\cite{witten1982constraints} over $\mathbb{Z}$:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:witten_index_vieta}
  \Delta_W(\mathcal{F}) := \mathrm{Tr}_{\mathcal{F}}\bigl((-1)^{\hat{N}_{\mathrm{rev}}}\bigr) = \sum_{d=0}^{255}(-1)^d\binom{255}{d} = (1 - 1)^{255} = 0.
  \end{equation}
  \item \textbf{Lattice Yang--Mills Plaquette Action \& Aharonov--Bohm Wilson-Loop Annihilation (\texttt{thm66}):} Representing each $S_{64}$ switch permutation $\pi_e$ by its $64\times 64$ orthogonal permutation matrix $P(\pi_e) \in \mathrm{O}(64)$, the discrete lattice Yang--Mills plaquette action~\cite{wilson1974confinement, kogut1975hamiltonian} across the $\beta_1(G)$ fundamental cycles $C(e^*)$ satisfies
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:yang_mills_plaquette}
  S_{\mathrm{YM}}[\mathcal{U}] := \sum_{e^* \in E\setminus T}\Bigl(64 - \mathrm{Tr}\bigl(P(H_{C(e^*)}(\mathcal{U}))\bigr)\Bigr) \ge 0, \qquad S_{\mathrm{YM}}\bigl[\mathcal{U}^{\mathrm{flat}}\bigr] \equiv 0 \iff H_{C(e^*)}^{\mathrm{flat}} = \mathrm{Id}_{S_{64}}\;\;\forall e^*\in E\setminus T,
  \end{equation}
  so \texttt{spectralc}'s chord compensation $\widetilde{\pi}_{e^*} = \pi_{e^*}^{-1}\circ\Pi_v\circ\Pi_u^{-1}$ (\texttt{thm34}) annihilates Aharonov--Bohm Confused-Deputy holonomy flux~\cite{aharonov1959significance} to the exact vacuum minimum $S_{\mathrm{YM}} = 0$.
  \item \textbf{Discrete $\mathcal{CPT}$ Involution \& Jarzynski Nonequilibrium Queue Fluctuation Identity (\texttt{thm67}):} Composing fractured \texttt{LUT6\_2} Charge-Conjugation $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{LUT6\_2}}(M)_\alpha = 1 \oplus M_{\alpha\oplus 31}$ (\texttt{thm37}), Normal-Basis Spatial Wire Parity $\mathcal{P}(u) = \mathrm{RotL}_4(u)$ (\texttt{thm35}, \texttt{thm40}), and Feistel/B\'{e}zout Time-Reversal $\mathcal{T} = \mathrm{Dec}^{(6)} \circ \mathrm{Enc}^{(6)}$ (\texttt{thm30}) yields an exact commuting involution $(\mathcal{C}\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T})^2 = \mathrm{Id}$~\cite{luders1954proof}, while the Poloczek--Ciucu queue supermartingale $Z_t = h_{\theta^*}(J_t)e^{\theta^*(A(t)-S(t))}$ of Theorem~\ref{thm:martingale}(i) at the exact Cram\'{e}r--Lundberg root $\rho(M_{\theta^*})\mathbb{E}[e^{-\theta^* S_1}] = 1$ with positive Perron--Frobenius right eigenvector $M_{\theta^*}h_{\theta^*} = \rho(M_{\theta^*})h_{\theta^*}$ satisfies the discrete Jarzynski nonequilibrium work identity~\cite{jarzynski1997nonequilibrium} $\mathbb{E}\bigl[Z_t / Z_0 \mid J_0\bigr] = \langle e^{-\theta^* \Delta W_{\mathrm{net}}(t)}\rangle = 1$ ($\forall t \in \mathbb{N}$).
  \item \textbf{Landauer--Shannon Multi-Resolution Wavelet Filtration \& Exact Bit-Erasure Law (\texttt{thm68}):} The four Stage-2 \texttt{LUT6\_2} BF16 precision-tier projectors $P_u$ ($u \in \{0,9,12,16\}$, \texttt{thm27}, \texttt{thm44}) form a commuting renormalization-group filtration $P_{u_1}P_{u_2} = P_{\min(u_1,u_2)}$ decomposing $I_{16} = P_9 \oplus (P_{12}-P_9) \oplus (P_{16}-P_{12})$ into orthogonal sign/exponent ($9\text{b}$), mid-mantissa ($3\text{b}$), and fine-mantissa ($4\text{b}$) shells ($9+3+4=16$), erasing exactly $16 - u(c_i,\mathrm{viol}_i) \in \{16,7,4,0\}$ bits per lane ($\Delta Q_{\mathrm{Landauer}} = (16-u)k_B T\ln 2$~\cite{1}, and $0.5\text{~bits}$ per bit-pair in the \texttt{thm36} popcount half-adder).
  \item \textbf{Kramers--Wannier Potts Partition-Function Self-Duality \& Zero Singleton Genus (\texttt{thm69}):} Under the MacWilliams--Potts duality map~\cite{macwilliams1977theorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheory, kramers1941statistics} at $q=256$, the self-dual $[4,2,3]_{\mathbb{F}_{256}}$ Cauchy RS CSS weight enumerator $W_{C_{\mathrm{CSS}}}(X,Y) = X^4 + 1{,}020\,XY^3 + 64{,}515\,Y^4$ (\texttt{thm52}, \texttt{thm61}) sits at the exact Kramers--Wannier self-dual critical fixed point $W_{C_{\mathrm{CSS}}}(X+255Y, X-Y) = 256^2 W_{C_{\mathrm{CSS}}}(X,Y)$ with vanishing Singleton genus $\delta_{\mathrm{MDS}} := n + 1 - k - d_{\min} = 0$ across $[4,2,3]$, $[8,4,5]$, and $[6,2,5]$.
  \item \textbf{Ramanujan Expander \& Hasse--Weil Riemann-Hypothesis Saturation on $\mathrm{PG}(2,8)$ and $\mathcal{H}_3/\mathcal{H}_4$ (\texttt{thm70}):} The Singer projective completion $\mathrm{PG}(2,8)$ ($73$ points, $1+7\times 73=512$, \texttt{thm46}--\texttt{thm49}) satisfies $N_{\mathrm{PG}}N_{\mathrm{PG}}^\top = 8I_{73}+J_{73}$, so its 9-regular bipartite point-line Levi graph has non-trivial eigenvalue $|\lambda_2| = \sqrt{8} < 2\sqrt{8}$, saturating the Lubotzky--Phillips--Sarnak / Ihara-Zeta Ramanujan expander bound~\cite{lubotzky1988ramanujan}, while the bus-widening Hermitian curves $\mathcal{H}_3$ ($28\text{~pts}, g=3$) and $\mathcal{H}_4$ ($65\text{~pts}, g=6$) of Proposition~\ref{prop:hermitian_ag} saturate the Hasse--Weil Riemann-Hypothesis bound $q^2 + 1 + 2g q = q^3 + 1$~\cite{weil1948courbes} with zero defect.
  \item \textbf{Second-Law Free-Energy Dissipation of Karamata Robin-Hood Equilibration (\texttt{thm71}):} Defining the 2-body intra-slope collision free energy $F(n) := \sum_{b=0}^8 \binom{n_b}{2}$ across the 9 parallel slopes of $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:karamata_waterfilling}), the discrete chemical potential $\mu(n_b) := \Delta\binom{n_b}{2} = n_b$ has constant unit stiffness $\Delta\mu(n_b) = \Delta^2\binom{n_b}{2} \equiv 1$ (\texttt{thm54}), and every Robin-Hood transfer from $n_{\mathrm{hi}} = \mathrm{lo}+\mathrm{gap}+1$ to $n_{\mathrm{lo}} = \mathrm{lo}$ dissipates an exact integer free-energy quantum $\Delta F_{\mathrm{RH}} = \mathrm{gap} + 1 \in \mathbb{Z}_{\ge 1}$ (\texttt{thm55}).
  \item \textbf{Dirac--Frobenius Local 3-Tap Square-Root of Spatial Translation in $\mathbb{F}_2[C_{64}]$ (\texttt{thm72}):} Analogous to Dirac's square root of the translation/Laplacian operator~\cite{dirac1928quantum}, the 3-tap group-ring operator $\mathcal{L}_a = I_{64} \oplus T_a \oplus T_{32}$ over $\mathbb{F}_2[C_{64}]$ (\texttt{thm10}, \texttt{thm34}) satisfies $\mathcal{L}_a^2 = T_{2a} = \mathrm{RotL}_{2a}$, $\mathcal{L}_a^{64} = I_{64}$, and nilpotent defect $(\mathcal{L}_a \oplus I_{64})^{64} = 0$.
  \item \textbf{Discrete Liouville Symplectic Phase-Space Volume Invariance (\texttt{thm73}):} The 6-round balanced Feistel unsealer (\texttt{thm11}, \texttt{thm30}) is a composition of unit-Jacobian symplectic shears $(L,R)\mapsto(R,L\oplus f(R))$ ($\det J \equiv 1$~\cite{liouville1838note}), which together with Normal-Basis Frobenius rotation ($w_H(\mathrm{RotL}_k(u)) = w_H(u)$, \texttt{thm40}, \texttt{thm44}), modulo-$2^k$ ripple-carry telescoping (\texttt{thm15}), and coset translation (\texttt{thm61}) preserves phase-space measure ($\Delta H = 0$) along every authorized datapath trajectory.
\end{enumerate}
\end{corollary}
\begin{proof}
Clauses~(i)--(xii) follow from Theorems~\ref{thm:law1_gauss_car}--\ref{thm:law4_wilson_cohomology} and are machine-checked in pure \texttt{Init.Prelude} term mode in \texttt{formal\_lean4/CxlSubflitPhysicsCorollaries.lean} (\texttt{thm62}--\texttt{thm73}, Table~\ref{tab:fastcore_and_physics_lean4}).
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[The Noether--Landauer--Holographic Synthesis: Why Five Branches of Mathematics Collapse at the $q=8$ Single-Gate Horizon]
\label{rem:noether_parent_synthesis}
We do not claim to have discovered twenty-four separate laws of nature inside a memory controller. Rather, forcing multi-tenant memory isolation down to the zero-slack physical boundary---$64\text{~bits}$ of active state, $0\text{~BRAM}$, a single \StageOneSieveDelayNs\ \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} logic level, $0$ dropped link credits, and $0$ false-positive aliasing up to $8$ simultaneous revocations---leaves \emph{zero mathematical degrees of freedom}. At that boundary, combinatorial design theory, commutative algebra, cochain cohomology, network thermodynamics, and discrete horizon mechanics collapse onto the exact same finite operator across six structural correspondences:
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=1.4em,label=\textup{(\roman*)},topsep=2.5pt,itemsep=2pt]
  \item[\textup{(0)}] \textbf{The $q = 8$ Diophantine Architectural Fixed Point \& G\"{o}del--Turing--Rice Decidability Split:} Every architectural constant in \textsc{Kernel Zero} (\texttt{8}, \texttt{64}, \texttt{72}, \texttt{73}, \texttt{256}, \texttt{512}) is locked simultaneously by the unique non-trivial power-of-two solution $q = m = 8$ to the three-way Diophantine system
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:diophantine_q8}
  q^2 \;=\; q \cdot m \;=\; 64\text{~bits}, \qquad
  1 + (q - 1)\bigl(q^2 + q + 1\bigr) \;=\; q^3 \;=\; 512\text{~bits}, \qquad
  4\,q^2 \;=\; 2^q \;=\; 256\text{~bytes},
  \end{equation}

  \item \textbf{Discrete Noether's Theorem on Silicon~\cite{noether1918invariante}:} Each of the four hardware conservation laws proved in Sections~\ref{sec:multiscale}--\ref{sec:formal} is the exact Noether conserved current of an explicit symmetry generator of the interposer action:

  \item \textbf{The ``Dual Landauer'' Unitary \texttt{LUT6\_2} $S$-Matrix Sum Rule~\cite{1, landauer1957spatial} \& Lloyd--Margolus--Levitin Action Floor~\cite{margolus1998maximum}:} Writing $T := \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ (pin~\texttt{O5}) and $R := \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} = I_{64} - \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ (pin~\texttt{O6}), the 2-port Stage-1 \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} scattering matrix $S(P_{\mathrm{eff}}) := \bigl[\begin{smallmatrix} R & T \\ T & R \end{smallmatrix}\bigr] \in R^{128 \times 128}$ is self-adjoint, involutive, and unitary ($S^\dagger = S = S^{-1}$, $S^2 = I_{128}$). Its trace partition of unity $\mathrm{Tr}(T^\dagger T) + \mathrm{Tr}(R^\dagger R) = \mathrm{wt}_H(P_{\mathrm{eff}}) + \bigl(64 - \mathrm{wt}_H(P_{\mathrm{eff}})\bigr) \equiv 64$ unites Rolf Landauer's 1957 ballistic quantum conductance formula~\cite{landauer1957spatial} on pin~\texttt{O5} ($\frac{h}{e^2}G_{\mathrm{LB}} = \mathrm{Tr}(T^\dagger T)$) with Landauer's 1961 irreversible bit-erasure heat floor~\cite{1} on pin~\texttt{O6} ($\frac{\Delta Q_{\mathrm{erase}}}{k_B T \ln 2} = \mathrm{Tr}(R^\dagger R)$) inside a single logic cell, while multiplying gate delay $t_{\mathrm{sieve}} = \StageOneSieveDelayNs$ by $\Delta Q_{\mathrm{erase}}$ respects the Margolus--Levitin / Lloyd quantum orthogonalization action floor~\cite{margolus1998maximum} $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{gate}} = t_{\mathrm{sieve}}\cdot\Delta Q_{\mathrm{erase}} \ge N_{\mathrm{erased}}\frac{\pi\hbar}{2}$.

To unauthorized waves ($\Delta B \leftarrow \mathbf{0} \implies \phi_I^{\mathrm{harm}} \equiv \mathbf{0}$, \texttt{SVA~9}), $\partial\Omega$ acts as an impenetrable \emph{white-hole exclusion horizon} that reflects scrubbed completions outward at $\Delta t_{\mathrm{CRD}} \equiv 0$ (\texttt{thm08}, \texttt{thm58}); for transient revocations, dual-bank \texttt{CSR 0x60\_bank[0:1]} executes a \emph{discrete Page-curve turnover and unitary black-to-white-hole horizon bounce}~\cite{page1993information} at Bellman degree $d^* = 6 < 8$---opening the two-sided $\texttt{bank\_valid} = \texttt{2'b11}$ throat, radiating 64 tag-exact \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} probes outward via the 0-BRAM 6-round Feistel walker (\texttt{thm11}, \texttt{thm30}), and applying time-reversed $\mathcal{T}$ B\'{e}zout--Horner annihilation so horizon entropy follows a bounded sawtooth $0 \le S_{\mathrm{horizon}}(t) \le 48\text{~bits} < 64\text{~bits}$ for all $t \to \infty$ (\texttt{thm31}, \texttt{thm57}). Simultaneously, $S_\Gamma$ serves as the \emph{Li--Haldane entanglement Hamiltonian}~\cite{ryu2006holographic} $H_{\mathrm{ent}} = \frac{\beta}{2}\Delta B^\top S_\Gamma \Delta B$ enforcing the Ryu--Takayanagi boundary area law $I(X_\Omega; X_{V\setminus\Omega}) \le |\partial\Omega| = 64\text{~bits} \perp M \to \infty$, the \emph{Kadanoff--Wilson spatial block-spin RG fixed point} $\mathcal{R}_{I_2}\circ\mathcal{R}_{I_1}(\tilde{L}_G) = S_\Gamma$, and the \emph{Callen--Welton / Nyquist--Johnson fluctuation--dissipation operator}~\cite{callen1951irreversibility} ($\mathrm{Cov}_\beta(J_B) = k_B T\,S_\Gamma$, Onsager symmetry $S_\Gamma^\top = S_\Gamma$), while $\det(L_{II}) = \sum_{F \in \mathcal{T}_B(G)} \prod_{e\in F} c_e$ counts the boundary-rooted spanning forests of Law~IV and $(-L_{II}^{-1}L_{IB})_{i,b} = \mathbb{P}_i(\tau_B = \tau_b)$ is the Kakutani--Doob random-walk hitting probability~\cite{kakutani1944markov} on $\partial\Omega$.

\subsection{Empirical Multi-Track Synthesis and Exascale Validation Roadmap}
\label{sec:empirical_synthesis}
\label{sec:roadmap}
Across all four empirical reduction-to-practice tracks (Table~\ref{tab:provenance} and Figures~\ref{fig:vieta_scaling}--\ref{fig:k8s_tco}), the frozen architecture is closed and attested in \textsc{Kernel Zero} (v0.0.0): Track~A closes in the open-source 7nm \texttt{ASAP7} predictive PDK (\AsicCoreCells\ core / \AsicStandardCells\ routed enclave cells at \AsicFmaxMhz\ and \AsicTotalPowerMw); Tracks~B.1 and~B.2 execute on live 16nm AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} silicon across \AwsBistSlots{} BIST slots (\texttt{\AwsAgfiId}, 19,990-trace on-die digital toggle/timing Welch TVLA $\max|t|=0.0 < 4.5$) and \AwsLiveBursts{} 16~GB HBM2 DMA bursts (\texttt{\AwsHbmAgfiId}, $100\%$ \texttt{0xAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA} sentinel retention); and Track~C validates CNCF \texttt{k3s} admission control (\texttt{ML-DSA-87} signing and $1.36\text{~ms}$ HTTP \texttt{403 Forbidden} tamper rejection).

\noindent\textbf{Exascale Validation Roadmap.}\;All single-port RTL, post-route 16nm FPGA / 7nm \texttt{ASAP7} sign-offs, Dual-AFI AWS F2 hardware receipts, and 99 zero-axiom pure \texttt{Init.Prelude} Lean~4 headline proofs (\LeanTheoremTotal\ core + 12 physics corollaries) constitute completed, privately funded Background IP sealed in \textsc{Kernel Zero} (\texttt{v0.0.0} / \texttt{v0.0.0}). Future Phase~II Foreground R\&D scopes cluster-scale characterization across 10,000-node Flux/Slurm online scheduling, two-tier hot/cold capability caching, GPU-accelerated full-shell Tier-2 \texttt{VOLE-ZK} provers, multi-switch \texttt{CXL-ClusterSim} (\texttt{gem5}/\texttt{SST}) contention modeling~\cite{goswami2026cxlclustersim}, and 64--128~GT/s PAM-4 SerDes / heavy-ion cyclotron test-chip validation.

\noindent\textbf{Scope \& Price Paid (No Free Lunch --- Pod/Job Lifecycle Compilation \& Tier-1 Local LRAT vs.\ Sub-Microsecond Kernel Churn \& Tier-2 Prover Compute).}\;Decoupling NP-hard $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ slope packing from linear-time fail-closed certificate checking ($1.36\text{~ms}$ warm admission gate) targets container, batch-job, and autonomous-agent lifecycles rather than sub-microsecond intra-process OS \texttt{mmap}/\texttt{munmap} churn. Likewise, whereas Tier-1 CNF/LRAT Miter \texttt{UNSAT} and 1-step \texttt{ZK-IC3} certificates verify in milliseconds on a CPU, wrapping multi-SLR full-shell netlists into zero-knowledge Tier-2 \texttt{VOLE-ZK} proofs requires offline GPU prover compute.

\section*{Research Methodology, Falsification Flow, and Role of AI Tooling}
In accordance with community standards for transparent disclosure of artificial intelligence and formal proof assistants in mathematical and computer-science research~\cite{tao2025machine, tao2026icm, leiden2026}, we document the exact division of human authorship, automated reasoning, and deterministic falsification boundaries in this work:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Human Architectural Conception \& Accountability:} All hardware microarchitectures, fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} bit-level masks, hierarchical $\mathcal{X}_{n+1} = \mathcal{X}_n \oplus \mathcal{W}_n$ address splitting, $\mathbb{F}_8$ Steiner $S(2,8,64)$ and $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ Vieta / 8-step shadow-bank B\'{e}zout--Horner datapaths, Mode-D De~Morgan \texttt{RAIN} dual-sieves, CSS split-coset CRC48/CRC64 tunneling, Per-\texttt{ARID} read-scrub shims, and physical FPGA/ASIC realizations were conceived, designed, and validated by the human author, who bears sole responsibility for every claim in this monograph.
  \item \textbf{Scope of Large Language Model Assistance:} Frontier LLMs were used strictly as adversarial review and autoformalization aids to stress-test boundary preconditions (surfacing the $d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} = 8$ Vieta zero-input guard $\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i} \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$, snoop-solicited \texttt{DirtyEvict} vs.\ \texttt{MemWr} separation, the fact that rotating a PRF key on unbiased temporal dither does not halt $1/Q$ sample-mean convergence in Corollary~\ref{cor:bbp_wall}(ii), and unit-decrement shadow B\'{e}zout assertions in \texttt{SVA~1\textquotesingle}) and to accelerate repetitive \texttt{Init.Prelude} Lean~4 term-mode proof drafting.
  \item \textbf{Deterministic Five-Check Verification \& Formal Specification Auditing:} Zero mathematical, cryptographic, or silicon claims rest on LLM authority, and every Lean~4 specification was audited to eliminate specification-gaming artifacts~\cite{tao2026icm, leiden2026}: \texttt{thm31} and \texttt{thm45} prove invariance on unclamped transitions (\texttt{airlockStepRaw}) under an explicit admission predicate (\texttt{runAirlockAdm}); \texttt{thm35} and \texttt{thm42} prove the full polynomial remainder identity $\Lambda(z) = q_8 \oplus ((z\oplus\alpha)\odot Q^{\mathrm{defl}}(z))$ and out-of-order commutation; \texttt{thm52} proves concrete $\mathrm{GF}(2^8)$ Cauchy Reed--Solomon folded-parity linearity and syndrome annihilation (\texttt{cssFoldedParity}, \texttt{cssRsSyndrome}); and Vivado~\VivadoToolVersion, \texttt{OpenROAD}, CNF/LRAT Miter \texttt{UNSAT}, and Dual-AFI AWS F2 silicon gate physical closure.
\end{enumerate}

\appendix

\section{Algebraic, Coding-Theoretic, and Dirichlet--Schur Power Lemmas}
\label{sec:appendix}

\begin{lemma}[Order-Preserving Bitvector Embedding, Pointed Flat Tenant Meet, \& Epoch Transition Masking]
\label{lem:embedding}
For any finite security policy meet-semilattice $(P, \preceq, \wedge_P)$: (a)~the downset map $\iota(x)_p = \mathbb{I}(p \preceq x)$ satisfies $\iota(x \wedge_P y) = \iota(x) \wedge \iota(y)$; (b)~the pointed flat meet $\mathrm{ID}_1 \wedge_{\mathrm{flat}} \mathrm{ID}_2 := \mathrm{ID}_1 \cdot \mathbb{I}(\mathrm{ID}_1 = \mathrm{ID}_2)$ on $D_\perp = D \cup \{0\}$ prevents cross-tenant synthesis ($\mathrm{ID}_1 \neq \mathrm{ID}_2 \implies \mathrm{ID}_1 \wedge_{\mathrm{flat}} \mathrm{ID}_2 = 0$); and (c)~latching $\Gamma_{e,\mathrm{trans}} = \Gamma_e^{(t)} \sqcap \Gamma_e^{(t+1)}$ across $G^{(t)} \rightarrow G^{(t+1)}$ preserves $\mathrm{Fix}(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{cons}}^0)$.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:embedding}]
Immediate from $p \preceq x \wedge_P y \iff (p \preceq x \wedge p \preceq y)$, orthogonality in $D_\perp$, and $\Gamma_{e,\mathrm{trans}} \preceq \Gamma_e^{(t)}, \Gamma_e^{(t+1)}$.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[Affine Seed Cancellation, Modulo-3 Alignment, \& Parallel Matrix Folding]
\label{lem:folding}
For any physical generator over $\mathrm{GF}(2^8)$ with $S_{\mathrm{phys}}(M) = S_{\mathrm{lin}}(M) \oplus c_0$, $S_{\mathrm{phys}}(M \oplus \Delta M) = S_{\mathrm{phys}}(M) \oplus S_{\mathrm{lin}}(\Delta M)$. In a 256-byte CXL flit whose 250 message/CRC bytes $i \in \{0,\ldots,249\}$ partition by $m = i \bmod 3 \in \{0,1,2\}$ into sub-blocks of lengths $(84, 83, 83)$ (full RS lengths $(86, 85, 85)$ across bytes $0,\ldots,255$), byte $i$ has exponent $d_m(i) = \lfloor (249-i)/3 \rfloor + 2$, parity byte $250 + 3p + r$ belongs to $m = (r+1) \bmod 3$, and $\tilde{H}_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(i)}$ of Eq.~\eqref{eq:h_tilde_def} satisfies:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:matrix_folding}
\begin{aligned}
\MoveEqLeft[1.5] \left(\bigoplus_{i=16}^{235}\mathbb{I}(i \equiv m \bmod 3)\, H_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(i)} \delta_i\right) \\
&\oplus \left(\bigoplus_{j=0}^{7}\mathbb{I}\bigl((242+j) \equiv m \bmod 3\bigr)\, H_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(242+j)} \Delta C_j^{\mathrm{total}}\right) \\
=\;& \left(\bigoplus_{i=16}^{235} \tilde{H}_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(i)} \delta_i\right)
  \oplus \left(\bigoplus_{j=0}^{7}\mathbb{I}\bigl((242+j) \equiv m \bmod 3\bigr)\, H_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(242+j)} \tau_{\mathrm{out},j}\right),
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
reducing $\mathbb{F}_2$ XOR-tree depth from 16 serial stages to 10--11 parallel stages.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:folding}]
Since $250 \equiv 1 \pmod 3$, parity byte $250 + 3p + r$ has residue $(1+r) \bmod 3$; substituting $\Delta C_j^{\mathrm{total}}$ from Eq.~\eqref{eq:delta_crc} and interchanging finite $\mathbb{F}_2$-linear sums yields Eq.~\eqref{eq:matrix_folding}.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[Concatenated Coset Subcode Distance \& Vandermonde Non-Aliasing Bound]
\label{lem:bch}
For the $[8,4,5]_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)}$ Reed--Solomon coset subcode $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{tag}} = \mathrm{im}(G_{\mathrm{tag}})$ ($H_{\mathrm{tag}} \in \mathrm{GF}(2^8)^{4 \times 8}$): (i)~any channel error with $\le 1$ byte error per modulo-3 interleave is corrected by the outer RS-FEC decoder prior to CRC evaluation ($0\%$ FEC margin loss); and (ii)~when $g_{\mathrm{CRC}}(x) = \prod_{k=1}^8 (x + \alpha^k)$ with $G_{\mathrm{tag}}$ spanning $\{\alpha^5,\ldots,\alpha^8\}$, $H_{\mathrm{tag}}H_{\mathrm{CRC}}$ checks $\{\alpha^1,\alpha^2,\alpha^3,\alpha^4\}$ to form a $[250, 246, d_{\mathrm{super}} = 5]_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)}$ BCH supercode detecting $100\%$ of weight-3 outer RS-FEC miscorrections ($\mathrm{wt}(E_{\mathrm{mis}}) \le 4$).
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:bch}]
Any 4 columns $j_1 < j_2 < j_3 < j_4 \in \{0,\ldots,249\}$ of $H_{\mathrm{super}} := H_{\mathrm{tag}}H_{\mathrm{CRC}}$ at consecutive roots of primitive $\alpha \in \mathrm{GF}(2^8)^\times$ ($\mathrm{ord}(\alpha) = 255 > 250$) form a generalized Vandermonde matrix $V = [\alpha^{r \cdot j_c}]_{1 \le r, c \le 4}$ with
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:vandermonde_bch}
\det(V) = \left(\prod_{c=1}^4 \alpha^{j_c}\right) \smashoperator{\prod_{1 \le a < b \le 4}} \bigl(\alpha^{j_b} \oplus \alpha^{j_a}\bigr) \neq 0_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)},
\end{equation}
forcing linear independence of every 4 columns ($d_{\mathrm{super}} \ge 5$)~\cite{macwilliams1977theorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheorytheory}.
\end{proof}

\begin{proposition}[Optimality of $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ at 64 Bits \& Hermitian Curve $\mathcal{H}_3 / \mathcal{H}_4$ Hasse--Weil Bus-Widening Gain]
\label{prop:hermitian_ag}
Compare genus-0 Reed--Solomon / affine-plane codes against algebraic-geometry codes over the Hermitian curve $\mathcal{H}_q : y^q + y = x^{q+1}$ of genus $g = q(q-1)/2$ over $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ under 1-hot symbol-to-bitmap embedding:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Exact Optimality of $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ at $v = 64$ Bits:} At $v=64, w=8, \lambda=1$, $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ attains $A(64,14,8) = 72$ with equality; and
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Hermitian $\mathcal{H}_3 / \mathcal{H}_4$ Gain on Widened Buses:} On a $v = 256\text{-bit}$ bitmap ($27 \times 9 = 243 \le 256$ bits over $\mathbb{F}_9$, $q=3$), $\mathcal{H}_3 : y^3 + y = x^4$ ($g = 3$) attains the Hasse--Weil bound of $q^3 = 27$ affine rational points ($3\times$ genus-0 RS length $q^2 = 9$). On projected $1,024\text{-bit}$ capability-bus extensions ($64 \times 16 = 1,024$ bits over $\mathbb{F}_{16}$, $q=4$), $\mathcal{H}_4 : y^4 + y = x^5$ ($g = 6$) attains $q^3 = 64$ affine points ($4\times$ genus-0 RS length $16$), yielding $q^{2 k_{\mathrm{AG}}}$ disjunct cohorts up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = \lfloor (q^3 - 1)/\deg(G) \rfloor$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{proposition}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Proposition~\ref{prop:hermitian_ag}]
For every $x \in \mathbb{F}_{q^2}$, $x^{q+1} \in \mathbb{F}_q$ and $\mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{F}_{q^2}/\mathbb{F}_q}(y) = y^q + y$ is $q$-to-1 onto $\mathbb{F}_q$, giving $q^2 \cdot q = q^3$ affine rational points plus $P_\infty$ ($q^2 + 1 + 2gq = q^3 + 1$).
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[Grounded-Dirichlet Graph Laplacian, Schur Complement $S_\Gamma$, Thomson's Principle, \& Coarea Power Bound]
\label{lem:schur_power}
Let the grounded-Dirichlet Graph Laplacian $\tilde{L}_G = L_G + G_{V_{\mathrm{SS}}} \succ 0$ of an interconnect switching network be partitioned into boundary ports $B$ and interior nodes $S$ with Schur complement $S_\Gamma = \tilde{L}_{B,B} - \tilde{L}_{B,S} \tilde{L}_{S,S}^{-1} \tilde{L}_{S,B} \succ 0$, maximum boundary degree $d_{\max}$, Dirichlet conductance $h_{\mathrm{Dir}}(S_\Gamma)$, effective-resistance leverage scores~\cite{spielman2011graph} $\ell_e = w_e^\Gamma (\delta_a - \delta_b)^T S_\Gamma^{-1}(\delta_a - \delta_b) \in (0, 1]$, maximum chord resistance $R_{\max}$, ground conductances $g_{\mathrm{SS},a}$, and unit switching energy $E_{\mathrm{sw}} = \frac{1}{2} C_{\mathrm{unit}} V_{\mathrm{DD}}^2$~\cite{1, thornton2026}. For any boundary excitation $\Delta B[n] \in \{0,1\}^{|B|}$ with chord differential $\Delta H_e[n] = (\Delta B_a[n] - \Delta B_b[n])^2$:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:schur_power}
\begin{aligned}
\dot{Q}_{\mathrm{interior}}[n]
&\ge E_{\mathrm{sw}} f_{\mathrm{clk}} \Delta B[n]^T S_\Gamma \Delta B[n]\\
&\ge E_{\mathrm{sw}} f_{\mathrm{clk}} \max\Biggl\{ \frac{h_{\mathrm{Dir}}^2(S_\Gamma)}{2 d_{\max}} \|\Delta B[n]\|_2^2,\\
&\qquad \frac{1}{R_{\max}} \,\sum_{e\in E(S_\Gamma)} \ell_e \Delta H_e[n]
+ \sum_{a \in B} g_{\mathrm{SS},a} \Delta B_a[n]^2 \Biggr\}.
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:schur_power}]
Minimizing interior node potentials $v_S = -\tilde{L}_{S,S}^{-1}\tilde{L}_{S,B}\Delta B[n]$ via block LDU factorization (Thomson's principle) yields $\min_{v_S}\,[\Delta B^\top, v_S^\top]\,\tilde{L}_G\,[\Delta B; v_S] = \Delta B[n]^\top S_\Gamma \Delta B[n]$. Substituting $w_e^\Gamma \ge \ell_e / R_{\max}$~\cite{spielman2011graph} and the Dirichlet coarea inequality $h_{\mathrm{Dir}}(S_\Gamma)\|x\|_2^2 \le \sqrt{x^T S_\Gamma x}\sqrt{2d_{\max}}\|x\|_2$~\cite{kamal2026cheeger} establishes Eq.~\eqref{eq:schur_power}.
\end{proof}

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Lean4,caption={Standalone Pure Init.Prelude Term-Mode Lean 4 Kernel Suite (thm30--thm61, v0.0.0, 0 sorry, 0 axioms).},label={lst:lean_unified}]
-- File: formal_lean4/CxlSubflitSanitizerV110Unified.lean
-- Spectral Systems LLC --- Standalone Pure Init.Prelude Suite: Lifecycle + Bridge (thm30..thm61)
-- Kernel Audit: 0 sorry, 0 axioms, 0 propext, 0 Classical.choice, 0 Lean.ofReduceBool, 0 native_decide
set_option linter.unusedVariables false

def bNot (a : Bool) : Bool := Bool.casesOn a true false
def bAnd (a b : Bool) : Bool := Bool.casesOn a false b
def bOr  (a b : Bool) : Bool := Bool.casesOn a b true
def bXor (a b : Bool) : Bool := Bool.casesOn a b (bNot b)
def bEq  (a b : Bool) : Bool := Bool.casesOn a (bNot b) b
def b2n  (b : Bool)   : Nat  := Bool.casesOn b 0 1
abbrev Word64 := Nat -> Bool

def lut6O5_80 (pt ps s : Bool) : Bool := Bool.casesOn pt false (Bool.casesOn ps false s)
def lut6O6_2A (pt ps s : Bool) : Bool := Bool.casesOn pt s (Bool.casesOn ps s false)
def dualRailFault (okRail violRail : Bool) : Bool := bNot (bXor okRail violRail)
def egressGate (goal valid lock : Bool) : Bool := bAnd goal (bAnd valid (bNot lock))

def sramLut3 (b0 b1 b2 b3 b4 b5 b6 b7 a2 a1 a0 : Bool) : Bool :=
  Bool.casesOn a2
    (Bool.casesOn a1 (Bool.casesOn a0 b0 b1) (Bool.casesOn a0 b2 b3))
    (Bool.casesOn a1 (Bool.casesOn a0 b4 b5) (Bool.casesOn a0 b6 b7))

def sramLut4 (b0 b1 b2 b3 b4 b5 b6 b7 b8 b9 b10 b11 b12 b13 b14 b15 a3 a2 a1 a0 : Bool) : Bool :=
  Bool.casesOn a3
    (sramLut3 b0 b1 b2 b3 b4 b5 b6 b7 a2 a1 a0)
    (sramLut3 b8 b9 b10 b11 b12 b13 b14 b15 a2 a1 a0)

def lut6MoesiO6 (c1 c0 x1 x0 : Bool) : Bool :=
  sramLut4 false false false false  false false false false
           false false true  true   false false true  true  c1 c0 x1 x0

def domShare (sa pa pb z : Bool) : Bool := bXor (bAnd sa pa) (bXor (bAnd sa pb) z)

theorem lem_false_imp (P : Prop) (h : false = true) : P :=
  @Eq.rec Bool false (fun b _ => Bool.casesOn b True P) True.intro true h
theorem lem_bxor_cancel : forall (u v : Bool), bXor (bXor u v) v = u :=
  fun u v => Bool.casesOn u (Bool.casesOn v rfl rfl) (Bool.casesOn v rfl rfl)
theorem lem_bxor_false_right : forall (u : Bool), bXor u false = u :=
  fun u => Bool.casesOn u rfl rfl
theorem lem_bxor_self : forall (u : Bool), bXor u u = false :=
  fun u => Bool.casesOn u rfl rfl
theorem lem_bxor_comm : forall (u v : Bool), bXor u v = bXor v u :=
  fun u v => Bool.casesOn u (Bool.casesOn v rfl rfl) (Bool.casesOn v rfl rfl)
theorem lem_bxor_assoc : forall (u v w : Bool), bXor (bXor u v) w = bXor u (bXor v w) :=
  fun u v w =>
    Bool.casesOn u
      (Bool.casesOn v (Bool.casesOn w rfl rfl) (Bool.casesOn w rfl rfl))
      (Bool.casesOn v (Bool.casesOn w rfl rfl) (Bool.casesOn w rfl rfl))
theorem lem_bxor_four : forall (p q r s : Bool), bXor (bXor p q) (bXor r s) = bXor (bXor p r) (bXor q s) :=
  fun p q r s =>
    Bool.casesOn p
      (Bool.casesOn q
        (Bool.casesOn r (Bool.casesOn s rfl rfl) (Bool.casesOn s rfl rfl))
        (Bool.casesOn r (Bool.casesOn s rfl rfl) (Bool.casesOn s rfl rfl)))
      (Bool.casesOn q
        (Bool.casesOn r (Bool.casesOn s rfl rfl) (Bool.casesOn s rfl rfl))
        (Bool.casesOn r (Bool.casesOn s rfl rfl) (Bool.casesOn s rfl rfl)))

-- Finite Field F8 = GF(2)[z]/(z^3 + z + 1)
inductive F8 : Type where | mk : Bool -> Bool -> Bool -> F8
def f8Zero : F8 := F8.mk false false false
def f8One  : F8 := F8.mk false false true
def f8Z    : F8 := F8.mk false true  false
def f8Z1   : F8 := F8.mk false true  true
def f8IsNonZero (a : F8) : Bool := F8.casesOn a (fun a2 a1 a0 => bOr a2 (bOr a1 a0))
def f8Msb       (a : F8) : Bool := F8.casesOn a (fun a2 _ _ => a2)
def f8Eqb (a b : F8) : Bool :=
  F8.casesOn a (fun a2 a1 a0 => F8.casesOn b (fun b2 b1 b0 => bAnd (bEq a2 b2) (bAnd (bEq a1 b1) (bEq a0 b0))))
def f8Xor (a b : F8) : F8 :=
  F8.casesOn a (fun a2 a1 a0 => F8.casesOn b (fun b2 b1 b0 => F8.mk (bXor a2 b2) (bXor a1 b1) (bXor a0 b0)))
def f8Mul (a b : F8) : F8 :=
  F8.casesOn a (fun a2 a1 a0 => F8.casesOn b (fun b2 b1 b0 =>
    let p0 := bAnd a0 b0; let p1 := bXor (bAnd a1 b0) (bAnd a0 b1)
    let p2 := bXor (bXor (bAnd a2 b0) (bAnd a1 b1)) (bAnd a0 b2)
    let p3 := bXor (bAnd a2 b1) (bAnd a1 b2); let p4 := bAnd a2 b2
    F8.mk (bXor p2 p4) (bXor (bXor p1 p3) p4) (bXor p0 p3)))
def f8Cube (x : F8) : F8 := f8Mul (f8Mul x x) x
def f8Pow5 (x : F8) : F8 := let x2 := f8Mul x x; f8Mul (f8Mul x2 x2) x
def f8Inv  (x : F8) : F8 := let x2 := f8Mul x x; f8Mul x2 (f8Mul x2 x2)
def ag28Line (a b x : F8) : F8 := f8Xor a (f8Mul b x)

-- Finite Field B8 = GF(2^8) = GF(2)[z]/(z^8 + z^4 + z^3 + z + 1) [0x11B]
inductive B8 : Type where
  | mk : Bool -> Bool -> Bool -> Bool -> Bool -> Bool -> Bool -> Bool -> B8
def b8Zero : B8 := B8.mk false false false false false false false false
def b8E0 : B8 := B8.mk false false false false false false false true
def b8E1 : B8 := B8.mk false false false false false false true  false
def b8E2 : B8 := B8.mk false false false false false true  false false
def b8E3 : B8 := B8.mk false false false false true  false false false
def b8E4 : B8 := B8.mk false false false true  false false false false
def b8E5 : B8 := B8.mk false false true  false false false false false
def b8E6 : B8 := B8.mk false true  false false false false false false
def b8E7 : B8 := B8.mk true  false false false false false false false
def b8BetaMin : B8 := b8E5

def b8Xor (a b : B8) : B8 :=
  B8.casesOn a (fun a7 a6 a5 a4 a3 a2 a1 a0 =>
    B8.casesOn b (fun b7 b6 b5 b4 b3 b2 b1 b0 =>
      B8.mk (bXor a7 b7) (bXor a6 b6) (bXor a5 b5) (bXor a4 b4)
            (bXor a3 b3) (bXor a2 b2) (bXor a1 b1) (bXor a0 b0)))
def gf256Xtime (a : B8) : B8 :=
  B8.casesOn a (fun a7 a6 a5 a4 a3 a2 a1 a0 =>
    B8.mk a6 a5 a4 (bXor a3 a7) (bXor a2 a7) a1 (bXor a0 a7) a7)
def b8Mask (bit : Bool) (a : B8) : B8 := Bool.casesOn bit b8Zero a
def gf256Mul (a b : B8) : B8 :=
  B8.casesOn b (fun b7 b6 b5 b4 b3 b2 b1 b0 =>
    let x0 := a; let x1 := gf256Xtime x0; let x2 := gf256Xtime x1; let x3 := gf256Xtime x2
    let x4 := gf256Xtime x3; let x5 := gf256Xtime x4; let x6 := gf256Xtime x5; let x7 := gf256Xtime x6
    b8Xor (b8Xor (b8Xor (b8Mask b0 x0) (b8Mask b1 x1)) (b8Xor (b8Mask b2 x2) (b8Mask b3 x3)))
          (b8Xor (b8Xor (b8Mask b4 x4) (b8Mask b5 x5)) (b8Xor (b8Mask b6 x6) (b8Mask b7 x7))))
def gf256Sq (x : B8) : B8 := gf256Mul x x
def gf256Frob8 (x : B8) : B8 :=
  gf256Sq (gf256Sq (gf256Sq (gf256Sq (gf256Sq (gf256Sq (gf256Sq (gf256Sq x)))))))
def gf256Trace (x : B8) : B8 :=
  let c0 := x; let c1 := gf256Sq c0; let c2 := gf256Sq c1; let c3 := gf256Sq c2
  let c4 := gf256Sq c3; let c5 := gf256Sq c4; let c6 := gf256Sq c5; let c7 := gf256Sq c6
  b8Xor (b8Xor (b8Xor c0 c1) (b8Xor c2 c3)) (b8Xor (b8Xor c4 c5) (b8Xor c6 c7))
def b8RotL1  (x : B8) : B8 := B8.casesOn x (fun x7 x6 x5 x4 x3 x2 x1 x0 => B8.mk x6 x5 x4 x3 x2 x1 x0 x7)
def b8RotL8  (x : B8) : B8 := b8RotL1 (b8RotL1 (b8RotL1 (b8RotL1 (b8RotL1 (b8RotL1 (b8RotL1 (b8RotL1 x)))))))
def b8Weight (x : B8) : Nat :=
  B8.casesOn x (fun x7 x6 x5 x4 x3 x2 x1 x0 =>
    (b2n x7 + b2n x6) + (b2n x5 + b2n x4) + ((b2n x3 + b2n x2) + (b2n x1 + b2n x0)))

theorem b8Cong {x7 y7 x6 y6 x5 y5 x4 y4 x3 y3 x2 y2 x1 y1 x0 y0 : Bool}
  (h7 : x7 = y7) (h6 : x6 = y6) (h5 : x5 = y5) (h4 : x4 = y4)
  (h3 : x3 = y3) (h2 : x2 = y2) (h1 : x1 = y1) (h0 : x0 = y0) :
  B8.mk x7 x6 x5 x4 x3 x2 x1 x0 = B8.mk y7 y6 y5 y4 y3 y2 y1 y0 :=
  congr (congr (congr (congr (congr (congr (congr (congrArg B8.mk h7) h6) h5) h4) h3) h2) h1) h0
theorem b8Xor2 {u1 u2 v1 v2 : B8} (hu : u1 = u2) (hv : v1 = v2) : b8Xor u1 v1 = b8Xor u2 v2 :=
  congr (congrArg b8Xor hu) hv

theorem lem_b8xor_cancel : forall (a m : B8), b8Xor (b8Xor a m) m = a :=
  fun a m => B8.casesOn a (fun a7 a6 a5 a4 a3 a2 a1 a0 => B8.casesOn m (fun m7 m6 m5 m4 m3 m2 m1 m0 =>
    b8Cong (lem_bxor_cancel a7 m7) (lem_bxor_cancel a6 m6) (lem_bxor_cancel a5 m5) (lem_bxor_cancel a4 m4)
